

    """
    =============================================================================
     BEARING FAULT DIAGNOSIS — ORDER-DOMAIN PHYSICS DESCRIPTORS
     Cell 1/5 : configuration, bearing geometry, order mathematics, utilities
    =============================================================================
    """
    import os, re, glob, json, time, warnings, hashlib
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft, rfft, irfft, rfftfreq, fftfreq
    from scipy.signal import get_window
    from scipy.stats import kurtosis, skew
    from scipy.io import loadmat
    from sklearn.preprocessing import QuantileTransformer
    from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
    from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingClassifier
    from sklearn.metrics import (accuracy_score, f1_score, roc_auc_score,
                                 confusion_matrix, classification_report)
    warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")

    SEED = 0
    np.random.seed(SEED)

    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- paths
    PATHS = dict(
        CWRU  = r"F:\Umar-Wisal-Work\Datasets\CWRU_original",
        PB    = r"F:\Umar-Wisal-Work\Datasets\Paderborn",
        OUT   = "results_v2",
        CACHE = "cache_v2",
        FIGS  = "figs_v2",
    )
    for k in ("OUT", "CACHE", "FIGS"):
        os.makedirs(PATHS[k], exist_ok=True)

    for k in ("CWRU", "PB"):
        print(f"{k:<5} {'OK ' if os.path.isdir(PATHS[k]) else 'MISSING'} {PATHS[k]}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ geometry
    #   key: (n_balls, ball_dia_mm, pitch_dia_mm, contact_angle_deg, description)
    BEARINGS = {
        "CWRU_DE_6205":   (9, 7.94, 39.04, 0.0, "SKF 6205-2RS JEM — CWRU drive end"),
        "CWRU_FE_6203":   (8, 6.75, 28.50, 0.0, "SKF 6203-2RS JEM — CWRU fan end"),
        "PADERBORN_6203": (8, 6.75, 28.50, 0.0, "6203 — Paderborn KAt test rig"),
    }

    def char_orders(key):
        """Characteristic fault frequencies expressed in shaft orders (× f_r)."""
        Nb, d, D, phi, _ = BEARINGS[key]
        n = (d / D) * np.cos(np.deg2rad(phi))
        return {"FTF":  0.5 * (1 - n),
                "BPFO": 0.5 * Nb * (1 - n),
                "BPFI": 0.5 * Nb * (1 + n),
                "BSF":  (D / (2 * d)) * (1 - n**2)}

    def zeta(order, max_int=10):
        """Collision margin: (nearest integer shaft order, distance to it)."""
        m = min(range(1, max_int + 1), key=lambda k: abs(order - k))
        return m, abs(order - m)

    def separation_bins(order, fr, fs, seg_len):
        """FFT bins separating this order from its nearest shaft harmonic."""
        _, z = zeta(order)
        return z * fr / (fs / seg_len)

    def geometry_table():
        ref = (("CWRU_50rev", 29.2, 12000.0, 20500),
               ("PB_100rev",  25.0, 64000.0, 256000),
               ("PB_900rpm",  15.0, 64000.0, 256000))
        rows = []
        for key in BEARINGS:
            for name, o in char_orders(key).items():
                m, z = zeta(o)
                r = dict(bearing=key, order_name=name, order=round(o, 4),
                         nearest_int=m, zeta=round(z, 4))
                for tag, fr, fs, L in ref:
                    r[f"bins_{tag}"] = round(separation_bins(o, fr, fs, L), 2)
                rows.append(r)
        return pd.DataFrame(rows)

    def usable_orders(key, fr, df_res, min_bins=1.0):
        """Split characteristic orders into resolvable / unresolvable at this
           shaft speed and spectral resolution. FTF is always kept."""
        keep, drop = {}, {}
        for name, o in char_orders(key).items():
            if name == "FTF":
                keep[name] = o; continue
            _, z = zeta(o)
            (keep if z * fr / df_res >= min_bins else drop)[name] = o
        return keep, drop

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------- utilities
    def fast_len_leq(n, primes=(2, 3, 5, 7)):
        """Largest FFT-friendly length <= n (avoids slow large prime factors)."""
        while n > 1:
            m = n
            for p in primes:
                while m % p == 0:
                    m //= p
            if m == 1:
                return n
            n -= 1
        return 1

    def save_table(df, name, out=None):
        p = os.path.join(out or PATHS["OUT"], f"{name}.csv")
        df.to_csv(p, index=False)
        print(f"  -> {p}")
        return df

    def make_clf(kind):
        if kind == "lr":
            return LogisticRegression(max_iter=4000, C=0.5,
                                      class_weight="balanced", random_state=SEED)
        return HistGradientBoostingClassifier(max_depth=4, learning_rate=0.06,
                                              max_iter=350, l2_regularization=1.0,
                                              random_state=SEED)

    def report(y, p, classes, tag):
        cm = confusion_matrix(y, p, labels=classes)
        out = {}
        for i, c in enumerate(classes):
            FP = cm[:, i].sum() - cm[i, i]
            TN = cm.sum() - cm[i, :].sum() - cm[:, i].sum() + cm[i, i]
            out[f"FPR_{c}"] = round(FP / (FP + TN + 1e-9), 4)
        if "N" in classes:
            i = classes.index("N")
            out["FALSE_ALARM"]  = round(1 - cm[i, i] / (cm[i, :].sum() + 1e-9), 4)
            out["MISSED_FAULT"] = round((cm[:, i].sum() - cm[i, i]) /
                                        (cm.sum() - cm[i, :].sum() + 1e-9), 4)
        print(f"\n=== {tag}  acc={accuracy_score(y,p):.4f}  "
              f"macroF1={f1_score(y,p,average='macro'):.4f}")
        print(out)
        print(pd.DataFrame(cm, index=classes, columns=classes))
        return out, cm

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------- verify
    T1 = geometry_table()
    save_table(T1, "T1_geometry")
    print()
    print(T1.to_string(index=False))

    print("\nEXPECTED (must match exactly):")

    print("  6203  FTF 0.3816  BPFO 3.0526  BPFI 4.9474  BSF 1.9927")

    for key, fr, fs, L in (("CWRU_DE_6205", 29.2, 12000.0, 20500),
                           ("PADERBORN_6203", 25.0, 64000.0, 256000),
                           ("PADERBORN_6203", 15.0, 64000.0, 256000)):
        keep, drop = usable_orders(key, fr, fs / L)
        print(f"\n{key} @ {fr*60:.0f} rpm, {L} samples @ {fs/1000:.0f} kHz")
        print(f"   resolvable : {sorted(keep)}")
        print(f"   unresolvable: {sorted(drop) if drop else '(none)'}")

    """
    =============================================================================
     Cell 2/5 : dataset loaders
     Both datasets return records with an identical schema so everything
     downstream is dataset-agnostic.
    =============================================================================
    """
    from collections import namedtuple

    Record = namedtuple("Record", "sig fs fr meta")

    # ============================================================== CWRU =========
    _cwru_lab_path = os.path.join(PATHS["CWRU"], "labels.csv")
    if not os.path.exists(_cwru_lab_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"missing {_cwru_lab_path} — rerun the download script")

    CWRU_LABELS = pd.read_csv(_cwru_lab_path)
    CWRU_LUT = {int(r.num): dict(label=str(r.label), dia=float(r.fault_dia_in),
                                 load=int(r.load_hp), rpm_nom=float(r.rpm_nominal),
                                 or_pos=("" if pd.isna(r.or_pos) else str(r.or_pos)))
                for r in CWRU_LABELS.itertuples()}

    def cwru_records(channel="DE", keep_dia=(0.007, 0.014, 0.021),
                     or_pos_keep=("", "@6"), verbose=True):
        """CWRU drive-end records. Handles three known packaging quirks:
           99.mat also contains X098_* variables; 98/99 have no RPM variable;
           3001-3008 carry internal names (X048-X059) unrelated to the filename."""
        out, skipped = [], []
        for f in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(PATHS["CWRU"], "*.mat"))):
            stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(f))[0]
            if not stem.isdigit():
                continue
            num = int(stem)
            if num not in CWRU_LUT:
                skipped.append((num, "not in labels.csv")); continue
            md = CWRU_LUT[num]
            if md["label"] != "N" and md["dia"] not in keep_dia:
                continue
            if md["label"] == "OR" and md["or_pos"] not in or_pos_keep:
                continue
            m = loadmat(f, squeeze_me=True, struct_as_record=False)
            keys = [k for k in m if not k.startswith("__")]

            exact = f"X{num:03d}_{channel}_time"
            if exact in keys:
                var = exact
            else:
                cands = [k for k in keys if k.endswith(f"_{channel}_time")] or \
                        [k for k in keys if k.endswith("_time")]
                if not cands:
                    skipped.append((num, f"no time channel in {keys}")); continue
                var = sorted(cands)[-1]

            rpm_keys = [k for k in keys if k.endswith("RPM")]
            rpm = float(np.ravel(m[rpm_keys[0]])[0]) if rpm_keys else md["rpm_nom"]
            sig = np.asarray(m[var], float).ravel()
            if sig.size < 20000 or not np.isfinite(sig).all():
                skipped.append((num, f"bad signal len={sig.size}")); continue

            out.append(Record(sig, 12000.0, rpm / 60.0, dict(
                dataset="CWRU", bearing_key="CWRU_DE_6205",
                label=md["label"], kind="artificial",
                severity=md["dia"], condition=f"load{md['load']}",
                unit=f"CWRU_{md['label']}_{md['dia']:.3f}{md['or_pos']}",
                file=f"{num}.mat", var=var,
                rpm_source="file" if rpm_keys else "nominal")))
        if verbose:
            print(f"CWRU: {len(out)} records, {len(skipped)} skipped")
            if skipped: print("  skipped:", skipped[:8])
        return out

    # ========================================================= PADERBORN =========
    PB_CODE2LAB = {}
    for codes, lab, kind in [
        ({"K001","K002","K003","K004","K005","K006"},              "N",  "healthy"),
        ({"KI01","KI03","KI05","KI07","KI08"},                     "IR", "artificial"),
        ({"KA01","KA03","KA05","KA06","KA07","KA08","KA09"},       "OR", "artificial"),
        ({"KI04","KI14","KI16","KI17","KI18","KI21"},              "IR", "real"),
        ({"KA04","KA15","KA16","KA22","KA30"},                     "OR", "real"),
    ]:
        for c in codes:
            PB_CODE2LAB[c] = (lab, kind)
    PB_COMBINED = {"KB23", "KB24", "KB27"}      # inner+outer together — excluded

    PB_CONDITIONS = {
        "N15_M07_F10": dict(rpm=1500, torque=0.7, force=1000),
        "N09_M07_F10": dict(rpm= 900, torque=0.7, force=1000),
        "N15_M01_F10": dict(rpm=1500, torque=0.1, force=1000),
        "N15_M07_F04": dict(rpm=1500, torque=0.7, force= 400),
    }

    def pb_scan(conditions=None, drop_combined=True):
        """Build a manifest without loading any signal data. Fast."""
        rows = []
        for f in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(PATHS["PB"], "**", "*.mat"),
                                  recursive=True)):
            b = os.path.basename(f).upper()
            codes = re.findall(r"K[AIB]\d{2}|K0\d{2}", b)
            cond = re.search(r"N\d{2}_M\d{2}_F\d{2}", b)
            if not codes:
                continue
            code = codes[-1]
            if drop_combined and code in PB_COMBINED:
                continue
            if code not in PB_CODE2LAB:
                continue
            c = cond.group(0) if cond else "NA"
            if conditions and c not in conditions:
                continue
            lab, kind = PB_CODE2LAB[code]
            rows.append(dict(path=f, unit=code, label=lab, kind=kind, condition=c))
        return pd.DataFrame(rows)

    def pb_load_one(path):
        m = loadmat(path, squeeze_me=True, struct_as_record=False)
        key = [k for k in m if not k.startswith("__")][0]
        Y = m[key].Y
        ch = {str(y.Name): np.asarray(y.Data, float).ravel()
              for y in np.atleast_1d(Y)}
        if "vibration_1" not in ch:
            raise KeyError(f"no vibration_1; have {sorted(ch)}")
        rpm = float(np.mean(ch["speed"])) if "speed" in ch else np.nan
        return ch["vibration_1"], 64000.0, rpm / 60.0

    def pb_records(manifest, n_per_unit_condition=None, verbose=True):
        """Sample evenly within each (unit, condition) cell so class balance and
           speed coverage stay controlled."""
        out, skipped = [], []
        for (unit, cond), g in manifest.groupby(["unit", "condition"]):
            g = g.reset_index(drop=True)
            idx = (np.linspace(0, len(g)-1,
                               min(n_per_unit_condition, len(g))).astype(int)
                   if n_per_unit_condition else np.arange(len(g)))
            for i in np.unique(idx):
                r = g.loc[i]
                try:
                    sig, fs, fr = pb_load_one(r.path)
                except Exception as e:
                    skipped.append((os.path.basename(r.path), str(e))); continue
                if not np.isfinite(fr) or fr < 5 or sig.size < 50000:
                    skipped.append((os.path.basename(r.path), "bad fr/len")); continue
                out.append(Record(sig, fs, fr, dict(
                    dataset="PB", bearing_key="PADERBORN_6203",
                    label=r.label, kind=r.kind, severity=np.nan,
                    condition=cond, unit=unit,
                    file=os.path.basename(r.path), var="vibration_1",
                    rpm_source="channel")))
        if verbose:
            print(f"Paderborn: {len(out)} records, {len(skipped)} skipped")
            if skipped: print("  skipped:", skipped[:5])
        return out

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------- verify
    MAN = pb_scan()
    print("Paderborn manifest — files per label × condition:")
    print(pd.crosstab(MAN.label, MAN.condition))
    print("\nunits per label:", MAN.groupby("label").unit.nunique().to_dict())
    print("label × kind:", MAN.groupby(["label","kind"]).size().to_dict())
    print("total files:", len(MAN))

    CW_RECS = cwru_records()
    cw_meta = pd.DataFrame([r.meta for r in CW_RECS])
    print("\nCWRU — label × severity:")
    print(cw_meta.groupby(["label","severity"]).size().unstack(fill_value=0))
    print("conditions:", sorted(cw_meta.condition.unique()))
    print("rpm_source:", cw_meta.rpm_source.value_counts().to_dict())
    print("fr range: %.2f – %.2f Hz (%.0f – %.0f rpm)" % (
        min(r.fr for r in CW_RECS), max(r.fr for r in CW_RECS),
        60*min(r.fr for r in CW_RECS), 60*max(r.fr for r in CW_RECS)))

    print("\nPaderborn speed check — 3 files per condition:")
    for c in PB_CONDITIONS:
        sub = MAN[MAN.condition == c].head(3)
        frs = []
        for p in sub.path:
            try: frs.append(pb_load_one(p)[2])
            except Exception: pass
        print(f"  {c}: fr = {[round(x,2) for x in frs]} Hz  "
              f"(nominal {PB_CONDITIONS[c]['rpm']/60:.1f} Hz)")

    """
    =============================================================================
     Cell 3/5 : feature extraction — order-targeted band selection with a
                search-matched null.

     A blind kurtogram maximises impulsiveness, which is NOT the same as
     fault-frequency visibility; empirically it selects the same high band for
     every class. Instead each candidate order gets its own band search. That
     search inflates peak-to-floor through multiple comparisons, so the SAME
     search is run at randomly chosen non-characteristic orders and every score
     is normalised by that null. Nothing here uses the class label.
    =============================================================================
    """

    def _band_analytic(X, f, f1, f2):
        """Band-limited analytic signal from a precomputed full FFT."""
        H = np.zeros(len(X))
        H[(f >= f1) & (f <= f2)] = 2.0
        return ifft(X * H)


    def band_list(fs, levels=(1, 1.6, 2, 2.6, 3, 3.6, 4), fmin_frac=0.02,
                  min_bw=300.0):
        """1/3-binary tree bands (Antoni). Sorted and de-duplicated."""
        nyq = fs / 2.0
        out = set()
        for L in levels:
            nb = int(round(2 ** L))
            e = np.linspace(fmin_frac * nyq, nyq, nb + 1)
            for i in range(nb):
                if e[i + 1] - e[i] >= min_bw:
                    out.add((round(float(e[i]), 1), round(float(e[i + 1]), 1)))
        return sorted(out)


    def null_orders(seed_str, forbidden_orders, n=12, lo=1.5, hi=7.5, guard=0.22):
        """Random non-characteristic orders, clear of fault orders and integer
           shaft harmonics. Guaranteed to return exactly n: falls back to the
           widest available gaps if rejection sampling runs short."""
        h = int(hashlib.md5(str(seed_str).encode()).hexdigest()[:8], 16)
        rng = np.random.RandomState(h % (2 ** 31))
        banned = [q for q in list(forbidden_orders) + list(range(1, 10))
                  if lo - guard <= q <= hi + guard]

        out, tries = [], 0
        while len(out) < n and tries < 20000:
            tries += 1
            o = float(rng.uniform(lo, hi))
            if all(abs(o - q) > guard for q in banned + out):
                out.append(o)

        if len(out) < n:                        # deterministic top-up
            cand = np.linspace(lo, hi, 2000)
            pool = [float(c) for c in cand
                    if all(abs(c - q) > guard for q in banned)]
            rng.shuffle(pool)
            for c in pool:
                if len(out) >= n:
                    break
                if all(abs(c - q) > guard * 0.6 for q in out):
                    out.append(c)

        if len(out) < n:                        # last resort: relax spacing
            cand = np.linspace(lo, hi, 5000)
            pool = [float(c) for c in cand
                    if all(abs(c - q) > guard for q in banned)]
            rng.shuffle(pool)
            out.extend(pool[:n - len(out)])

        return sorted(out[:n])


    def extract_features(sig, fs, fr, bearing_key, seed_str="x", f_max=200.0,
                         n_harm=3, n_null=12, min_bins=1.0, return_detail=False):
        """Feature dict for one record. Label-free."""
        co_all = char_orders(bearing_key)

        n = fast_len_leq(len(sig))
        x = np.asarray(sig[:n], float)
        xc = x - x.mean()
        X = fft(xc)
        ffull = fftfreq(n, 1.0 / fs)
        w = get_window("hann", n)
        wsum = 0.5 * w.sum()
        faf = rfftfreq(n, 1.0 / fs)
        keep = faf <= f_max
        fa = faf[keep]
        d_fa = fa[1] - fa[0]
        tol = max(1.5 * d_fa, 0.010 * fr)

        # keep only orders resolvable from their nearest shaft harmonic
        orders, dropped = usable_orders(bearing_key, fr, d_fa, min_bins=min_bins)
        fault_orders = [o for o in ("BPFO", "BPFI", "BSF") if o in orders]

        probe = dict(orders)
        nulls = null_orders(seed_str, list(co_all.values()), n=n_null)
        for i, v in enumerate(nulls):
            probe[f"NULL{i}"] = v

        best = {k: dict(p2f=0.0, lo=np.nan, hi=np.nan) for k in probe}
        harm = {k: 0.0 for k in probe}
        side = {k: 0.0 for k in probe}

        for f1, f2 in band_list(fs):
            env2 = np.abs(_band_analytic(X, ffull, f1, f2)) ** 2
            env2 -= env2.mean()
            S = np.abs(rfft(env2 * w))[keep] / wsum
            Sn = S / (np.median(S[fa > 5.0]) + 1e-20)
            for k, o in probe.items():
                ft = o * fr
                if ft >= fa[-1]:
                    continue
                m = (fa >= ft - tol) & (fa <= ft + tol)
                sd = ((fa >= ft - 10 * tol) & (fa < ft - 3 * tol)) | \
                     ((fa >  ft + 3 * tol)  & (fa <= ft + 10 * tol))
                if not m.any() or not sd.any():
                    continue
                v = float(Sn[m].max() / (np.median(Sn[sd]) + 1e-20))
                if v > best[k]["p2f"]:
                    best[k] = dict(p2f=v, lo=f1, hi=f2)
                    h = 0.0
                    for hh in range(2, n_harm + 1):
                        fh = o * hh * fr
                        if fh < fa[-1]:
                            mh = (fa >= fh - tol) & (fa <= fh + tol)
                            if mh.any():
                                h += float(Sn[mh].max())
                    harm[k] = h
                    s = 0.0
                    for sgn in (-1, 1):
                        fsb = ft + sgn * fr
                        if 0 < fsb < fa[-1]:
                            ms = (fa >= fsb - tol) & (fa <= fsb + tol)
                            if ms.any():
                                s += float(Sn[ms].max())
                    side[k] = s

        # --- search-matched null (use however many actually resolved) ---
        null_keys = [k for k in best if k.startswith("NULL")]
        nv = np.array([best[k]["p2f"] for k in null_keys], dtype=float)
        if nv.size < 4:
            raise RuntimeError(f"only {nv.size} null orders resolved — "
                               f"widen [lo,hi] or lower n_null")
        nmed = float(np.median(nv))
        n95  = float(np.percentile(nv, 95))
        nstd = float(np.std(nv) + 1e-9)

        F = dict(fr=fr, null_med=nmed, null_95=n95, null_std=nstd,
                 n_null_used=int(nv.size), n_orders=len(fault_orders))

        for k in orders:
            p = best[k]["p2f"]
            F[f"{k}_p2f"]  = p
            F[f"{k}_z"]    = p / (nmed + 1e-9)              # null-normalised
            F[f"{k}_zs"]   = (p - nmed) / nstd              # standardised
            F[f"{k}_sig"]  = float(p > n95)                 # beats its own null
            F[f"{k}_band"] = 0.5 * (best[k]["lo"] + best[k]["hi"]) / 1000.0
            F[f"{k}_harm"] = float(np.log1p(harm[k]))
            F[f"{k}_sb"]   = float(side[k] / (side[k] + p + 1.0))

        for i, a in enumerate(fault_orders):
            for b in fault_orders[i + 1:]:
                F[f"LR_{a}_{b}"] = float(np.log((best[a]["p2f"] + 1e-9) /
                                                (best[b]["p2f"] + 1e-9)))
        if "FTF" in orders:
            for a in fault_orders:
                F[f"LR_{a}_FTF"] = float(np.log((best[a]["p2f"] + 1e-9) /
                                                (best["FTF"]["p2f"] + 1e-9)))

        if fault_orders:
            tot = sum(best[o]["p2f"] for o in fault_orders) + 1e-9
            for o in fault_orders:
                F[f"R_{o}"] = best[o]["p2f"] / tot
            ranked = sorted(fault_orders, key=lambda o: best[o]["p2f"], reverse=True)
            F["argmax_order"] = ranked[0]
            F["max_z"] = best[ranked[0]]["p2f"] / (nmed + 1e-9)
            F["margin"] = (float(np.log((best[ranked[0]]["p2f"] + 1e-9) /
                                        (best[ranked[1]]["p2f"] + 1e-9)))
                           if len(ranked) > 1 else 0.0)
            F["n_sig"] = float(sum(F[f"{o}_sig"] for o in fault_orders))

        F["sig_kurt"] = float(kurtosis(x, fisher=True))
        F["sig_skew"] = float(skew(x))
        F["crest"]    = float(np.max(np.abs(x)) / (np.sqrt(np.mean(x ** 2)) + 1e-12))
        F["rms_log"]  = float(np.log(np.sqrt(np.mean(x ** 2)) + 1e-12))

        if return_detail:
            return F, best, (fa, orders, dropped)
        return F


    # ------------------------------------------------------------------- verify
    print("smoke test — one record per Paderborn class, N15_M07_F10\n")
    man1 = pb_scan(conditions=("N15_M07_F10",))
    for lab in ("N", "IR", "OR"):
        recs = pb_records(man1[man1.label == lab].head(1), verbose=False)
        r = recs[0]
        L = fast_len_leq(min(int(100 * r.fs / r.fr), len(r.sig)))
        t0 = time.time()
        F, best, (fa, keep_o, drop_o) = extract_features(
            r.sig[:L], r.fs, r.fr, r.meta["bearing_key"],
            seed_str=r.meta["file"], return_detail=True)
        print(f"{lab} ({r.meta['unit']}, {r.meta['kind']}, {time.time()-t0:.1f}s)")
        print(f"   orders kept={sorted(keep_o)} dropped={sorted(drop_o)}")
        print(f"   null: med={F['null_med']:.2f} p95={F['null_95']:.2f} "
              f"n_used={F['n_null_used']}")
        for o in ("BPFO", "BPFI"):
            print(f"   {o}: p2f={F[o+'_p2f']:7.2f}  z={F[o+'_z']:6.2f}  "
                  f"sig={int(F[o+'_sig'])}  band={F[o+'_band']:.1f} kHz")
        print(f"   argmax={F['argmax_order']}  margin={F['margin']:+.2f}  "
              f"max_z={F['max_z']:.2f}  n_feat={len(F)}\n")

    """
    =============================================================================
     Cell 4/5 : build cached feature tables, then run cross-condition protocols.

     Note on Paderborn: every bearing is measured under all four conditions, so
     leave-one-condition-out and leave-one-bearing-out cannot be crossed naively
     (removing the test condition's bearings empties the training set). The
     nested_split() protocol handles this with disjoint bearing subsets.
    =============================================================================
    """

    META_COLS = {"label", "kind", "condition", "unit", "file", "dataset",
                 "bearing_key", "severity", "or_pos", "rpm_source", "var",
                 "argmax_order", "fr", "null_med", "null_95", "null_std",
                 "n_null_used", "n_orders", "seg", "ufold"}


    def segments(sig, fs, fr, revs, overlap, max_seg):
        L = fast_len_leq(min(int(revs * fs / fr), len(sig)))
        step = max(1, int(L * (1 - overlap)))
        segs = [sig[i:i + L] for i in range(0, len(sig) - L + 1, step)]
        if not segs:
            return [sig[:fast_len_leq(len(sig))]]
        if len(segs) > max_seg:
            idx = np.linspace(0, len(segs) - 1, max_seg).astype(int)
            segs = [segs[i] for i in idx]
        return segs


    def build(records, tag, revs=100, overlap=0.0, max_seg=1, force=False):
        p = os.path.join(PATHS["CACHE"], f"feat_{tag}.parquet")
        if os.path.exists(p) and not force:
            df = pd.read_parquet(p)
            print(f"  cache hit {p}  ({len(df)} rows)")
            return df
        rows, t0 = [], time.time()
        for i, r in enumerate(records):
            for si, s in enumerate(segments(r.sig, r.fs, r.fr, revs, overlap, max_seg)):
                try:
                    F = extract_features(s, r.fs, r.fr, r.meta["bearing_key"],
                                         seed_str=f"{r.meta['file']}#{si}")
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"\n  [skip] {r.meta['file']}#{si}: {e}")
                    continue
                F.update(r.meta)
                F["seg"] = si
                rows.append(F)
            if i % 20 == 0:
                el = time.time() - t0
                print(f"  {tag} {i}/{len(records)}  {el:.0f}s  "
                      f"eta {el/max(i,1)*(len(records)-i):.0f}s", end="\r")
        df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        df.to_parquet(p)
        print(f"\n  {tag}: {len(df)} rows -> {p}")
        return df


    CW = build(cwru_records(verbose=False), "cwru",
               revs=50, overlap=0.75, max_seg=24)
    PB = build(pb_records(MAN, n_per_unit_condition=5, verbose=False), "pb",
               revs=100, overlap=0.0, max_seg=1)

    print("\nCWRU rows:")
    print(CW.groupby(["label", "condition"]).size().unstack(fill_value=0))
    print("\nPaderborn rows:")
    print(PB.groupby(["label", "condition"]).size().unstack(fill_value=0))
    print("\nPaderborn units per label:", PB.groupby("label").unit.nunique().to_dict())


    # --------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
    def XY(df, classes):
        d = df[df.label.isin(classes)].copy()
        num = d.select_dtypes(include=[np.number])
        cols = sorted(c for c in num.columns if c not in META_COLS)
        X = num[cols].replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna(0.0).values
        return X, d.label.values, d, cols


    def _fit_predict(tr, te, cls, kind):
        Xtr, ytr, _, _ = XY(tr, cls)
        Xte, yte, _, _ = XY(te, cls)
        q = QuantileTransformer(output_distribution="normal",
                                n_quantiles=min(500, max(10, len(Xtr))),
                                random_state=SEED).fit(Xtr)
        p = make_clf(kind).fit(q.transform(Xtr), ytr).predict(q.transform(Xte))
        return yte, p


    def leave_one_group_out(df, classes, group_col, kind="lr", tag="",
                            unseen_units=False, verbose=True):
        """Train on every group but one; test on the held-out group."""
        cls = [c for c in classes if c in set(df.label.unique())]
        rows = []
        for g in sorted(df[group_col].unique()):
            tr, te = df[df[group_col] != g], df[df[group_col] == g]
            if unseen_units:
                tr = tr[~tr.unit.isin(set(te.unit.unique()))]
            if tr.empty or te.empty or tr.label.nunique() < 2:
                continue
            yte, p = _fit_predict(tr, te, cls, kind)
            rows.append(dict(held_out=g, n_tr=len(tr), n_te=len(yte),
                             acc=round(accuracy_score(yte, p), 4),
                             macroF1=round(f1_score(yte, p, average="macro"), 4)))
        r = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        if verbose:
            print(f"\n=== {tag}  [{group_col}, unseen_units={unseen_units}, {kind}] ===")
            if r.empty:
                print("    NO VALID FOLDS — these two holdouts are not crossable here.")
                return r
            print(r.to_string(index=False))
            print(f"    MEAN acc={r.acc.mean():.4f} ± {r.acc.std():.4f}   "
                  f"macroF1={r.macroF1.mean():.4f}")
        return r


    def nested_split(df, classes, kind="hgb", n_unit_folds=3, tag="", seed=SEED):
        """Strict protocol: test on an UNSEEN condition measured on UNSEEN
           bearings. Bearings are split into disjoint folds within each label so
           both sides keep every class."""
        cls = [c for c in classes if c in set(df.label.unique())]
        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
        unit_fold = {}
        for lab in cls:
            us = list(rng.permutation(sorted(df[df.label == lab].unit.unique())))
            for i, u in enumerate(us):
                unit_fold[u] = i % n_unit_folds
        d = df.copy()
        d["ufold"] = d.unit.map(unit_fold)

        rows = []
        for cond in sorted(d.condition.unique()):
            for k in range(n_unit_folds):
                tr = d[(d.condition != cond) & (d.ufold != k)]
                te = d[(d.condition == cond) & (d.ufold == k)]
                if tr.empty or te.empty: continue
                if tr.label.nunique() < 2 or te.label.nunique() < 2: continue
                yte, p = _fit_predict(tr, te, cls, kind)
                rows.append(dict(condition=cond, ufold=k, n_tr=len(tr), n_te=len(yte),
                                 acc=round(accuracy_score(yte, p), 4),
                                 macroF1=round(f1_score(yte, p, average="macro"), 4)))
        r = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        print(f"\n=== {tag}  [UNSEEN condition + UNSEEN bearings, {kind}] ===")
        if r.empty:
            print("    NO VALID FOLDS"); return r
        print(r.to_string(index=False))
        print(f"    OVERALL acc={r.acc.mean():.4f} ± {r.acc.std():.4f}")
        print("\n  by condition:")
        print(r.groupby("condition")[["acc", "macroF1"]].mean().round(4))
        return r


    res = {}

    # ------------------------------------------- CWRU: leave-one-RPM(load)-out
    res["cwru_4"] = leave_one_group_out(CW, ["N","IR","OR","B"], "condition",
                                        "hgb", "CWRU 4-class")
    res["cwru_3"] = leave_one_group_out(CW, ["N","IR","OR"], "condition",
                                        "lr", "CWRU 3-class")

    # -------------------------------------- Paderborn: leave-one-CONDITION-out
    res["pb_3"] = leave_one_group_out(PB, ["N","IR","OR"], "condition",
                                      "hgb", "PB 3-class")
    res["pb_3l"] = leave_one_group_out(PB, ["N","IR","OR"], "condition",
                                       "lr", "PB 3-class linear")

    # ---------------------------------------- Paderborn: leave-one-BEARING-out
    res["pb_unit"] = leave_one_group_out(PB, ["N","IR","OR"], "unit",
                                         "hgb", "PB leave-one-bearing-out",
                                         verbose=False)
    print(f"\n=== PB leave-one-bearing-out (29 folds)  "
          f"MEAN acc={res['pb_unit'].acc.mean():.4f} ± {res['pb_unit'].acc.std():.4f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------ strict protocol
    res["pb_nested"]    = nested_split(PB, ["N","IR","OR"], "hgb",
                                       tag="PB 3-class STRICT")
    res["pb_nested_lr"] = nested_split(PB, ["N","IR","OR"], "lr",
                                       tag="PB 3-class STRICT linear")

    # ------------------------------------------------- isolate the speed effect
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("CONTROLLED TRANSFER — one factor changed at a time")
    for a, b, nm in [
        (("N15_M07_F10", "N15_M01_F10", "N15_M07_F04"), ("N09_M07_F10",),
         "SPEED  1500 -> 900 rpm"),
        (("N09_M07_F10",), ("N15_M07_F10",), "SPEED  900 -> 1500 rpm"),
        (("N15_M07_F10", "N15_M01_F10"), ("N15_M07_F04",),
         "FORCE  1000 -> 400 N"),
        (("N15_M07_F10", "N15_M07_F04"), ("N15_M01_F10",),
         "TORQUE 0.7 -> 0.1 Nm"),
    ]:
        tr, te = PB[PB.condition.isin(a)], PB[PB.condition.isin(b)]
        yte, p = _fit_predict(tr, te, ["N", "IR", "OR"], "hgb")
        print(f"  {nm:<28} acc={accuracy_score(yte,p):.4f}  "
              f"macroF1={f1_score(yte,p,average='macro'):.4f}  n_te={len(yte)}")

    # ------------------------------------------ training-free rule + detector
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("TRAINING-FREE RULE: predict OR if argmax_order==BPFO, IR if BPFI")
    for name, d in (("Paderborn", PB), ("CWRU", CW)):
        f = d[d.label.isin(["IR", "OR"])]
        if len(f):
            acc = ((f.label == "OR") == (f.argmax_order == "BPFO")).mean()
            print(f"\n  {name}: IR-vs-OR acc = {acc:.4f}  (n={len(f)})")
            print(pd.crosstab(d.label, d.argmax_order, normalize="index").round(3))
        if "N" in set(d.label):
            print(f"  healthy-vs-faulty AUC on max_z = "
                  f"{roc_auc_score((d.label != 'N').astype(int), d.max_z):.4f}")

    # ------------------------- is the speed failure physical or model-induced?
    print("\nmedian null-normalised scores by condition (Paderborn):")
    print(PB.groupby(["condition", "label"])[["max_z", "margin", "BPFO_z", "BPFI_z"]]
          .median().round(2))

    for k, v in res.items():
        if isinstance(v, pd.DataFrame) and not v.empty:
            save_table(v, f"T_{k}")

      -> results_v2\T_pb_nested_lr.csv

    # ===== does the training-free rule survive the speed shift? =====
    print("ARGMAX RULE, per condition (no training at all):")
    for c in sorted(PB.condition.unique()):
        d = PB[PB.condition == c]; f = d[d.label.isin(["IR","OR"])]
        acc = ((f.label == "OR") == (f.argmax_order == "BPFO")).mean()
        print(f"  {c:<14} IR-vs-OR acc={acc:.4f}  n={len(f)}")
    print()
    for c in sorted(CW.condition.unique()):
        d = CW[CW.condition == c]; f = d[d.label.isin(["IR","OR"])]
        acc = ((f.label == "OR") == (f.argmax_order == "BPFO")).mean()
        print(f"  CWRU {c:<9} IR-vs-OR acc={acc:.4f}  n={len(f)}")

    # ===== two-stage rule: max_z gate for health, argmax for fault type =====
    def two_stage(df_tr, df_te, cls=("N","IR","OR")):
        """Only ONE parameter is fitted: the max_z health threshold, chosen on
           the training condition. Fault typing is parameter-free."""
        tr = df_tr[df_tr.label.isin(cls)]
        best_t, best_a = None, -1
        for t in np.quantile(tr.max_z, np.linspace(0.05, 0.95, 60)):
            p = np.where(tr.max_z < t, "N",
                         np.where(tr.argmax_order == "BPFO", "OR", "IR"))
            a = (p == tr.label.values).mean()
            if a > best_a: best_a, best_t = a, t
        te = df_te[df_te.label.isin(cls)]
        p = np.where(te.max_z < best_t, "N",
                     np.where(te.argmax_order == "BPFO", "OR", "IR"))
        return best_t, best_a, accuracy_score(te.label, p), \
               f1_score(te.label, p, average="macro"), te.label.values, p

    print("\n" + "="*70)
    print("TWO-STAGE PHYSICS RULE vs TRAINED MODEL, leave-one-condition-out")
    print(f"{'held out':<14}{'rule acc':>10}{'rule F1':>10}{'model acc':>11}{'thr':>7}")
    rows = []
    for c in sorted(PB.condition.unique()):
        tr, te = PB[PB.condition != c], PB[PB.condition == c]
        t, atr, ate, f1te, y, p = two_stage(tr, te)
        m_acc = res["pb_3"].set_index("held_out").loc[c, "acc"]
        rows.append(dict(condition=c, rule_acc=round(ate,4), rule_f1=round(f1te,4),
                         model_acc=m_acc, thr=round(t,2)))
        print(f"{c:<14}{ate:>10.4f}{f1te:>10.4f}{m_acc:>11.4f}{t:>7.2f}")
    r2 = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    print(f"{'MEAN':<14}{r2.rule_acc.mean():>10.4f}{r2.rule_f1.mean():>10.4f}"
          f"{r2.model_acc.mean():>11.4f}")
    save_table(r2, "T_two_stage_vs_model")

    # ===== confusion for the worst fold (900 rpm) =====
    tr, te = PB[PB.condition != "N09_M07_F10"], PB[PB.condition == "N09_M07_F10"]
    _, _, _, _, y, p = two_stage(tr, te)
    report(y, p, ["N","IR","OR"], "TWO-STAGE RULE @ 900 rpm (model got 0.5172)")

    # ===== does per-condition rank normalisation rescue the trained model? =====
    def rank_normalise(df):
        """Rank features within each condition -- removes the amplitude shift
           while using no labels. Transductive; must be declared as such."""
        d = df.copy()
        num = [c for c in d.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns
               if c not in META_COLS]
        for c in sorted(d.condition.unique()):
            m = d.condition == c
            d.loc[m, num] = d.loc[m, num].rank(pct=True).values
        return d

    PBr = rank_normalise(PB)
    res["pb_3_rank"] = leave_one_group_out(PBr, ["N","IR","OR"], "condition",
                                           "hgb", "PB 3-class RANK-NORMALISED")
    res["pb_nested_rank"] = nested_split(PBr, ["N","IR","OR"], "hgb",
                                         tag="PB STRICT RANK-NORMALISED")

    # ===== which features are scale-free, and does restricting to them help? =====
    SCALE_FREE = lambda c: (c.startswith("LR_") or c.startswith("R_")
                            or c == "margin" or c.endswith("_sb")
                            or c.endswith("_sig") or c == "n_sig")
    ABSOLUTE   = lambda c: (c.endswith("_p2f") or c.endswith("_z")
                            or c.endswith("_zs") or c.endswith("_harm")
                            or c == "max_z" or c in ("sig_kurt","sig_skew",
                                                     "crest","rms_log"))

    def family_holdout(df, classes, subset_fn, name, kind="hgb", group_col="condition"):
        cls = [c for c in classes if c in set(df.label.unique())]
        accs, f1s = [], []
        for g in sorted(df[group_col].unique()):
            tr, te = df[df[group_col] != g], df[df[group_col] == g]
            Xtr, ytr, _, cols = XY(tr, cls); Xte, yte, _, _ = XY(te, cls)
            keep = [i for i, c in enumerate(cols) if subset_fn(c)]
            if len(keep) < 2: print(f"  {name}: too few features"); return None
            q = QuantileTransformer(output_distribution="normal",
                                    n_quantiles=min(500, len(Xtr)),
                                    random_state=SEED).fit(Xtr[:, keep])
            p = make_clf(kind).fit(q.transform(Xtr[:, keep]), ytr) \
                              .predict(q.transform(Xte[:, keep]))
            accs.append(accuracy_score(yte, p)); f1s.append(f1_score(yte, p, average="macro"))
        print(f"  {name:<26} n={len(keep):>2}  mean={np.mean(accs):.4f} "
              f"± {np.std(accs):.4f}   per-fold={np.round(accs,3)}")
        return dict(family=name, n_feat=len(keep), mean_acc=round(np.mean(accs),4),
                    std=round(np.std(accs),4), mean_f1=round(np.mean(f1s),4))

    print("PADERBORN — leave-one-condition-out, by feature family (hgb):")
    fam = [family_holdout(PB, ["N","IR","OR"], f, n) for f, n in [
        (lambda c: True,                    "ALL"),
        (SCALE_FREE,                        "SCALE-FREE only"),
        (ABSOLUTE,                          "ABSOLUTE only"),
        (lambda c: c.startswith("LR_"),     "log-ratios only"),
        (lambda c: not ABSOLUTE(c),         "ALL minus absolute"),
    ]]
    T_fam = pd.DataFrame([f for f in fam if f]); save_table(T_fam, "T_feature_family_pb")

    print("\nCWRU — leave-one-load-out, by feature family (hgb):")
    famc = [family_holdout(CW, ["N","IR","OR","B"], f, n) for f, n in [
        (lambda c: True,                    "ALL"),
        (SCALE_FREE,                        "SCALE-FREE only"),
        (ABSOLUTE,                          "ABSOLUTE only"),
        (lambda c: not ABSOLUTE(c),         "ALL minus absolute"),
    ]]
    save_table(pd.DataFrame([f for f in famc if f]), "T_feature_family_cwru")

    # ===== best combination: scale-free features on rank-normalised data =====
    print("\nSTRICT protocol, scale-free features:")
    def nested_subset(df, classes, subset_fn, name, kind="hgb", n_unit_folds=3):
        cls = [c for c in classes if c in set(df.label.unique())]
        rng = np.random.RandomState(SEED); uf = {}
        for lab in cls:
            us = list(rng.permutation(sorted(df[df.label==lab].unit.unique())))
            for i,u in enumerate(us): uf[u] = i % n_unit_folds
        d = df.copy(); d["ufold"] = d.unit.map(uf)
        accs = []
        for cond in sorted(d.condition.unique()):
            for k in range(n_unit_folds):
                tr = d[(d.condition!=cond)&(d.ufold!=k)]; te = d[(d.condition==cond)&(d.ufold==k)]
                if tr.empty or te.empty or te.label.nunique()<2: continue
                Xtr,ytr,_,cols = XY(tr,cls); Xte,yte,_,_ = XY(te,cls)
                keep = [i for i,c in enumerate(cols) if subset_fn(c)]
                q = QuantileTransformer(output_distribution="normal",
                                        n_quantiles=min(500,len(Xtr)),
                                        random_state=SEED).fit(Xtr[:,keep])
                p = make_clf(kind).fit(q.transform(Xtr[:,keep]),ytr).predict(q.transform(Xte[:,keep]))
                accs.append(accuracy_score(yte,p))
        print(f"  {name:<26} mean={np.mean(accs):.4f} ± {np.std(accs):.4f}")
        return np.mean(accs), np.std(accs)

    nested_subset(PB,  ["N","IR","OR"], lambda c: True, "ALL, raw")
    nested_subset(PB,  ["N","IR","OR"], SCALE_FREE,     "SCALE-FREE, raw")
    nested_subset(PBr, ["N","IR","OR"], lambda c: True, "ALL, rank-norm")
    nested_subset(PBr, ["N","IR","OR"], SCALE_FREE,     "SCALE-FREE, rank-norm")

      ABSOLUTE only              n=17  mean=0.7379 ± 0.1902   per-fold=[0.441 0.924 0.703 0.883]

    def save_publication_figure(fig, stem, supplementary=False):
        """
        Save every figure in raster and vector formats.

        PNG: visual inspection, presentations and submission previews
        PDF: manuscript vector graphics
        SVG: editable vector graphics
        """
        target = (
            MSSP_DIRS["figures_supp"]
            if supplementary
            else MSSP_DIRS["figures_main"]
        )

        paths = {}
        for extension in ("png", "pdf", "svg"):
            path = target / f"{stem}.{extension}"
            fig.savefig(path, format=extension)
            paths[extension] = str(path)

        return paths

    def json_converter(value):
        if isinstance(value, Path):
            return str(value)

        if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
            return value.tolist()

        if isinstance(value, np.integer):
            return int(value)

        if isinstance(value, np.floating):
            return float(value)

        if isinstance(value, np.bool_):
            return bool(value)

        if pd.isna(value):
            return None

        return str(value)


    def dataframe_digest(df):
        """
        Deterministic digest of a DataFrame.

        This lets us confirm later that exactly the same feature table was used.
        """
        preferred_sort = [
            "dataset", "label", "kind", "unit",
            "condition", "file", "seg"
        ]

        sort_columns = [c for c in preferred_sort if c in df.columns]
        ordered_columns = sorted(df.columns)

        work = df[ordered_columns].copy()

        if sort_columns:
            work = work.sort_values(
                sort_columns,
                kind="mergesort",
                na_position="last"
            )

        work = work.reset_index(drop=True)

        hashed = pd.util.hash_pandas_object(
            work,
            index=True,
            categorize=True
        ).values

        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        digest.update("|".join(ordered_columns).encode("utf-8"))
        digest.update(hashed.tobytes())

        return digest.hexdigest()


    def numeric_feature_columns(df):
        numeric = df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns

        return sorted(
            c for c in numeric
            if c not in META_COLS
        )


    def audit_feature_table(df, dataset_name):
        required = {
            "label", "condition", "unit", "file",
            "dataset", "bearing_key"
        }

        missing_required = sorted(required - set(df.columns))

        if missing_required:
            raise ValueError(
                f"{dataset_name}: missing required columns: "
                f"{missing_required}"
            )

        features = numeric_feature_columns(df)
        feature_matrix = (
            df[features]
            .replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
        )

        all_nan_features = [
            c for c in features
            if feature_matrix[c].isna().all()
        ]

        constant_features = [
            c for c in features
            if feature_matrix[c].nunique(dropna=True) <= 1
        ]

        observation_key = [
            c for c in
            ["dataset", "unit", "condition", "file", "seg"]
            if c in df.columns
        ]

        duplicate_observations = (
            int(df.duplicated(observation_key).sum())
            if observation_key
            else np.nan
        )

        unit_label_count = df.groupby("unit")["label"].nunique()
        units_with_multiple_labels = int((unit_label_count > 1).sum())

        file_label_count = df.groupby("file")["label"].nunique()
        files_with_multiple_labels = int((file_label_count > 1).sum())

        condition_unit_coverage = (
            df[["unit", "condition"]]
            .drop_duplicates()
            .groupby("unit")["condition"]
            .nunique()
        )

        result = {
            "dataset": dataset_name,
            "rows": int(len(df)),
            "columns": int(df.shape[1]),
            "numeric_features": int(len(features)),
            "classes": int(df["label"].nunique()),
            "class_names": ",".join(sorted(df["label"].astype(str).unique())),
            "conditions": int(df["condition"].nunique()),
            "units": int(df["unit"].nunique()),
            "files": int(df["file"].nunique()),
            "segments": (
                int(df["seg"].nunique())
                if "seg" in df.columns
                else 1
            ),
            "duplicate_observations": duplicate_observations,
            "units_with_multiple_labels": units_with_multiple_labels,
            "files_with_multiple_labels": files_with_multiple_labels,
            "feature_nan_cells": int(feature_matrix.isna().sum().sum()),
            "feature_inf_cells": int(
                np.isinf(
                    df[features].to_numpy(dtype=float)
                ).sum()
            ),
            "all_nan_features": ",".join(all_nan_features),
            "constant_features": ",".join(constant_features),
            "min_conditions_per_unit": int(condition_unit_coverage.min()),
            "max_conditions_per_unit": int(condition_unit_coverage.max()),
            "dataframe_sha256": dataframe_digest(df),
        }

        return result, features


    def make_balance_table(df, dataset_name):
        grouped = (
            df.groupby(
                ["label", "condition"],
                dropna=False
            )
            .agg(
                rows=("label", "size"),
                units=("unit", "nunique"),
                files=("file", "nunique"),
            )
            .reset_index()
        )

        grouped.insert(0, "dataset", dataset_name)

        return grouped


    def make_unit_table(df, dataset_name):
        columns = [
            c for c in
            ["unit", "label", "kind", "condition", "file", "seg"]
            if c in df.columns
        ]

        unit_table = (
            df[columns]
            .groupby(
                [c for c in ["unit", "label", "kind"] if c in columns],
                dropna=False
            )
            .agg(
                conditions=("condition", "nunique"),
                files=("file", "nunique"),
                rows=("file", "size"),
            )
            .reset_index()
        )

        unit_table.insert(0, "dataset", dataset_name)

        return unit_table

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Run audits
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    cw_audit, cw_features = audit_feature_table(CW, "CWRU")
    pb_audit, pb_features = audit_feature_table(PB, "Paderborn")

    AUDIT_SUMMARY = pd.DataFrame([cw_audit, pb_audit])

    DATA_BALANCE = pd.concat(
        [
            make_balance_table(CW, "CWRU"),
            make_balance_table(PB, "Paderborn"),
        ],
        ignore_index=True
    )

    UNIT_SUMMARY = pd.concat(
        [
            make_unit_table(CW, "CWRU"),
            make_unit_table(PB, "Paderborn"),
        ],
        ignore_index=True
    )

    AUDIT_SUMMARY.to_csv(
        MSSP_DIRS["audit"] / "audit_summary.csv",
        index=False
    )

    DATA_BALANCE.to_csv(
        MSSP_DIRS["audit"] / "data_balance.csv",
        index=False
    )

    UNIT_SUMMARY.to_csv(
        MSSP_DIRS["audit"] / "unit_summary.csv",
        index=False
    )

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Operating-condition overlap audit
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def condition_unit_overlap(df, dataset_name):
        conditions = sorted(df["condition"].unique())
        rows = []

        for test_condition in conditions:
            test_units = set(
                df.loc[
                    df["condition"] == test_condition,
                    "unit"
                ]
            )

            training_units = set(
                df.loc[
                    df["condition"] != test_condition,
                    "unit"
                ]
            )

            shared = test_units & training_units

            rows.append({
                "dataset": dataset_name,
                "test_condition": test_condition,
                "test_units": len(test_units),
                "training_units": len(training_units),
                "shared_units": len(shared),
                "test_units_seen_in_training_fraction": (
                    len(shared) / max(len(test_units), 1)
                ),
            })

        return pd.DataFrame(rows)


    CONDITION_OVERLAP = pd.concat(
        [
            condition_unit_overlap(CW, "CWRU"),
            condition_unit_overlap(PB, "Paderborn"),
        ],
        ignore_index=True
    )

    CONDITION_OVERLAP.to_csv(
        MSSP_DIRS["audit"] / "condition_unit_overlap.csv",
        index=False
    )
