    Send the complete decision report. We will use its false-positive rate and target-specific power to accept or revise the null design before running the full Monte Carlo experiment.

    """
    =============================================================================
    MSSP UPGRADE
    Cell 9: repeated split-conformal healthy-reference calibration -- pilot
    =============================================================================

    Run after Cell 8. This cell reuses the saved 620 simulation trials and does
    not repeat signal generation or feature extraction.

    Scientific correction
    ---------------------
    The order-space tail fractions from Cell 7 are retained only as descriptive
    within-signal evidence. Pilot Cell 8 showed that they are not calibrated
    p-values because physical target orders and random null orders are not
    exchangeable under all noise regimes.

    Cell 9 calibrates the family statistic

        T(x) = max(BPFO_z, BPFI_z, BSF_z)

    against independent healthy calibration signals. The conformal family
    probability is

        p_family = (1 + number of calibration T >= test T) / (n_cal + 1).

    Because the maximum is calibrated directly, no additional Holm correction is
    needed for the family-level healthy/faulty decision. Fault localization remains
    the order attaining the largest calibrated evidence score.
    """

    from pathlib import Path
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    import hashlib
    import json

    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Prerequisites and configuration
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    required_cell9_names = [
        "MSSP_DIRS",
        "MSSP_COLORS",
        "save_publication_figure",
    ]

    missing_cell9_names = [
        name for name in required_cell9_names
        if name not in globals()
    ]

    if missing_cell9_names:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Run Cell 6 first. Missing: " + ", ".join(missing_cell9_names)
        )

    CONFORMAL_CONFIG = {
        "seed": 20260819,
        "n_repeated_splits": 100,
        "calibration_fraction": 0.60,
        "alpha": 0.05,
        "stratify_columns": ["bearing_key", "noise_profile", "fr"],
        "family_score": "max_z",
    }

    CONFORMAL_ROOT = Path(MSSP_DIRS["statistics"]) / "conformal_pilot"
    CONFORMAL_ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


    def configuration_digest(configuration):
        text = json.dumps(
            configuration,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        )
        return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


    CONFORMAL_CONFIG_HASH = configuration_digest(CONFORMAL_CONFIG)

    with open(
        CONFORMAL_ROOT / "conformal_pilot_config.json",
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8",
    ) as file:
        json.dump(
            {
                "created_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                "config_sha256": CONFORMAL_CONFIG_HASH,
                "config": CONFORMAL_CONFIG,
            },
            file,
            indent=2,
        )


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Load Cell 8 trials if necessary
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    if "SIM_TRIALS" not in globals():
        candidate = (
            Path(MSSP_DIRS["simulation"]) /
            "pilot" /
            "simulation_trials_pilot.csv"
        )

        if not candidate.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(
                "Cell 8 results are not in memory and the saved pilot file "
                f"was not found: {candidate}"
            )

        SIM_TRIALS = pd.read_csv(candidate)

    required_trial_columns = {
        "trial_id",
        "hypothesis",
        "bearing_key",
        "noise_profile",
        "fr",
        "target_order",
        "snr_db",
        "max_z",
        "argmax_order",
        "argmax_raw_order",
        "min_p_holm",
        "BPFO_z",
        "BPFI_z",
    }

    missing_trial_columns = sorted(
        required_trial_columns - set(SIM_TRIALS.columns)
    )

    if missing_trial_columns:
        raise ValueError(
            "Cell 8 trial table is missing: " + ", ".join(missing_trial_columns)
        )

    SIM_TRIALS = SIM_TRIALS.copy()
    SIM_TRIALS["bearing_key"] = SIM_TRIALS["bearing_key"].astype(str)

    healthy_all = SIM_TRIALS[
        SIM_TRIALS.hypothesis == "H0"
    ].copy()
    faulty_all = SIM_TRIALS[
        SIM_TRIALS.hypothesis == "H1"
    ].copy()

    if healthy_all.empty or faulty_all.empty:
        raise RuntimeError("Both healthy and faulty simulation trials are required")


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Conformal utilities
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def conformal_upper_probability(calibration_scores, test_scores):
        """Vectorized finite-sample upper-tail split-conformal probability."""
        calibration_scores = np.asarray(calibration_scores, dtype=float)
        calibration_scores = calibration_scores[np.isfinite(calibration_scores)]
        test_scores = np.asarray(test_scores, dtype=float)

        if calibration_scores.size == 0:
            raise ValueError("Empty conformal calibration set")

        exceedances = (
            calibration_scores[None, :] >= test_scores[:, None]
        ).sum(axis=1)

        return (1.0 + exceedances) / (calibration_scores.size + 1.0)


    def stratified_calibration_split(
        healthy_frame,
        rng,
        calibration_fraction,
        stratum_columns,
    ):
        calibration_indices = []
        evaluation_indices = []

        for _, stratum in healthy_frame.groupby(
            stratum_columns,
            sort=True,
            dropna=False,
        ):
            indices = stratum.index.to_numpy()
            indices = rng.permutation(indices)

            n_total = len(indices)

            if n_total < 2:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Every conformal stratum needs at least two healthy trials"
                )

            n_calibration = int(round(calibration_fraction * n_total))
            n_calibration = min(max(1, n_calibration), n_total - 1)

            calibration_indices.extend(indices[:n_calibration])
            evaluation_indices.extend(indices[n_calibration:])

        return (
            np.asarray(calibration_indices, dtype=int),
            np.asarray(evaluation_indices, dtype=int),
        )


    def minimum_attainable_probability(n_calibration):
        return 1.0 / (n_calibration + 1.0)


    def conformal_alarm_threshold(calibration_scores, alpha):
        """
        Smallest score at which the finite-sample conformal probability can be
        <= alpha. With small pilot calibration sets this is often just above the
        maximum healthy calibration score.
        """
        scores = np.sort(np.asarray(calibration_scores, dtype=float))
        n = len(scores)
        allowed_exceedances = int(np.floor(alpha * (n + 1) - 1.0 + 1e-12))

        if allowed_exceedances < 0:
            return np.inf

        rank_from_top = min(allowed_exceedances, n - 1)
        return float(scores[n - 1 - rank_from_top])


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Repeated stratified split-conformal evaluation
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    split_rng = np.random.default_rng(CONFORMAL_CONFIG["seed"])
    split_seeds = split_rng.integers(
        0,
        2 ** 32 - 1,
        size=CONFORMAL_CONFIG["n_repeated_splits"],
        dtype=np.uint32,
    )

    h0_result_frames = []
    h1_result_frames = []
    calibration_records = []

    for split_index, split_seed in enumerate(split_seeds):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(split_seed))

        calibration_index, evaluation_index = stratified_calibration_split(
            healthy_frame=healthy_all,
            rng=rng,
            calibration_fraction=CONFORMAL_CONFIG["calibration_fraction"],
            stratum_columns=CONFORMAL_CONFIG["stratify_columns"],
        )

        healthy_calibration = healthy_all.loc[calibration_index].copy()
        healthy_evaluation = healthy_all.loc[evaluation_index].copy()

        for bearing_key in sorted(SIM_TRIALS.bearing_key.unique()):
            cal = healthy_calibration[
                healthy_calibration.bearing_key == bearing_key
            ].copy()
            h0_test = healthy_evaluation[
                healthy_evaluation.bearing_key == bearing_key
            ].copy()
            h1_test = faulty_all[
                faulty_all.bearing_key == bearing_key
            ].copy()

            if cal.empty or h0_test.empty or h1_test.empty:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"Incomplete split for bearing {bearing_key}"
                )

            calibration_scores = cal[
                CONFORMAL_CONFIG["family_score"]
            ].to_numpy(dtype=float)

            calibration_records.append({
                "split": split_index,
                "split_seed": int(split_seed),
                "bearing_key": bearing_key,
                "n_calibration": len(cal),
                "n_h0_evaluation": len(h0_test),
                "minimum_p": minimum_attainable_probability(len(cal)),
                "threshold_alpha": conformal_alarm_threshold(
                    calibration_scores,
                    CONFORMAL_CONFIG["alpha"],
                ),
                "calibration_score_min": float(np.min(calibration_scores)),
                "calibration_score_median": float(
                    np.median(calibration_scores)
                ),
                "calibration_score_max": float(np.max(calibration_scores)),
            })

            h0_test["split"] = split_index
            h0_test["split_seed"] = int(split_seed)
            h0_test["family_p_conformal"] = conformal_upper_probability(
                calibration_scores,
                h0_test[CONFORMAL_CONFIG["family_score"]].to_numpy(),
            )
            h0_test["alarm_conformal"] = (
                h0_test["family_p_conformal"] <=
                CONFORMAL_CONFIG["alpha"]
            ).astype(int)
            h0_test["alarm_order_null"] = (
                h0_test["min_p_holm"] <= CONFORMAL_CONFIG["alpha"]
            ).astype(int)
            h0_result_frames.append(h0_test)

            h1_test["split"] = split_index
            h1_test["split_seed"] = int(split_seed)
            h1_test["family_p_conformal"] = conformal_upper_probability(
                calibration_scores,
                h1_test[CONFORMAL_CONFIG["family_score"]].to_numpy(),
            )
            h1_test["alarm_conformal"] = (
                h1_test["family_p_conformal"] <=
                CONFORMAL_CONFIG["alpha"]
            ).astype(int)
            h1_test["correct_localization_calibrated"] = (
                h1_test["argmax_order"] == h1_test["target_order"]
            ).astype(int)
            h1_test["correct_localization_raw"] = (
                h1_test["argmax_raw_order"] == h1_test["target_order"]
            ).astype(int)
            h1_test["correct_diagnosis_conformal"] = (
                h1_test["alarm_conformal"].astype(bool) &
                h1_test["correct_localization_calibrated"].astype(bool)
            ).astype(int)
            h1_result_frames.append(h1_test)

    H0_CONFORMAL_RESULTS = pd.concat(h0_result_frames, ignore_index=True)
    H1_CONFORMAL_RESULTS = pd.concat(h1_result_frames, ignore_index=True)
    CONFORMAL_CALIBRATION_SETS = pd.DataFrame(calibration_records)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Split-level metrics
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def split_rate_table(dataframe, group_columns, indicator, metric_name):
        result = (
            dataframe.groupby(group_columns, dropna=False)[indicator]
            .agg(["sum", "count", "mean"])
            .reset_index()
            .rename(
                columns={
                    "sum": "successes",
                    "count": "n",
                    "mean": "rate",
                }
            )
        )
        result["metric"] = metric_name
        return result


    FPR_CONFORMAL_BY_SPLIT = split_rate_table(
        H0_CONFORMAL_RESULTS,
        ["split", "bearing_key"],
        "alarm_conformal",
        "Conformal family FPR",
    )

    FPR_ORDER_NULL_BY_SPLIT = split_rate_table(
        H0_CONFORMAL_RESULTS,
        ["split", "bearing_key"],
        "alarm_order_null",
        "Order-null Holm FPR",
    )

    FPR_BY_PROFILE_SPLIT = split_rate_table(
        H0_CONFORMAL_RESULTS,
        ["split", "bearing_key", "noise_profile"],
        "alarm_conformal",
        "Conformal family FPR",
    )

    POWER_BY_SPLIT = split_rate_table(
        H1_CONFORMAL_RESULTS,
        ["split", "bearing_key", "target_order", "snr_db"],
        "alarm_conformal",
        "Conformal detection power",
    )

    CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS_BY_SPLIT = split_rate_table(
        H1_CONFORMAL_RESULTS,
        ["split", "bearing_key", "target_order", "snr_db"],
        "correct_diagnosis_conformal",
        "Conformal correct-diagnosis power",
    )


    def summarize_across_splits(dataframe, group_columns):
        return (
            dataframe.groupby(group_columns, dropna=False)["rate"]
            .agg(
                mean="mean",
                std="std",
                median="median",
                q05=lambda values: values.quantile(0.05),
                q95=lambda values: values.quantile(0.95),
                minimum="min",
                maximum="max",
            )
            .reset_index()
        )


    FPR_CONFORMAL_SUMMARY = summarize_across_splits(
        FPR_CONFORMAL_BY_SPLIT,
        ["bearing_key"],
    )

    FPR_ORDER_NULL_SUMMARY = summarize_across_splits(
        FPR_ORDER_NULL_BY_SPLIT,
        ["bearing_key"],
    )

    FPR_PROFILE_SUMMARY = summarize_across_splits(
        FPR_BY_PROFILE_SPLIT,
        ["bearing_key", "noise_profile"],
    )

    POWER_CONFORMAL_SUMMARY = summarize_across_splits(
        POWER_BY_SPLIT,
        ["bearing_key", "target_order", "snr_db"],
    )

    CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS_SUMMARY = summarize_across_splits(
        CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS_BY_SPLIT,
        ["bearing_key", "target_order", "snr_db"],
    )


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Save complete results and tables
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    result_files = {
        "conformal_h0_repeated_splits.csv": H0_CONFORMAL_RESULTS,
        "conformal_h1_repeated_splits.csv": H1_CONFORMAL_RESULTS,
        "conformal_calibration_sets.csv": CONFORMAL_CALIBRATION_SETS,
    }

    for filename, dataframe in result_files.items():
        dataframe.to_csv(CONFORMAL_ROOT / filename, index=False)

    table_outputs = {
        "T_conformal_fpr_pilot.csv": FPR_CONFORMAL_SUMMARY,
        "T_order_null_fpr_pilot.csv": FPR_ORDER_NULL_SUMMARY,
        "T_conformal_fpr_by_profile_pilot.csv": FPR_PROFILE_SUMMARY,
        "T_conformal_power_pilot.csv": POWER_CONFORMAL_SUMMARY,
        "T_conformal_correct_diagnosis_pilot.csv": (
            CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS_SUMMARY
        ),
        "T_conformal_calibration_sizes_pilot.csv": (
            CONFORMAL_CALIBRATION_SETS
            .groupby("bearing_key")
            .agg(
                n_calibration=("n_calibration", "median"),
                n_h0_evaluation=("n_h0_evaluation", "median"),
                minimum_p=("minimum_p", "median"),
                threshold_median=("threshold_alpha", "median"),
                threshold_q05=(
                    "threshold_alpha",
                    lambda values: values.quantile(0.05),
                ),
                threshold_q95=(
                    "threshold_alpha",
                    lambda values: values.quantile(0.95),
                ),
            )
            .reset_index()
        ),
    }

    for filename, dataframe in table_outputs.items():
        dataframe.to_csv(
            Path(MSSP_DIRS["tables"]) / filename,
            index=False,
        )


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Figures
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def short_bearing_name(bearing_key):
        name = str(bearing_key).replace("CWRU_DE_", "CWRU ")
        return name.replace("PADERBORN_", "PB ")


    def figure_fpr_comparison():
        bearings = sorted(SIM_TRIALS.bearing_key.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(bearings),
            figsize=(9.4, 4.2),
            sharey=True,
            squeeze=False,
        )
        axes = axes.ravel()

        for axis, bearing_key in zip(axes, bearings):
            raw = FPR_ORDER_NULL_BY_SPLIT.loc[
                FPR_ORDER_NULL_BY_SPLIT.bearing_key == bearing_key,
                "rate",
            ].to_numpy()
            conformal = FPR_CONFORMAL_BY_SPLIT.loc[
                FPR_CONFORMAL_BY_SPLIT.bearing_key == bearing_key,
                "rate",
            ].to_numpy()

            box = axis.boxplot(
                [raw, conformal],
                labels=["Order-null\nHolm", "Healthy-reference\nconformal"],
                patch_artist=True,
                widths=0.60,
                showmeans=True,
            )
            box["boxes"][0].set_facecolor("#E45756")
            box["boxes"][1].set_facecolor("#4C78A8")

            for patch in box["boxes"]:
                patch.set_alpha(0.75)

            axis.axhline(
                CONFORMAL_CONFIG["alpha"],
                color="black",
                linestyle="--",
                linewidth=1.2,
                label="Nominal 0.05",
            )
            axis.set_title(short_bearing_name(bearing_key))
            axis.set_ylim(-0.02, 0.65)
            axis.legend(loc="upper right", fontsize=8)

        axes[0].set_ylabel("Healthy family-wise false-positive rate")
        fig.suptitle(
            "Reference-free order ranks versus healthy-reference calibration",
            y=1.02,
        )
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_conformal_curves(summary, title, ylabel):
        definitions = list(
            summary[["bearing_key", "target_order"]]
            .drop_duplicates()
            .sort_values(["bearing_key", "target_order"])
            .itertuples(index=False, name=None)
        )
        n_columns = 3
        n_rows = int(np.ceil(len(definitions) / n_columns))
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            n_rows,
            n_columns,
            figsize=(12.2, 3.7 * n_rows),
            sharex=True,
            sharey=True,
            squeeze=False,
        )
        flat_axes = axes.ravel()

        for axis, (bearing_key, target_order) in zip(flat_axes, definitions):
            group = summary[
                (summary.bearing_key == bearing_key) &
                (summary.target_order == target_order)
            ].sort_values("snr_db")

            axis.plot(
                group["snr_db"],
                group["mean"],
                marker="o",
                color=MSSP_COLORS[target_order],
                linewidth=1.8,
            )
            axis.fill_between(
                group["snr_db"].to_numpy(dtype=float),
                group["q05"].to_numpy(dtype=float),
                group["q95"].to_numpy(dtype=float),
                color=MSSP_COLORS[target_order],
                alpha=0.18,
                label="5th-95th split percentile",
            )
            axis.set_title(
                f"{short_bearing_name(bearing_key)}: {target_order}"
            )
            axis.set_xlabel("Fault-to-background SNR (dB)")
            axis.set_ylim(-0.03, 1.03)
            axis.set_xticks(sorted(faulty_all.snr_db.unique()))
            axis.legend(loc="lower right", fontsize=7.5)

        for axis in flat_axes[len(definitions):]:
            axis.set_visible(False)

        for row_index in range(n_rows):
            axes[row_index, 0].set_ylabel(ylabel)

        fig.suptitle(title, y=1.01)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_profile_fpr():
        bearings = sorted(SIM_TRIALS.bearing_key.unique())
        profiles = sorted(healthy_all.noise_profile.unique())
        x_positions = np.arange(len(profiles))
        width = 0.34
        fig, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(8.8, 4.5))

        for bearing_index, bearing_key in enumerate(bearings):
            group = (
                FPR_PROFILE_SUMMARY[
                    FPR_PROFILE_SUMMARY.bearing_key == bearing_key
                ]
                .set_index("noise_profile")
                .reindex(profiles)
            )
            positions = x_positions + (
                bearing_index - (len(bearings) - 1) / 2
            ) * width
            axis.bar(
                positions,
                group["mean"],
                width=width,
                label=short_bearing_name(bearing_key),
                alpha=0.82,
            )

        axis.axhline(
            CONFORMAL_CONFIG["alpha"],
            color="black",
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=1.2,
            label="Nominal 0.05",
        )
        axis.set_xticks(x_positions)
        axis.set_xticklabels(profiles)
        axis.set_ylabel("Mean conformal false-positive rate")
        axis.set_title("Residual calibration error by noise regime")
        axis.set_ylim(0, 0.35)
        axis.legend()
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    figure_jobs = [
        (
            figure_fpr_comparison(),
            "S008_fpr_order_null_vs_conformal_pilot",
        ),
        (
            figure_conformal_curves(
                POWER_CONFORMAL_SUMMARY,
                "Healthy-reference conformal fault-detection power",
                "Detection probability",
            ),
            "S009_conformal_detection_power_pilot",
        ),
        (
            figure_conformal_curves(
                CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS_SUMMARY,
                "Conformal detection with correct fault localization",
                "Correct-diagnosis probability",
            ),
            "S010_conformal_correct_diagnosis_pilot",
        ),
        (
            figure_profile_fpr(),
            "S011_conformal_fpr_by_noise_pilot",
        ),
    ]

    CELL9_FIGURE_PATHS = {}

    for figure, stem in figure_jobs:
        CELL9_FIGURE_PATHS[stem] = save_publication_figure(
            figure,
            stem,
            supplementary=True,
        )
        plt.show()
        plt.close(figure)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 8. Decision report
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    raw_fpr_report = FPR_ORDER_NULL_SUMMARY.set_index("bearing_key")
    conformal_fpr_report = FPR_CONFORMAL_SUMMARY.set_index("bearing_key")

    power_report = POWER_CONFORMAL_SUMMARY.pivot_table(
        index=["bearing_key", "target_order"],
        columns="snr_db",
        values="mean",
    )

    diagnosis_report = CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS_SUMMARY.pivot_table(
        index=["bearing_key", "target_order"],
        columns="snr_db",
        values="mean",
    )

    print("\n" + "=" * 92)
    print("CELL 9 CONFORMAL PILOT DECISION REPORT")
    print("=" * 92)

    print("\nHealthy family-wise false-positive rate across repeated splits:")

    for bearing_key in raw_fpr_report.index:
        print(
            f"  {bearing_key:<18} "
            f"order-null mean={raw_fpr_report.loc[bearing_key, 'mean']:.4f}  "
            f"conformal mean={conformal_fpr_report.loc[bearing_key, 'mean']:.4f}  "
            f"conformal 5-95%=["
            f"{conformal_fpr_report.loc[bearing_key, 'q05']:.4f}, "
            f"{conformal_fpr_report.loc[bearing_key, 'q95']:.4f}]"
        )

    print("\nMean conformal detection power:")
    print(power_report.round(3).to_string())

    print("\nMean conformal correct-diagnosis power:")
    print(diagnosis_report.round(3).to_string())

    print("\nCalibration sizes and attainable probabilities:")
    print(
        table_outputs["T_conformal_calibration_sizes_pilot.csv"]
        .round(4)
        .to_string(index=False)
    )

    print("\nSaved detailed results:")
    print(f"  {CONFORMAL_ROOT.resolve()}")

    print("\nCELL 9 COMPLETE")
    print(
        "Send this decision report. If healthy-reference conformal calibration "
        "controls false alarms while retaining power, Cell 10 will formalize "
        "the v4 method and run a publication-scale calibration experiment."
    )

       bearing_key  n_calibration  n_h0_evaluation  minimum_p  threshold_median  threshold_q05  threshold_q95

    Saved detailed results:
      F:\Bearings Conference Work Wisal\Implementation\results_v2\MSSP_study_v1\06_statistics\conformal_pilot

    CELL 9 COMPLETE
    Send this decision report. If healthy-reference conformal calibration controls false alarms while retaining power, Cell 10 will formalize the v4 method and run a publication-scale calibration experiment.

    """
    =============================================================================
    MSSP UPGRADE
    Cell 10: v4 environment-robust conformal publication simulation
    =============================================================================

    Run after Cells 6-8. Cell 9 is recommended but not required.

    Scientific purpose
    ------------------
    Cell 9 established marginal calibration, but Paderborn colored noise retained
    an unacceptable conditional false-positive rate. This cell evaluates a v4
    family-level detector that is valid for every represented healthy nuisance
    environment at a known operating speed.

    For a test score T=max(BPFO_z, BPFI_z, BSF_z), let p_g be the split-conformal
    upper-tail probability from healthy calibration bank g. At the known bearing
    and shaft speed, the proposed probability is

        p_robust = max_g p_g,

    where g ranges over the healthy nuisance environments. If the test signal is
    exchangeable with any represented bank, p_robust >= its valid p_g; therefore
    P(p_robust <= alpha) <= alpha. The test noise label is not used. The physical
    fault localization is still argmax(BPFO_z, BPFI_z, BSF_z).

    Default publication design
    --------------------------
    * 99 independent healthy calibration trials per environment and speed;
    * 100 independent healthy test trials per environment and speed;
    * 20 independent faulty test trials per target/noise/SNR/speed cell;
    * 199 search- and collision-matched null orders per signal;
    * deterministic seeds, configuration hash, checkpoints and safe resume.

    Set CELL10_MODE="smoke" before running only for a rapid software test. Smoke
    results are not scientifically interpretable and are saved separately.
    """

    from pathlib import Path
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    import hashlib
    import json
    import os
    import time

    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    from scipy.stats import binomtest, norm


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Prerequisites and registered configuration
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    required_cell10_names = [
        "extract_features_v3",
        "simulate_bearing_record",
        "MSSP_DIRS",
        "MSSP_COLORS",
        "save_publication_figure",
    ]

    missing_cell10_names = [
        name for name in required_cell10_names
        if name not in globals()
    ]

    if missing_cell10_names:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Run Cells 6, 7 and 8 first. Missing: "
            + ", ".join(missing_cell10_names)
        )

    CELL10_MODE = str(globals().get("CELL10_MODE", "publication")).lower()

    if CELL10_MODE not in {"publication", "smoke"}:
        raise ValueError("CELL10_MODE must be 'publication' or 'smoke'")

    if CELL10_MODE == "publication":
        replication = {
            "n_calibration_healthy": 99,
            "n_test_healthy": 100,
            "n_repetitions_fault": 20,
            "n_null": 199,
            "checkpoint_every": 20,
        }
    else:
        replication = {
            "n_calibration_healthy": 3,
            "n_test_healthy": 2,
            "n_repetitions_fault": 1,
            "n_null": 19,
            "checkpoint_every": 10,
        }

    V4_CONFIG = {
        "method_version": "v4.0-environment-robust-conformal",
        "mode": CELL10_MODE,
        "seed": 20260820,
        "alpha": 0.05,
        "bearing_scenarios": {
            "CWRU_DE_6205": {
                "fs": 12000.0,
                "revolutions": 40,
                "speeds_hz": [15.0, 25.0, 30.0],
                "fault_targets": ["BPFO", "BPFI", "BSF"],
            },
            "PADERBORN_6203": {
                "fs": 12000.0,
                "revolutions": 100,
                "speeds_hz": [15.0, 25.0],
                "fault_targets": ["BPFO", "BPFI"],
            },
        },
        "healthy_environments": [
            "white",
            "colored",
            "impulsive",
            "harmonic",
        ],
        "fault_environments": ["colored", "impulsive"],
        "snr_db": [-12.0, -8.0, -4.0, 0.0],
        **replication,
    }


    def v4_json_digest(value):
        encoded = json.dumps(
            value,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        ).encode("utf-8")
        return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()


    V4_CONFIG_HASH = v4_json_digest(V4_CONFIG)
    V4_ROOT = (
        Path(MSSP_DIRS["simulation"]) /
        f"v4_{CELL10_MODE}"
    )
    V4_ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    V4_TRIAL_PATH = V4_ROOT / f"simulation_trials_v4_{CELL10_MODE}.csv"
    V4_CONFIG_PATH = V4_ROOT / f"simulation_config_v4_{CELL10_MODE}.json"
    V4_ERROR_PATH = V4_ROOT / f"simulation_errors_v4_{CELL10_MODE}.csv"

    config_record = {
        "created_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "config_sha256": V4_CONFIG_HASH,
        "config": V4_CONFIG,
    }

    if V4_CONFIG_PATH.exists():
        with open(V4_CONFIG_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            previous_record = json.load(file)

        if previous_record.get("config_sha256") != V4_CONFIG_HASH:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Configuration conflict at {V4_CONFIG_PATH}. "
                "Do not mix simulations with different registrations."
            )
    else:
        with open(V4_CONFIG_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            json.dump(config_record, file, indent=2)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Deterministic, independent calibration/test design
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def make_v4_design(config):
        rows = []
        trial_counter = 0
        master_rng = np.random.default_rng(config["seed"])

        for bearing_key, scenario in config["bearing_scenarios"].items():
            for fr in scenario["speeds_hz"]:
                for environment in config["healthy_environments"]:
                    healthy_roles = [
                        (
                            "calibration",
                            config["n_calibration_healthy"],
                        ),
                        ("test", config["n_test_healthy"]),
                    ]

                    for split_role, n_repetitions in healthy_roles:
                        for repetition in range(n_repetitions):
                            rows.append({
                                "trial_id": f"V4H0_{trial_counter:07d}",
                                "trial_seed": int(
                                    master_rng.integers(0, 2 ** 32 - 1)
                                ),
                                "bearing_key": bearing_key,
                                "fs": float(scenario["fs"]),
                                "revolutions": int(
                                    scenario["revolutions"]
                                ),
                                "hypothesis": "H0",
                                "split_role": split_role,
                                "fault_name": "N",
                                "target_order": "NONE",
                                "fr": float(fr),
                                "rpm": float(60.0 * fr),
                                "noise_profile": environment,
                                "snr_db": np.nan,
                                "repetition": repetition,
                            })
                            trial_counter += 1

            for target_order in scenario["fault_targets"]:
                for snr_db in config["snr_db"]:
                    for fr in scenario["speeds_hz"]:
                        for environment in config["fault_environments"]:
                            for repetition in range(
                                config["n_repetitions_fault"]
                            ):
                                rows.append({
                                    "trial_id": f"V4H1_{trial_counter:07d}",
                                    "trial_seed": int(
                                        master_rng.integers(
                                            0,
                                            2 ** 32 - 1,
                                        )
                                    ),
                                    "bearing_key": bearing_key,
                                    "fs": float(scenario["fs"]),
                                    "revolutions": int(
                                        scenario["revolutions"]
                                    ),
                                    "hypothesis": "H1",
                                    "split_role": "test",
                                    "fault_name": {
                                        "BPFO": "OR",
                                        "BPFI": "IR",
                                        "BSF": "B",
                                    }[target_order],
                                    "target_order": target_order,
                                    "fr": float(fr),
                                    "rpm": float(60.0 * fr),
                                    "noise_profile": environment,
                                    "snr_db": float(snr_db),
                                    "repetition": repetition,
                                })
                                trial_counter += 1

        design = pd.DataFrame(rows)
        design["config_sha256"] = V4_CONFIG_HASH
        return design


    V4_DESIGN = make_v4_design(V4_CONFIG)

    n_healthy_calibration = int(
        (
            (V4_DESIGN.hypothesis == "H0") &
            (V4_DESIGN.split_role == "calibration")
        ).sum()
    )
    n_healthy_test = int(
        (
            (V4_DESIGN.hypothesis == "H0") &
            (V4_DESIGN.split_role == "test")
        ).sum()
    )
    n_fault_test = int((V4_DESIGN.hypothesis == "H1").sum())

    runtime_rate = np.nan

    if "SIM_TRIALS" in globals() and "extract_seconds" in SIM_TRIALS:
        runtime_rate = float(
            pd.to_numeric(
                SIM_TRIALS["extract_seconds"],
                errors="coerce",
            ).median()
        )

    print("V4 environment-robust conformal simulation")
    print(f"  mode                : {CELL10_MODE}")
    print(f"  registered trials   : {len(V4_DESIGN)}")
    print(f"  healthy calibration : {n_healthy_calibration}")
    print(f"  healthy test        : {n_healthy_test}")
    print(f"  faulty test         : {n_fault_test}")
    print(f"  output              : {V4_ROOT.resolve()}")

    if np.isfinite(runtime_rate):
        print(
            "  estimated extraction: "
            f"{len(V4_DESIGN) * runtime_rate / 60.0:.1f} minutes "
            f"at {runtime_rate:.3f} seconds/trial"
        )


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Resumable feature extraction
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def v4_atomic_csv_write(dataframe, path):
        path = Path(path)
        temporary = path.with_name(path.stem + "_temporary.csv")
        dataframe.to_csv(temporary, index=False)
        os.replace(temporary, path)


    if V4_TRIAL_PATH.exists():
        completed_v4 = pd.read_csv(V4_TRIAL_PATH)

        if not completed_v4.empty:
            hashes = set(completed_v4["config_sha256"].unique())

            if hashes != {V4_CONFIG_HASH}:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "The v4 cache contains a different configuration."
                )
    else:
        completed_v4 = pd.DataFrame()

    completed_v4_ids = (
        set(completed_v4["trial_id"])
        if not completed_v4.empty
        else set()
    )

    pending_v4 = V4_DESIGN[
        ~V4_DESIGN.trial_id.isin(completed_v4_ids)
    ].copy()

    print(f"\n  cache contains      : {len(completed_v4_ids)} trials")
    print(f"  pending             : {len(pending_v4)} trials\n")

    new_v4_rows = []
    v4_error_rows = []
    v4_start_time = time.time()

    for run_index, trial in enumerate(
        pending_v4.itertuples(index=False),
        start=1,
    ):
        try:
            fault_target = (
                None if trial.hypothesis == "H0" else trial.target_order
            )
            trial_snr = (
                None if trial.hypothesis == "H0" else trial.snr_db
            )

            signal, simulation_metadata = simulate_bearing_record(
                seed=trial.trial_seed,
                bearing_key=trial.bearing_key,
                fs=trial.fs,
                revolutions=trial.revolutions,
                fr=trial.fr,
                noise_profile=trial.noise_profile,
                fault_name=fault_target,
                snr_db=trial_snr,
            )

            extraction_start = time.time()
            features = extract_features_v3(
                sig=signal,
                fs=trial.fs,
                fr=trial.fr,
                bearing_key=trial.bearing_key,
                seed_str=f"V4::{trial.trial_id}",
                n_null=V4_CONFIG["n_null"],
                alpha=V4_CONFIG["alpha"],
                return_detail=False,
            )
            extraction_seconds = time.time() - extraction_start

            result = trial._asdict()
            result.update(simulation_metadata)
            result["extract_seconds"] = float(extraction_seconds)

            scalar_feature_names = [
                "argmax_order",
                "argmax_raw_order",
                "max_z",
                "margin",
                "margin_raw",
                "min_p_emp",
                "min_p_holm",
                "n_sig_raw",
                "n_sig_holm",
                "n_sig_bh",
            ]

            for feature_name in scalar_feature_names:
                result[feature_name] = features.get(feature_name, np.nan)

            for order_name in ("BPFO", "BPFI", "BSF"):
                for suffix in (
                    "p2f",
                    "z",
                    "p_emp",
                    "p_holm",
                    "q_bh",
                    "harm",
                ):
                    key = f"{order_name}_{suffix}"
                    result[key] = features.get(key, np.nan)

            result["correct_localization"] = int(
                trial.hypothesis == "H1" and
                features["argmax_order"] == trial.target_order
            )
            result["correct_raw_localization"] = int(
                trial.hypothesis == "H1" and
                features["argmax_raw_order"] == trial.target_order
            )

            new_v4_rows.append(result)

        except Exception as error:
            v4_error_rows.append({
                "trial_id": trial.trial_id,
                "error_type": type(error).__name__,
                "error_message": str(error),
            })

        checkpoint_due = (
            run_index % V4_CONFIG["checkpoint_every"] == 0 or
            run_index == len(pending_v4)
        )

        if checkpoint_due:
            accumulated_v4 = pd.concat(
                [completed_v4, pd.DataFrame(new_v4_rows)],
                ignore_index=True,
            )

            if not accumulated_v4.empty:
                accumulated_v4 = (
                    accumulated_v4
                    .drop_duplicates("trial_id", keep="last")
                    .sort_values("trial_id")
                    .reset_index(drop=True)
                )
                v4_atomic_csv_write(accumulated_v4, V4_TRIAL_PATH)

            if v4_error_rows:
                pd.DataFrame(v4_error_rows).to_csv(
                    V4_ERROR_PATH,
                    index=False,
                )

            elapsed = time.time() - v4_start_time
            rate = run_index / max(elapsed, 1e-12)
            remaining = len(pending_v4) - run_index
            eta_seconds = remaining / max(rate, 1e-12)

            print(
                f"  {run_index:>5}/{len(pending_v4)} pending trials  "
                f"elapsed={elapsed / 60:7.2f} min  "
                f"ETA={eta_seconds / 60:7.2f} min  "
                f"errors={len(v4_error_rows)}"
            )


    if V4_TRIAL_PATH.exists():
        V4_TRIALS = pd.read_csv(V4_TRIAL_PATH)
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("No v4 simulation results were produced")

    if v4_error_rows:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{len(v4_error_rows)} trials failed. Inspect {V4_ERROR_PATH}."
        )

    expected_v4_ids = set(V4_DESIGN.trial_id)
    actual_v4_ids = set(V4_TRIALS.trial_id)

    if actual_v4_ids != expected_v4_ids:
        missing_v4_ids = sorted(expected_v4_ids - actual_v4_ids)
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"V4 simulation incomplete: {len(missing_v4_ids)} missing."
        )


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Pooled, oracle and environment-robust conformal calibration
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def v4_conformal_upper_probability(calibration_scores, test_scores):
        calibration_scores = np.asarray(calibration_scores, dtype=float)
        calibration_scores = calibration_scores[
            np.isfinite(calibration_scores)
        ]
        test_scores = np.asarray(test_scores, dtype=float)

        if calibration_scores.size == 0:
            raise ValueError("Empty v4 calibration bank")

        exceedances = (
            calibration_scores[None, :] >= test_scores[:, None]
        ).sum(axis=1)
        return (1.0 + exceedances) / (calibration_scores.size + 1.0)


    V4_CALIBRATION = V4_TRIALS[
        (V4_TRIALS.hypothesis == "H0") &
        (V4_TRIALS.split_role == "calibration")
    ].copy()

    V4_H0_TEST = V4_TRIALS[
        (V4_TRIALS.hypothesis == "H0") &
        (V4_TRIALS.split_role == "test")
    ].copy()

    V4_H1_TEST = V4_TRIALS[
        V4_TRIALS.hypothesis == "H1"
    ].copy()

    if min(len(V4_CALIBRATION), len(V4_H0_TEST), len(V4_H1_TEST)) == 0:
        raise RuntimeError("Calibration, healthy test and faulty test are required")


    def attach_v4_probabilities(test_frame, calibration_frame):
        output_frames = []

        for (bearing_key, fr), test_group in test_frame.groupby(
            ["bearing_key", "fr"],
            sort=True,
        ):
            test_group = test_group.copy()
            calibration_condition = calibration_frame[
                (calibration_frame.bearing_key == bearing_key) &
                np.isclose(calibration_frame.fr, fr)
            ].copy()

            if calibration_condition.empty:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"No calibration records for {bearing_key}, fr={fr}"
                )

            test_scores = test_group.max_z.to_numpy(dtype=float)
            pooled_scores = calibration_condition.max_z.to_numpy(dtype=float)
            test_group["p_pooled_condition"] = (
                v4_conformal_upper_probability(pooled_scores, test_scores)
            )

            bank_probabilities = {}

            for environment, bank in calibration_condition.groupby(
                "noise_profile",
                sort=True,
            ):
                bank_probabilities[environment] = (
                    v4_conformal_upper_probability(
                        bank.max_z.to_numpy(dtype=float),
                        test_scores,
                    )
                )

            probability_matrix = np.column_stack(
                list(bank_probabilities.values())
            )
            test_group["p_robust_environment"] = np.max(
                probability_matrix,
                axis=1,
            )

            oracle_probabilities = np.full(len(test_group), np.nan)
            test_environments = test_group.noise_profile.to_numpy()

            for environment, probabilities in bank_probabilities.items():
                mask = test_environments == environment
                oracle_probabilities[mask] = probabilities[mask]

            if not np.isfinite(oracle_probabilities).all():
                missing_environments = sorted(
                    set(test_environments) - set(bank_probabilities)
                )
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Test environments absent from calibration: "
                    + ", ".join(missing_environments)
                )

            test_group["p_oracle_environment"] = oracle_probabilities
            output_frames.append(test_group)

        result = pd.concat(output_frames, ignore_index=True)

        alpha = V4_CONFIG["alpha"]
        result["alarm_order_null"] = (
            result.min_p_holm <= alpha
        ).astype(int)
        result["alarm_pooled_condition"] = (
            result.p_pooled_condition <= alpha
        ).astype(int)
        result["alarm_oracle_environment"] = (
            result.p_oracle_environment <= alpha
        ).astype(int)
        result["alarm_robust_environment"] = (
            result.p_robust_environment <= alpha
        ).astype(int)
        return result


    V4_H0_RESULTS = attach_v4_probabilities(V4_H0_TEST, V4_CALIBRATION)
    V4_H1_RESULTS = attach_v4_probabilities(V4_H1_TEST, V4_CALIBRATION)

    for alarm_column in [
        "alarm_order_null",
        "alarm_pooled_condition",
        "alarm_oracle_environment",
        "alarm_robust_environment",
    ]:
        method = alarm_column.replace("alarm_", "")
        V4_H1_RESULTS[f"correct_diagnosis_{method}"] = (
            V4_H1_RESULTS[alarm_column].astype(bool) &
            (
                V4_H1_RESULTS.argmax_order ==
                V4_H1_RESULTS.target_order
            )
        ).astype(int)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Independent-test statistical summaries
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    V4_METHODS = {
        "order_null": "Order-null Holm",
        "pooled_condition": "Pooled conformal",
        "oracle_environment": "Oracle environment",
        "robust_environment": "Robust max-p conformal",
    }


    def v4_wilson_interval(successes, total, confidence=0.95):
        if total <= 0:
            return np.nan, np.nan

        z_value = norm.ppf(0.5 + confidence / 2.0)
        proportion = successes / total
        denominator = 1.0 + z_value ** 2 / total
        centre = (
            proportion + z_value ** 2 / (2.0 * total)
        ) / denominator
        half_width = (
            z_value / denominator *
            np.sqrt(
                proportion * (1.0 - proportion) / total +
                z_value ** 2 / (4.0 * total ** 2)
            )
        )
        return centre - half_width, centre + half_width


    def v4_binary_summary(
        dataframe,
        group_columns,
        indicator_column,
        metric,
        method,
    ):
        result = (
            dataframe.groupby(group_columns, dropna=False)[indicator_column]
            .agg(["sum", "count", "mean"])
            .reset_index()
            .rename(
                columns={
                    "sum": "successes",
                    "count": "n",
                    "mean": "rate",
                }
            )
        )

        intervals = [
            v4_wilson_interval(successes, total)
            for successes, total in zip(result.successes, result.n)
        ]
        result["ci_low"] = [interval[0] for interval in intervals]
        result["ci_high"] = [interval[1] for interval in intervals]
        result["metric"] = metric
        result["method"] = method
        result["method_label"] = V4_METHODS.get(method, method)
        return result


    fwer_overall_frames = []
    fwer_conditional_frames = []
    power_frames = []
    power_detailed_frames = []
    diagnosis_frames = []

    for method in V4_METHODS:
        alarm_column = f"alarm_{method}"
        diagnosis_column = f"correct_diagnosis_{method}"

        fwer_overall_frames.append(
            v4_binary_summary(
                V4_H0_RESULTS,
                ["bearing_key"],
                alarm_column,
                "Healthy family-wise false-positive rate",
                method,
            )
        )
        fwer_conditional_frames.append(
            v4_binary_summary(
                V4_H0_RESULTS,
                ["bearing_key", "noise_profile", "fr"],
                alarm_column,
                "Conditional healthy false-positive rate",
                method,
            )
        )
        power_frames.append(
            v4_binary_summary(
                V4_H1_RESULTS,
                ["bearing_key", "target_order", "snr_db"],
                alarm_column,
                "Fault-detection power",
                method,
            )
        )
        power_detailed_frames.append(
            v4_binary_summary(
                V4_H1_RESULTS,
                [
                    "bearing_key",
                    "target_order",
                    "noise_profile",
                    "fr",
                    "snr_db",
                ],
                alarm_column,
                "Conditional fault-detection power",
                method,
            )
        )
        diagnosis_frames.append(
            v4_binary_summary(
                V4_H1_RESULTS,
                ["bearing_key", "target_order", "snr_db"],
                diagnosis_column,
                "Detection with correct localization",
                method,
            )
        )

    V4_FWER_OVERALL = pd.concat(fwer_overall_frames, ignore_index=True)
    V4_FWER_CONDITIONAL = pd.concat(
        fwer_conditional_frames,
        ignore_index=True,
    )
    V4_POWER = pd.concat(power_frames, ignore_index=True)
    V4_POWER_DETAILED = pd.concat(
        power_detailed_frames,
        ignore_index=True,
    )
    V4_CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS = pd.concat(
        diagnosis_frames,
        ignore_index=True,
    )

    V4_LOCALIZATION = v4_binary_summary(
        V4_H1_RESULTS,
        ["bearing_key", "target_order", "snr_db"],
        "correct_localization",
        "Calibrated-order localization",
        "argmax_z",
    )


    def v4_holm_adjust(probabilities):
        probabilities = np.asarray(probabilities, dtype=float)
        n = len(probabilities)
        order = np.argsort(probabilities)
        sorted_probabilities = probabilities[order]
        adjusted_sorted = np.maximum.accumulate(
            (n - np.arange(n)) * sorted_probabilities
        )
        adjusted_sorted = np.minimum(adjusted_sorted, 1.0)
        adjusted = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
        adjusted[order] = adjusted_sorted
        return adjusted


    robust_conditional_mask = (
        V4_FWER_CONDITIONAL.method == "robust_environment"
    )
    robust_conditional = V4_FWER_CONDITIONAL.loc[
        robust_conditional_mask
    ].copy()

    robust_conditional["p_excess_nominal"] = [
        binomtest(
            int(successes),
            int(total),
            V4_CONFIG["alpha"],
            alternative="greater",
        ).pvalue
        for successes, total in zip(
            robust_conditional.successes,
            robust_conditional.n,
        )
    ]
    robust_conditional["p_excess_holm"] = v4_holm_adjust(
        robust_conditional.p_excess_nominal
    )

    V4_FWER_CONDITIONAL.loc[
        robust_conditional.index,
        "p_excess_nominal",
    ] = robust_conditional.p_excess_nominal
    V4_FWER_CONDITIONAL.loc[
        robust_conditional.index,
        "p_excess_holm",
    ] = robust_conditional.p_excess_holm


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Calibration-bank audit and registered decision
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    bank_rows = []

    for keys, bank in V4_CALIBRATION.groupby(
        ["bearing_key", "fr", "noise_profile"],
        sort=True,
    ):
        bearing_key, fr, environment = keys
        scores = np.sort(bank.max_z.to_numpy(dtype=float))
        n_bank = len(scores)
        allowed_exceedances = int(
            np.floor(V4_CONFIG["alpha"] * (n_bank + 1) - 1.0 + 1e-12)
        )

        if allowed_exceedances < 0:
            alpha_threshold = np.inf
        else:
            threshold_index = max(
                0,
                min(n_bank - 1, n_bank - 1 - allowed_exceedances),
            )
            alpha_threshold = float(scores[threshold_index])

        bank_rows.append({
            "bearing_key": bearing_key,
            "fr": fr,
            "rpm": 60.0 * fr,
            "noise_profile": environment,
            "n_calibration": n_bank,
            "minimum_p": 1.0 / (n_bank + 1.0),
            "score_min": float(np.min(scores)),
            "score_median": float(np.median(scores)),
            "score_q95": float(np.quantile(scores, 0.95)),
            "score_max": float(np.max(scores)),
            "alpha_threshold_order_statistic": alpha_threshold,
        })

    V4_CALIBRATION_BANKS = pd.DataFrame(bank_rows)

    robust_overall = V4_FWER_OVERALL[
        V4_FWER_OVERALL.method == "robust_environment"
    ].copy()

    robust_power_at_minus4 = V4_POWER[
        (V4_POWER.method == "robust_environment") &
        np.isclose(V4_POWER.snr_db, -4.0)
    ]
    robust_power_at_zero = V4_POWER[
        (V4_POWER.method == "robust_environment") &
        np.isclose(V4_POWER.snr_db, 0.0)
    ]

    if CELL10_MODE == "publication":
        decision_checks = {
            "overall_fwer_rate_at_most_0.075": bool(
                (robust_overall.rate <= 0.075).all()
            ),
            "no_conditional_rate_above_0.10": bool(
                (robust_conditional.rate <= 0.10).all()
            ),
            "no_holm_significant_excess_fwer": bool(
                (robust_conditional.p_excess_holm >= 0.05).all()
            ),
            "minus4db_power_at_least_0.75": bool(
                (robust_power_at_minus4.rate >= 0.75).all()
            ),
            "zero_db_power_at_least_0.90": bool(
                (robust_power_at_zero.rate >= 0.90).all()
            ),
        }
    else:
        decision_checks = {
            "smoke_execution_only": True,
        }

    V4_DECISION = pd.DataFrame([
        {"criterion": key, "passed": value}
        for key, value in decision_checks.items()
    ])

    V4_PUBLICATION_GATE_PASSED = bool(V4_DECISION.passed.all())


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Save detailed data and publication tables
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    V4_RESULT_ROOT = Path(MSSP_DIRS["statistics"]) / f"v4_{CELL10_MODE}"
    V4_RESULT_ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    result_outputs = {
        "v4_h0_results.csv": V4_H0_RESULTS,
        "v4_h1_results.csv": V4_H1_RESULTS,
        "v4_calibration_trials.csv": V4_CALIBRATION,
    }

    for filename, dataframe in result_outputs.items():
        dataframe.to_csv(V4_RESULT_ROOT / filename, index=False)

    table_outputs = {
        f"T_v4_fwer_overall_{CELL10_MODE}.csv": V4_FWER_OVERALL,
        f"T_v4_fwer_conditional_{CELL10_MODE}.csv": (
            V4_FWER_CONDITIONAL
        ),
        f"T_v4_power_{CELL10_MODE}.csv": V4_POWER,
        f"T_v4_power_detailed_{CELL10_MODE}.csv": V4_POWER_DETAILED,
        f"T_v4_correct_diagnosis_{CELL10_MODE}.csv": (
            V4_CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS
        ),
        f"T_v4_localization_{CELL10_MODE}.csv": V4_LOCALIZATION,
        f"T_v4_calibration_banks_{CELL10_MODE}.csv": (
            V4_CALIBRATION_BANKS
        ),
        f"T_v4_decision_{CELL10_MODE}.csv": V4_DECISION,
    }

    for filename, dataframe in table_outputs.items():
        dataframe.to_csv(Path(MSSP_DIRS["tables"]) / filename, index=False)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 8. Publication figures S012-S018
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    METHOD_COLORS = {
        "order_null": "#E45756",
        "pooled_condition": "#F2CF5B",
        "oracle_environment": "#59A14F",
        "robust_environment": "#4C78A8",
    }


    def v4_short_bearing_name(bearing_key):
        return (
            str(bearing_key)
            .replace("CWRU_DE_", "CWRU ")
            .replace("PADERBORN_", "PB ")
        )


    def figure_v4_overall_fwer():
        methods = list(V4_METHODS)
        bearings = sorted(V4_FWER_OVERALL.bearing_key.unique())
        x = np.arange(len(methods))
        width = 0.36
        fig, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(10.2, 4.8))

        for bearing_index, bearing_key in enumerate(bearings):
            group = (
                V4_FWER_OVERALL[
                    V4_FWER_OVERALL.bearing_key == bearing_key
                ]
                .set_index("method")
                .reindex(methods)
            )
            positions = x + (
                bearing_index - (len(bearings) - 1) / 2.0
            ) * width
            errors = np.maximum(
                0.0,
                np.vstack([
                    group.rate.to_numpy() - group.ci_low.to_numpy(),
                    group.ci_high.to_numpy() - group.rate.to_numpy(),
                ]),
            )
            axis.bar(
                positions,
                group.rate,
                width=width,
                yerr=errors,
                capsize=3,
                alpha=0.82,
                label=v4_short_bearing_name(bearing_key),
            )

        axis.axhline(
            V4_CONFIG["alpha"],
            color="black",
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=1.2,
            label="Nominal 0.05",
        )
        axis.set_xticks(x)
        axis.set_xticklabels(
            [V4_METHODS[method] for method in methods],
            rotation=12,
            ha="right",
        )
        axis.set_ylabel("Healthy family-wise false-positive rate")
        axis.set_title("Independent-test family-wise error with 95% Wilson CI")
        axis.legend(fontsize=8)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_v4_conditional_fwer():
        robust = V4_FWER_CONDITIONAL[
            V4_FWER_CONDITIONAL.method == "robust_environment"
        ].copy()
        bearings = sorted(robust.bearing_key.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(bearings),
            figsize=(11.0, 4.5),
            sharey=True,
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, bearing_key in zip(axes.ravel(), bearings):
            group = robust[robust.bearing_key == bearing_key].copy()
            group["condition"] = (
                group.noise_profile.astype(str) +
                "\n" +
                group.fr.astype(int).astype(str) +
                " Hz"
            )
            group = group.sort_values(["noise_profile", "fr"])
            errors = np.maximum(
                0.0,
                np.vstack([
                    group.rate.to_numpy() - group.ci_low.to_numpy(),
                    group.ci_high.to_numpy() - group.rate.to_numpy(),
                ]),
            )
            axis.bar(
                np.arange(len(group)),
                group.rate,
                yerr=errors,
                capsize=2.5,
                color="#4C78A8",
                alpha=0.82,
            )
            axis.axhline(
                V4_CONFIG["alpha"],
                color="black",
                linestyle="--",
                linewidth=1.1,
            )
            axis.set_xticks(np.arange(len(group)))
            axis.set_xticklabels(group.condition, rotation=55, ha="right")
            axis.set_title(v4_short_bearing_name(bearing_key))

        axes[0, 0].set_ylabel("Conditional false-positive rate")
        fig.suptitle(
            "Robust max-p conformal calibration by nuisance environment",
            y=1.01,
        )
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_v4_power_comparison():
        definitions = list(
            V4_POWER[["bearing_key", "target_order"]]
            .drop_duplicates()
            .sort_values(["bearing_key", "target_order"])
            .itertuples(index=False, name=None)
        )
        n_columns = 3
        n_rows = int(np.ceil(len(definitions) / n_columns))
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            n_rows,
            n_columns,
            figsize=(12.4, 3.8 * n_rows),
            sharex=True,
            sharey=True,
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, (bearing_key, target_order) in zip(
            axes.ravel(),
            definitions,
        ):
            subset = V4_POWER[
                (V4_POWER.bearing_key == bearing_key) &
                (V4_POWER.target_order == target_order)
            ]

            for method in V4_METHODS:
                group = subset[subset.method == method].sort_values("snr_db")
                axis.plot(
                    group.snr_db,
                    group.rate,
                    marker="o",
                    linewidth=1.6,
                    color=METHOD_COLORS[method],
                    label=V4_METHODS[method],
                )

            axis.set_title(
                f"{v4_short_bearing_name(bearing_key)}: {target_order}"
            )
            axis.set_xlabel("Fault-to-background SNR (dB)")
            axis.set_xticks(V4_CONFIG["snr_db"])
            axis.set_ylim(-0.03, 1.03)
            axis.legend(fontsize=7, loc="lower right")

        for axis in axes.ravel()[len(definitions):]:
            axis.set_visible(False)

        for row_index in range(n_rows):
            axes[row_index, 0].set_ylabel("Detection probability")

        fig.suptitle("Detection-power cost of robust calibration", y=1.01)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_v4_correct_diagnosis():
        data = V4_CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS[
            V4_CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS.method == "robust_environment"
        ].copy()
        definitions = list(
            data[["bearing_key", "target_order"]]
            .drop_duplicates()
            .sort_values(["bearing_key", "target_order"])
            .itertuples(index=False, name=None)
        )
        n_columns = 3
        n_rows = int(np.ceil(len(definitions) / n_columns))
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            n_rows,
            n_columns,
            figsize=(12.4, 3.8 * n_rows),
            sharex=True,
            sharey=True,
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, (bearing_key, target_order) in zip(
            axes.ravel(),
            definitions,
        ):
            group = data[
                (data.bearing_key == bearing_key) &
                (data.target_order == target_order)
            ].sort_values("snr_db")
            color = MSSP_COLORS[target_order]
            axis.plot(
                group.snr_db,
                group.rate,
                marker="o",
                linewidth=1.8,
                color=color,
            )
            axis.fill_between(
                group.snr_db.to_numpy(dtype=float),
                group.ci_low.to_numpy(dtype=float),
                group.ci_high.to_numpy(dtype=float),
                color=color,
                alpha=0.18,
                label="95% Wilson CI",
            )
            axis.set_title(
                f"{v4_short_bearing_name(bearing_key)}: {target_order}"
            )
            axis.set_xlabel("Fault-to-background SNR (dB)")
            axis.set_xticks(V4_CONFIG["snr_db"])
            axis.set_ylim(-0.03, 1.03)
            axis.legend(fontsize=7, loc="lower right")

        for axis in axes.ravel()[len(definitions):]:
            axis.set_visible(False)

        for row_index in range(n_rows):
            axes[row_index, 0].set_ylabel("Correct-diagnosis probability")

        fig.suptitle(
            "Environment-robust detection with correct localization",
            y=1.01,
        )
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_v4_calibration_curves():
        bearings = sorted(V4_H0_RESULTS.bearing_key.unique())
        thresholds = np.array([0.01, 0.025, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20])
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(bearings),
            figsize=(9.8, 4.2),
            sharex=True,
            sharey=True,
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, bearing_key in zip(axes.ravel(), bearings):
            group = V4_H0_RESULTS[
                V4_H0_RESULTS.bearing_key == bearing_key
            ]

            for method in [
                "pooled_condition",
                "oracle_environment",
                "robust_environment",
            ]:
                values = group[f"p_{method}"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
                observed = np.array([
                    np.mean(values <= threshold)
                    for threshold in thresholds
                ])
                axis.plot(
                    thresholds,
                    observed,
                    marker="o",
                    color=METHOD_COLORS[method],
                    label=V4_METHODS[method],
                )

            axis.plot(
                thresholds,
                thresholds,
                color="black",
                linestyle="--",
                label="Ideal upper bound",
            )
            axis.set_title(v4_short_bearing_name(bearing_key))
            axis.set_xlabel("Nominal family-wise error rate")
            axis.legend(fontsize=7)

        axes[0, 0].set_ylabel("Observed family-wise false-positive rate")
        fig.suptitle("Independent healthy-test calibration curves", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_v4_bank_thresholds():
        bearings = sorted(V4_CALIBRATION_BANKS.bearing_key.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(bearings),
            figsize=(10.8, 4.4),

            squeeze=False,

        for axis, bearing_key in zip(axes.ravel(), bearings):
            group = V4_CALIBRATION_BANKS[
                V4_CALIBRATION_BANKS.bearing_key == bearing_key
            ].copy()

            for environment, environment_group in group.groupby(
                "noise_profile",

                environment_group = environment_group.sort_values("fr")
                axis.plot(
                    environment_group.fr,
                    environment_group.alpha_threshold_order_statistic,
                    marker="o",
                    label=environment,
                )

            axis.set_title(v4_short_bearing_name(bearing_key))
            axis.set_xlabel("Shaft speed (Hz)")
            axis.set_xticks(sorted(group.fr.unique()))
            axis.legend(fontsize=8)

        axes[0, 0].set_ylabel("Healthy max-z threshold order statistic")
        fig.suptitle("Calibration-bank heterogeneity", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_v4_power_gap():
        oracle = V4_POWER[
            V4_POWER.method == "oracle_environment"
        ][
            ["bearing_key", "target_order", "snr_db", "rate"]
        ].rename(columns={"rate": "oracle_power"})
        robust = V4_POWER[
            V4_POWER.method == "robust_environment"
        ][
            ["bearing_key", "target_order", "snr_db", "rate"]
        ].rename(columns={"rate": "robust_power"})
        gap = oracle.merge(
            robust,
            on=["bearing_key", "target_order", "snr_db"],
            validate="one_to_one",
        )
        gap["power_cost"] = gap.oracle_power - gap.robust_power
        gap["label"] = (
            gap.bearing_key.map(v4_short_bearing_name) +
            " " + gap.target_order
        )
        fig, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(9.4, 4.8))

        for label, group in gap.groupby("label", sort=True):
            axis.plot(
                group.sort_values("snr_db").snr_db,
                group.sort_values("snr_db").power_cost,
                marker="o",
                label=label,
            )

        axis.axhline(0.0, color="black", linewidth=1.0)
        axis.set_xticks(V4_CONFIG["snr_db"])
        axis.set_xlabel("Fault-to-background SNR (dB)")
        axis.set_ylabel("Oracle power minus robust power")
        axis.set_title("Power cost of removing the test noise-regime label")
        axis.legend(fontsize=8, ncol=2)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    figure_jobs = [
        (figure_v4_overall_fwer(), "S012_v4_overall_fwer"),
        (figure_v4_conditional_fwer(), "S013_v4_conditional_fwer"),

        (figure_v4_calibration_curves(), "S016_v4_calibration_curves"),
        (figure_v4_bank_thresholds(), "S017_v4_bank_thresholds"),
        (figure_v4_power_gap(), "S018_v4_robust_power_cost"),
    ]

    CELL10_FIGURE_PATHS = {}

    for figure, stem in figure_jobs:
        full_stem = f"{stem}_{CELL10_MODE}"
        CELL10_FIGURE_PATHS[full_stem] = save_publication_figure(
            figure,
            full_stem,
            supplementary=True,
        )
        plt.show()
        plt.close(figure)

    robust_fwer_report = robust_overall[

        "Do not modify the registered criteria after seeing the results."
    )
