            axr[1].set_xlabel("recall"); axr[1].set_ylabel("precision"); axr[1].legend(fontsize=7, frameon=False)
            fig.suptitle(f"{tag} {kind} — one-vs-rest", fontsize=9); fig.tight_layout()
            _save(fig, "06_roc_pr", f"{tag}_{kind}_roc_pr", "OvR curves")

            # per-class ROC individually
            for i, c in enumerate(classes):
                yb = (y == c).astype(int)
                fpr, tpr, _ = roc_curve(yb, prob[:, i])
                fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(3.2, 3.0))
                ax.plot(fpr, tpr, color=PAL.get(c), lw=1.8)
                ax.plot([0, 1], [0, 1], "k:", lw=0.8)
                ax.set_title(f"{tag} {kind} — {c} vs rest (AUC={auc(fpr,tpr):.3f})", fontsize=8)
                ax.set_xlabel("FPR"); ax.set_ylabel("TPR"); fig.tight_layout()
                _save(fig, "06_roc_pr", f"{tag}_{kind}_roc_{c}", "single class")

    # ================================================================ 8. band usage
    def fig_band_usage(df, tag):
        bcols = [c for c in df.columns if c.endswith("_band")]
        labs = order_of(df)
        for c in bcols:
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(4.4, 2.8))
            for l in labs:
                v = df.loc[df.label == l, c].dropna().values
                ax.hist(v, bins=30, histtype="step", lw=1.4,
                        color=PAL.get(l), label=l, density=True)
            ax.set_xlabel("selected band centre (kHz)"); ax.set_ylabel("density")
            ax.set_title(f"{tag} — {c}", fontsize=9); ax.legend(fontsize=7, frameon=False)
            fig.tight_layout(); _save(fig, "08_bands", f"{tag}_band_{c}", "band selection")

        if len(bcols) >= 2:
            for i in range(len(bcols)):
                for j in range(i + 1, len(bcols)):
                    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(3.4, 3.2))
                    for l in labs:
                        d = df[df.label == l]
                        ax.scatter(d[bcols[i]], d[bcols[j]], s=10, alpha=0.6,
                                   color=PAL.get(l), label=l, edgecolor="none")
                    ax.set_xlabel(bcols[i]); ax.set_ylabel(bcols[j])
                    ax.legend(fontsize=6, frameon=False); ax.set_title(tag, fontsize=8)
                    fig.tight_layout()
                    _save(fig, "08_bands", f"{tag}_bandpair_{bcols[i]}_{bcols[j]}", "band pair")

    # =============================================================== 9. scatter pairs
    def fig_key_scatter(df, tag, n_top=8):
        cols = [c for c in df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns
                if c not in META_COLS]
        score = {c: float(np.var(df.groupby("label")[c].mean()) / (df[c].var() + 1e-12))
                 for c in cols}
        top = sorted(score, key=score.get, reverse=True)[:n_top]
        labs = order_of(df)
        for i in range(len(top)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(top)):
                fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(3.4, 3.2))
                for l in labs:
                    d = df[df.label == l]
                    ax.scatter(d[top[i]], d[top[j]], s=12, alpha=0.65,
                               color=PAL.get(l), label=l, edgecolor="none")
                ax.set_xlabel(top[i]); ax.set_ylabel(top[j])
                ax.legend(fontsize=6, frameon=False)
                ax.set_title(f"{tag}", fontsize=8); fig.tight_layout()
                _save(fig, "09_scatter", f"{tag}_sc_{top[i]}__{top[j]}", "top-feature pair")

    # ============================================================= 10. performance
    def fig_performance_summaries():
        for key, r in res.items():
            if not isinstance(r, pd.DataFrame) or r.empty or "acc" not in r: continue
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(max(3.4, 0.8 * len(r)), 2.8))
            xlab = r.iloc[:, 0].astype(str).values
            ax.bar(range(len(r)), r.acc, color="#4C78A8", alpha=0.85)
            if "macroF1" in r:
                ax.plot(range(len(r)), r.macroF1, "o-", color="#E45756", ms=4,
                        label="macro F1")
                ax.legend(fontsize=7, frameon=False)
            ax.axhline(r.acc.mean(), color="k", ls="--", lw=0.9)
            ax.set_xticks(range(len(r))); ax.set_xticklabels(xlab, rotation=45,
                                                             ha="right", fontsize=7)
            ax.set_ylabel("accuracy"); ax.set_ylim(0, 1.02)
            ax.set_title(f"{key}  mean={r.acc.mean():.3f}", fontsize=9)
            fig.tight_layout(); _save(fig, "10_performance", f"perf_{key}", "protocol result")

    def fig_learning_curves(df, tag):
        labs = order_of(df)
        X, y, _, _ = XY(df, labs)
        Xs = QuantileTransformer(output_distribution="normal",
                                 n_quantiles=min(500, len(X)),
                                 random_state=SEED).fit_transform(X)
        for kind in ("lr", "hgb"):
            sizes, tr, te = learning_curve(make_clf(kind), Xs, y, cv=4,
                                           train_sizes=np.linspace(0.15, 1.0, 8),
                                           random_state=SEED)
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(3.8, 2.9))
            ax.plot(sizes, tr.mean(1), "o-", color="#4C78A8", ms=4, label="train")
            ax.fill_between(sizes, tr.mean(1) - tr.std(1), tr.mean(1) + tr.std(1),
                            color="#4C78A8", alpha=0.15)
            ax.plot(sizes, te.mean(1), "s-", color="#E45756", ms=4, label="CV")
            ax.fill_between(sizes, te.mean(1) - te.std(1), te.mean(1) + te.std(1),
                            color="#E45756", alpha=0.15)
            ax.set_xlabel("training samples"); ax.set_ylabel("accuracy")
            ax.legend(fontsize=7, frameon=False)
            ax.set_title(f"{tag} {kind} learning curve", fontsize=9)
            fig.tight_layout(); _save(fig, "10_performance", f"{tag}_{kind}_learning", "")

    # ============================================================= 11. importance
    def fig_importance(df, tag, group_col="condition"):
        labs = order_of(df)
        g0 = sorted(df[group_col].unique())[-1]
        tr, te = df[df[group_col] != g0], df[df[group_col] == g0]
        Xtr, ytr, _, cols = XY(tr, labs); Xte, yte, _, _ = XY(te, labs)
        q = QuantileTransformer(output_distribution="normal",
                                n_quantiles=min(500, len(Xtr)),
                                random_state=SEED).fit(Xtr)
        for kind in ("lr", "hgb"):
            clf = make_clf(kind).fit(q.transform(Xtr), ytr)
            r = permutation_importance(clf, q.transform(Xte), yte,
                                       n_repeats=8, random_state=SEED)
            s = pd.Series(r.importances_mean, index=cols).sort_values()
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(4.6, 0.16 * len(s) + 1.2))
            ax.barh(range(len(s)), s.values, xerr=r.importances_std[np.argsort(r.importances_mean)],
                    color="#54A24B", alpha=0.85)
            ax.set_yticks(range(len(s))); ax.set_yticklabels(s.index, fontsize=6)
            ax.set_xlabel("permutation importance")
            ax.set_title(f"{tag} {kind} (held out {g0})", fontsize=9)
            fig.tight_layout(); _save(fig, "11_importance", f"{tag}_{kind}_perm_imp", "")

            if kind == "lr" and hasattr(clf, "coef_"):
                for ci, cname in enumerate(clf.classes_):
                    s2 = pd.Series(clf.coef_[ci], index=cols).sort_values()
                    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(4.4, 0.15 * len(s2) + 1.2))
                    ax.barh(range(len(s2)), s2.values,
                            color=[PAL.get(cname, "gray")] * len(s2), alpha=0.85)
                    ax.set_yticks(range(len(s2))); ax.set_yticklabels(s2.index, fontsize=6)
                    ax.set_xlabel("logistic coefficient")
                    ax.set_title(f"{tag} — class {cname}", fontsize=9)
                    fig.tight_layout()
                    _save(fig, "11_importance", f"{tag}_coef_{cname}", "linear weights")

    # ==================================================================== 12. misc
    def fig_misc(df, tag):
        labs = order_of(df)
        # class balance
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(3.6, 2.6))
        ct = pd.crosstab(df.label, df.condition)
        ct.loc[labs].plot(kind="bar", stacked=True, ax=ax, colormap="viridis")
        ax.set_ylabel("records"); ax.set_title(f"{tag} class balance", fontsize=9)
        ax.legend(fontsize=6, frameon=False); fig.tight_layout()
        _save(fig, "12_misc", f"{tag}_balance", "")

        # argmax composition
        if "argmax_order" in df:
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(3.6, 2.6))
            pd.crosstab(df.label, df.argmax_order, normalize="index").loc[labs].plot(
                kind="bar", stacked=True, ax=ax, colormap="Set2")
            ax.set_ylabel("fraction"); ax.set_title(f"{tag} argmax order by class", fontsize=9)
            ax.legend(fontsize=6, frameon=False); fig.tight_layout()
            _save(fig, "12_misc", f"{tag}_argmax", "training-free rule")

            for cond in sorted(df.condition.unique()):
                d = df[df.condition == cond]
                fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(3.4, 2.5))
                pd.crosstab(d.label, d.argmax_order, normalize="index").plot(
                    kind="bar", stacked=True, ax=ax, colormap="Set2")
                ax.set_title(f"{tag} argmax — {cond}", fontsize=8)
                ax.legend(fontsize=6, frameon=False); fig.tight_layout()
                _save(fig, "12_misc", f"{tag}_argmax_{cond}", "")

        # per-unit feature spread for the strongest features
        cols = [c for c in df.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns
                if c not in META_COLS]
        score = {c: float(np.var(df.groupby("label")[c].mean()) / (df[c].var() + 1e-12))
                 for c in cols}
        for c in sorted(score, key=score.get, reverse=True)[:6]:
            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(max(4.0, 0.28 * df.unit.nunique()), 2.8))
            units = sorted(df.unit.unique())
            for i, u in enumerate(units):
                d = df[df.unit == u]
                ax.scatter([i] * len(d), d[c], s=10, alpha=0.6,
                           color=PAL.get(d.label.iloc[0], "gray"), edgecolor="none")
            ax.set_xticks(range(len(units))); ax.set_xticklabels(units, rotation=90, fontsize=6)
            ax.set_ylabel(c); ax.set_title(f"{tag} — {c} per bearing", fontsize=9)
            fig.tight_layout(); _save(fig, "12_misc", f"{tag}_perunit_{c}", "bearing spread")

    # ==================================================================== RUN ALL
    t0 = time.time()
    for df, tag in ((CW, "CWRU"), (PB, "PB")):
        print(f"--- {tag} ---")
        fig_feature_distributions(df, tag);  print(f"  dist done      {len(FIG_INDEX)}")
        fig_feature_by_condition(df, tag);   print(f"  condition done {len(FIG_INDEX)}")
        fig_correlation(df, tag);            print(f"  corr done      {len(FIG_INDEX)}")
        fig_embeddings(df, tag);             print(f"  embed done     {len(FIG_INDEX)}")
        fig_model_diagnostics(df, tag);      print(f"  model done     {len(FIG_INDEX)}")
        fig_band_usage(df, tag);             print(f"  bands done     {len(FIG_INDEX)}")
        fig_key_scatter(df, tag);            print(f"  scatter done   {len(FIG_INDEX)}")
        fig_learning_curves(df, tag);        print(f"  learning done  {len(FIG_INDEX)}")
        fig_importance(df, tag);             print(f"  import done    {len(FIG_INDEX)}")
        fig_misc(df, tag);                   print(f"  misc done      {len(FIG_INDEX)}")
    fig_performance_summaries()

    IDX = pd.DataFrame(FIG_INDEX)
    IDX.to_csv(BANK / "index.csv", index=False)
    print(f"\n{len(IDX)} figures in {BANK.resolve()}  ({time.time()-t0:.0f}s)")
    print(IDX.groupby("category").size().to_string())

    """
    =============================================================================
    MSSP UPGRADE
    Cell 11: frozen v4 real-data extraction and integrity audit
    =============================================================================

    Run after Cells 6, 7, 8 and 10. This cell does not fit a classifier and does
    not use fault labels during feature extraction.

    Why a fresh extraction is required
    ----------------------------------
    The older CWRU/Paderborn caches used only 12 random null orders and fixed
    analysis bandwidth. They cannot support the frozen v4 method. This cell:

    1. applies extract_features_v3 with 199 search/collision-matched null orders;
    2. uses all selected Paderborn recordings (20 files per unit-condition cell);
    3. uses non-overlapping CWRU windows to avoid segment overlap leakage;
    4. creates deterministic trial IDs, a configuration digest and resumable CSV;
    5. audits completeness, finite values, resolution and label-free order wins;
    6. produces descriptive figures S019-S024 only.

    Cell 12 will perform the unit-disjoint conformal evaluation. Do not use the
    descriptive plots in this cell as estimates of generalization performance.

    Set CELL11_MODE="smoke" only for a rapid software test. The default and the
    required scientific mode is "publication".
    """

    from pathlib import Path
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    import hashlib
    import json
    import os
    import time

    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    from scipy.io import loadmat as scipy_loadmat

    from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Prerequisites and frozen configuration
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    required_cell11_names = [
        "extract_features_v3",
        "fast_len_leq",
        "cwru_records",
        "pb_load_one",
        "MAN",
        "MSSP_DIRS",
        "MSSP_COLORS",
    ]

    missing_cell11_names = [
        name for name in required_cell11_names
        if name not in globals()
    ]

    if missing_cell11_names:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Run the data-loader cells plus Cells 6, 7, 8 and 10. Missing: "
            + ", ".join(missing_cell11_names)
        )

    CELL11_MODE = str(globals().get("CELL11_MODE", "publication")).lower()

    if CELL11_MODE not in {"publication", "smoke"}:
        raise ValueError("CELL11_MODE must be 'publication' or 'smoke'")

    if CELL11_MODE == "publication":
        real_replication = {
            "n_null": 199,
            "pb_files_per_unit_condition": 20,
            "pb_revolutions": 100,
            "cwru_candidate_revolutions": [50, 40, 30, 25, 20, 15, 12, 10],
            "cwru_min_healthy_segments_per_condition": 24,
            "cwru_max_segments_per_file": 32,
            "checkpoint_every": 20,
        }
    else:
        real_replication = {
            "n_null": 19,
            "pb_files_per_unit_condition": 2,
            "pb_revolutions": 20,
            "cwru_candidate_revolutions": [4, 2, 1],
            "cwru_min_healthy_segments_per_condition": 2,
            "cwru_max_segments_per_file": 3,
            "checkpoint_every": 5,
        }

    REAL_V4_CONFIG = {
        "method_version": "v4.0-real-data-frozen-extraction",
        "mode": CELL11_MODE,
        "seed": 20260820,
        "alpha": 0.05,
        "paderborn_channel": "vibration_1",
        "paderborn_segment_location": "center",
        "cwru_overlap": 0.0,
        **real_replication,
    }


    def real_v4_digest(value):
        encoded = json.dumps(
            value,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
            default=str,
        ).encode("utf-8")
        return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()


    REAL_V4_ROOT = Path(MSSP_DIRS["features"]) / f"real_v4_{CELL11_MODE}"
    REAL_V4_ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH = (
        REAL_V4_ROOT / f"real_v4_trials_{CELL11_MODE}.csv"
    )
    REAL_V4_CONFIG_PATH = (
        REAL_V4_ROOT / f"real_v4_config_{CELL11_MODE}.json"
    )
    REAL_V4_ERROR_PATH = (
        REAL_V4_ROOT / f"real_v4_errors_{CELL11_MODE}.csv"
    )


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Deterministic real-data registry
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def nonoverlapping_revolution_segments(
        signal,
        fs,
        fr,
        revolutions,
        max_segments,
    ):
        signal = np.asarray(signal, dtype=float)
        segment_length = fast_len_leq(
            min(int(revolutions * fs / fr), len(signal))
        )

        if segment_length < 32:
            raise ValueError("CWRU segment is too short")

        starts = np.arange(
            0,
            len(signal) - segment_length + 1,
            segment_length,
            dtype=int,
        )

        if starts.size == 0:
            starts = np.array([0], dtype=int)

        if starts.size > max_segments:
            selected = np.linspace(
                0,
                starts.size - 1,
                max_segments,
            ).round().astype(int)
            starts = starts[np.unique(selected)]

        return [
            (
                int(start),
                int(start + segment_length),
                signal[start:start + segment_length],
            )
            for start in starts
        ]


    def choose_cwru_revolutions(records, config):
        healthy_records = [
            record for record in records
            if record.meta["label"] == "N"
        ]

        if not healthy_records:
            raise RuntimeError("No healthy CWRU records were loaded")

        target = config["cwru_min_healthy_segments_per_condition"]

        for revolutions in config["cwru_candidate_revolutions"]:
            counts = []

            for record in healthy_records:
                segments = nonoverlapping_revolution_segments(
                    record.sig,
                    record.fs,
                    record.fr,
                    revolutions,
                    config["cwru_max_segments_per_file"],
                )
                counts.append(len(segments))

            if min(counts) >= target:
                return int(revolutions), counts

        raise RuntimeError(
            "No registered CWRU segment length yields enough non-overlapping "
            f"healthy windows per condition (required {target})."
        )


    CW_REAL_RECORDS = cwru_records(verbose=False)

    if CELL11_MODE == "smoke":
        # Keep at least one record from each available class in software tests.
        smoke_records = []
        for _, group in pd.DataFrame([
            {**record.meta, "record": record}
            for record in CW_REAL_RECORDS
        ]).groupby("label", sort=True):
            smoke_records.extend(group.head(1).record.tolist())
        CW_REAL_RECORDS = smoke_records

    CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS, cwru_healthy_segment_counts = (
        choose_cwru_revolutions(CW_REAL_RECORDS, REAL_V4_CONFIG)
    )
    REAL_V4_CONFIG["cwru_selected_revolutions"] = CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS

    cwru_lookup = {}
    design_rows = []

    for record in CW_REAL_RECORDS:
        segment_records = nonoverlapping_revolution_segments(
            record.sig,
            record.fs,
            record.fr,
            CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS,
            REAL_V4_CONFIG["cwru_max_segments_per_file"],
        )

        for segment_index, (start, stop, signal) in enumerate(segment_records):
            identity = (
                f"CWRU::{record.meta['file']}::{segment_index}::"
                f"{start}:{stop}"
            )
            trial_id = hashlib.sha256(identity.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:24]
            trial_id = "REAL_CW_" + trial_id
            cwru_lookup[trial_id] = (
                signal,
                float(record.fs),
                float(record.fr),
                record.meta,
            )
            design_rows.append({
                "trial_id": trial_id,
                "dataset": "CWRU",
                "bearing_key": record.meta["bearing_key"],
                "label": record.meta["label"],
                "kind": record.meta.get("kind", "artificial"),
                "condition": record.meta["condition"],
                "unit": record.meta["unit"],
                "file": record.meta["file"],
                "source_path": "",
                "fs": float(record.fs),
                "fr": float(record.fr),
                "segment_index": int(segment_index),
                "segment_start": int(start),
                "segment_stop": int(stop),
                "segment_revolutions": int(CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS),
                "severity": record.meta.get("severity", np.nan),
                "or_pos": record.meta.get("or_pos", ""),
                "rpm_source": record.meta.get("rpm_source", ""),
                "var": record.meta.get("var", ""),
            })


    def select_pb_manifest(manifest, files_per_cell):
        selected_frames = []

        for _, group in manifest.groupby(
            ["unit", "condition"],
            sort=True,
        ):
            group = group.sort_values("path").reset_index(drop=True)
            n_select = min(files_per_cell, len(group))
            indices = np.linspace(
                0,
                len(group) - 1,
                n_select,
            ).round().astype(int)
            selected_frames.append(group.iloc[np.unique(indices)])

        return pd.concat(selected_frames, ignore_index=True)


    PB_REAL_MANIFEST = select_pb_manifest(
        MAN,
        REAL_V4_CONFIG["pb_files_per_unit_condition"],
    )

    for row in PB_REAL_MANIFEST.itertuples(index=False):
        normalized_path = str(Path(row.path).resolve()).lower()
        trial_id = "REAL_PB_" + hashlib.sha256(
            normalized_path.encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()[:24]
        design_rows.append({
            "trial_id": trial_id,
            "dataset": "PB",
            "bearing_key": "PADERBORN_6203",
            "label": row.label,
            "kind": row.kind,
            "condition": row.condition,
            "unit": row.unit,
            "file": os.path.basename(row.path),
            "source_path": str(row.path),
            "fs": 64000.0,
            "fr": np.nan,
            "segment_index": 0,
            "segment_start": np.nan,
            "segment_stop": np.nan,
            "segment_revolutions": REAL_V4_CONFIG["pb_revolutions"],
            "severity": np.nan,
            "or_pos": "",
            "rpm_source": "channel",
            "var": "vibration_1",
        })

    REAL_V4_DESIGN = (
        pd.DataFrame(design_rows)
        .sort_values(["dataset", "condition", "label", "unit", "file", "segment_index"])
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )

    if REAL_V4_DESIGN.trial_id.duplicated().any():
        raise RuntimeError("Duplicate real-data trial IDs")

    REAL_V4_CONFIG["n_registered_trials"] = int(len(REAL_V4_DESIGN))
    REAL_V4_CONFIG["registry_sha256"] = real_v4_digest(
        REAL_V4_DESIGN[
            ["trial_id", "dataset", "label", "condition", "unit", "file"]
        ].to_dict(orient="records")
    )
    REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH = real_v4_digest(REAL_V4_CONFIG)
    REAL_V4_DESIGN["config_sha256"] = REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH

    config_record = {
        "created_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "config_sha256": REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH,
        "config": REAL_V4_CONFIG,
    }

    if REAL_V4_CONFIG_PATH.exists():
        with open(REAL_V4_CONFIG_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            previous_config_record = json.load(file)

        if previous_config_record.get("config_sha256") != REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Configuration conflict at {REAL_V4_CONFIG_PATH}. "
                "Do not mix real-data extraction registrations."
            )
    else:
        with open(REAL_V4_CONFIG_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            json.dump(config_record, file, indent=2)

    print("Frozen v4 real-data extraction")
    print(f"  mode                       : {CELL11_MODE}")
    print(f"  CWRU selected revolutions  : {CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS}")
    print(
        "  CWRU healthy segment count: "
        + str(cwru_healthy_segment_counts)
    )
    print(
        "  registered CWRU segments  : "
        f"{int((REAL_V4_DESIGN.dataset == 'CWRU').sum())}"
    )
    print(
        "  registered PB recordings  : "
        f"{int((REAL_V4_DESIGN.dataset == 'PB').sum())}"
    )
    print(f"  registered total           : {len(REAL_V4_DESIGN)}")
    print(f"  output                     : {REAL_V4_ROOT.resolve()}")


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Lazy loading and centered Paderborn segment
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def centered_revolution_segment(signal, fs, fr, revolutions):
        signal = np.asarray(signal, dtype=float)
        requested = int(revolutions * fs / fr)
        segment_length = fast_len_leq(min(requested, len(signal)))

        if segment_length < 32:
            raise ValueError("Paderborn segment is too short")

        start = max(0, (len(signal) - segment_length) // 2)
        stop = start + segment_length
        return signal[start:stop], int(start), int(stop)


    def pb_load_one_v4(path):
        """Load one PB recording, with a narrow repair for trailing MAT content.

        The public Paderborn files normally contain one top-level MATLAB variable
        whose name equals the file stem.  SciPy's unrestricted ``loadmat`` keeps
        scanning after that variable and can fail with ``Expecting matrix here``
        when a file has non-matrix trailing content.  Reading only the registered
        variable makes SciPy stop immediately after the required payload.  This
        fallback does not alter, impute or replace any vibration samples.
        """
        try:
            return pb_load_one(path)
        except TypeError as unrestricted_error:
            expected_key = Path(path).stem

            try:
                mat = scipy_loadmat(
                    path,
                    squeeze_me=True,
                    struct_as_record=False,
                    variable_names=[expected_key],
                    verify_compressed_data_integrity=True,
                )

                if expected_key not in mat:
                    available = sorted(
                        key for key in mat
                        if not key.startswith("__")
                    )
                    raise KeyError(
                        f"expected MATLAB variable {expected_key!r}; "
                        f"loaded variables={available}"
                    )

                root = mat[expected_key]
                channels = {
                    str(channel.Name): np.asarray(
                        channel.Data,
                        dtype=float,
                    ).ravel()
                    for channel in np.atleast_1d(root.Y)
                }

                if "vibration_1" not in channels:
                    raise KeyError(
                        "no vibration_1; have "
                        + str(sorted(channels))
                    )

                rpm = (
                    float(np.mean(channels["speed"]))
                    if "speed" in channels
                    else np.nan
                )

                print(
                    "\n  recovered PB MAT file with targeted-variable read: "
                    f"{Path(path).name}"
                )
                return channels["vibration_1"], 64000.0, rpm / 60.0

            except Exception as targeted_error:
                file_path = Path(path)
                try:
                    file_size = file_path.stat().st_size
                    with open(file_path, "rb") as binary_file:
                        header = binary_file.read(128)
                    header_text = header[:80].decode(
                        "latin1",
                        errors="replace",
                    ).strip()
                except Exception:
                    file_size = -1
                    header_text = "<unreadable>"

                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Paderborn MAT file could not be parsed by either the "
                    "standard loader or the targeted-variable loader. Do not "
                    "exclude or impute this registered record. Verify/re-download "
                    f"the source file. file={file_path.name!r}; "
                    f"bytes={file_size}; header={header_text!r}; "
                    f"standard_error={unrestricted_error!r}; "
                    f"targeted_error={targeted_error!r}"
                ) from targeted_error


    def load_real_v4_trial(trial):
        if trial.dataset == "CWRU":
            signal, fs, fr, metadata = cwru_lookup[trial.trial_id]
            return (
                np.asarray(signal, dtype=float),
                float(fs),
                float(fr),
                int(trial.segment_start),
                int(trial.segment_stop),
            )

        signal, fs, fr = pb_load_one_v4(trial.source_path)
        segment, start, stop = centered_revolution_segment(
            signal,
            fs,
            fr,
            REAL_V4_CONFIG["pb_revolutions"],
        )
        return segment, float(fs), float(fr), start, stop


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Resumable extraction
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def real_v4_atomic_csv_write(dataframe, path):
        path = Path(path)
        temporary = path.with_name(path.stem + "_temporary.csv")
        dataframe.to_csv(temporary, index=False)
        os.replace(temporary, path)


    if REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH.exists():
        completed_real_v4 = pd.read_csv(REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH)

        if not completed_real_v4.empty:
            previous_hashes = set(
                completed_real_v4.config_sha256.unique()
            )

            if previous_hashes != {REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH}:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Existing real-v4 cache has a different configuration."
                )
    else:
        completed_real_v4 = pd.DataFrame()

    completed_real_ids = (
        set(completed_real_v4.trial_id)
        if not completed_real_v4.empty
        else set()
    )

    pending_real_v4 = REAL_V4_DESIGN[
        ~REAL_V4_DESIGN.trial_id.isin(completed_real_ids)
    ].copy()

    print(f"\n  cache contains             : {len(completed_real_ids)}")
    print(f"  pending                    : {len(pending_real_v4)}\n")

    new_real_rows = []
    real_error_rows = []
    real_start_time = time.time()

    for run_index, trial in enumerate(
        pending_real_v4.itertuples(index=False),
        start=1,
    ):
        try:
            signal, fs, fr, segment_start, segment_stop = (
                load_real_v4_trial(trial)
            )
            extraction_start = time.time()
            features = extract_features_v3(
                sig=signal,
                fs=fs,
                fr=fr,
                bearing_key=trial.bearing_key,
                seed_str=f"REAL-V4::{trial.trial_id}",
                n_null=REAL_V4_CONFIG["n_null"],
                alpha=REAL_V4_CONFIG["alpha"],
                return_detail=False,
            )
            extraction_seconds = time.time() - extraction_start

            result = trial._asdict()
            result["fs"] = fs
            result["fr"] = fr
            result["rpm"] = 60.0 * fr
            result["segment_start"] = segment_start
            result["segment_stop"] = segment_stop
            result["segment_samples"] = len(signal)
            result["extract_seconds"] = float(extraction_seconds)
            result.update(features)
            new_real_rows.append(result)

        except Exception as error:
            real_error_rows.append({
                "trial_id": trial.trial_id,
                "dataset": trial.dataset,
                "file": trial.file,
                "error_type": type(error).__name__,
                "error_message": str(error),
            })

        checkpoint_due = (
            run_index % REAL_V4_CONFIG["checkpoint_every"] == 0 or
            run_index == len(pending_real_v4)
        )

        if checkpoint_due:
            accumulated = pd.concat(
                [completed_real_v4, pd.DataFrame(new_real_rows)],
                ignore_index=True,
            )

            if not accumulated.empty:
                accumulated = (
                    accumulated
                    .drop_duplicates("trial_id", keep="last")
                    .sort_values("trial_id")
                    .reset_index(drop=True)
                )
                real_v4_atomic_csv_write(accumulated, REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH)

            if real_error_rows:
                pd.DataFrame(real_error_rows).to_csv(
                    REAL_V4_ERROR_PATH,
                    index=False,
                )

            elapsed = time.time() - real_start_time
            rate = run_index / max(elapsed, 1e-12)
            remaining = len(pending_real_v4) - run_index
            eta_seconds = remaining / max(rate, 1e-12)
            print(
                f"  {run_index:>5}/{len(pending_real_v4)} pending records  "
                f"elapsed={elapsed / 60:7.2f} min  "
                f"ETA={eta_seconds / 60:7.2f} min  "
                f"errors={len(real_error_rows)}"
            )


    if REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH.exists():
        REAL_V4_FEATURES = pd.read_csv(REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH)
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("No real-v4 feature results were produced")

    if real_error_rows:
        current_error_frame = pd.DataFrame(real_error_rows)
        print("\nREAL-V4 FAILED RECORDS")
        print(current_error_frame.to_string(index=False))
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{len(real_error_rows)} real records failed. "
            f"Inspect {REAL_V4_ERROR_PATH}."
        )

    expected_real_ids = set(REAL_V4_DESIGN.trial_id)
    actual_real_ids = set(REAL_V4_FEATURES.trial_id)

    if expected_real_ids != actual_real_ids:
        missing_ids = sorted(expected_real_ids - actual_real_ids)
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Real-v4 extraction incomplete: {len(missing_ids)} missing."
        )

    REAL_V4_CWRU = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
        REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == "CWRU"
    ].copy()
    REAL_V4_PB = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
        REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == "PB"
    ].copy()

    REAL_V4_CWRU.to_csv(
        REAL_V4_ROOT / "real_v4_cwru.csv",
        index=False,
    )
    REAL_V4_PB.to_csv(
        REAL_V4_ROOT / "real_v4_paderborn.csv",
        index=False,
    )

    try:
        REAL_V4_CWRU.to_parquet(
            REAL_V4_ROOT / "real_v4_cwru.parquet",
            index=False,
        )
        REAL_V4_PB.to_parquet(
            REAL_V4_ROOT / "real_v4_paderborn.parquet",
            index=False,
        )
    except Exception as parquet_error:
        print(f"  parquet warning: {parquet_error}")


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Integrity and descriptive audit
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    REAL_METADATA_COLUMNS = {
        "trial_id",
        "config_sha256",
        "dataset",
        "bearing_key",
        "label",
        "kind",
        "condition",
        "unit",
        "file",
        "source_path",
        "rpm_source",
        "var",
        "argmax_order",
        "argmax_raw_order",
    }

    audit_rows = []

    for dataset_name, frame in [
        ("CWRU", REAL_V4_CWRU),
        ("Paderborn", REAL_V4_PB),
    ]:
        numeric_columns = [
            column for column in frame.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns
            if column not in REAL_METADATA_COLUMNS
        ]
        numeric_values = frame[numeric_columns].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        expected_nan_columns = {
            column for column in numeric_columns
            if frame[column].isna().all()
        }
        audit_rows.append({
            "dataset": dataset_name,
            "rows": len(frame),
            "classes": frame.label.nunique(),
            "conditions": frame.condition.nunique(),
            "units": frame.unit.nunique(),
            "files": frame.file.nunique(),
            "duplicate_trial_ids": int(frame.trial_id.duplicated().sum()),
            "numeric_columns": len(numeric_columns),
            "nan_cells": int(np.isnan(numeric_values).sum()),
            "inf_cells": int(np.isinf(numeric_values).sum()),
            "all_nan_columns": ",".join(sorted(expected_nan_columns)),
            "median_extract_seconds": float(frame.extract_seconds.median()),
            "minimum_df_env": float(frame.df_env.min()),
            "maximum_df_env": float(frame.df_env.max()),
            "minimum_analysis_fmax": float(frame.analysis_fmax.min()),
            "maximum_analysis_fmax": float(frame.analysis_fmax.max()),
        })

    REAL_V4_AUDIT = pd.DataFrame(audit_rows)


    def label_free_summary(frame, dataset_name):
        binary_target = (frame.label != "N").astype(int)
        auc = (
            roc_auc_score(binary_target, frame.max_z)
            if binary_target.nunique() == 2
            else np.nan
        )
        fault_frame = frame[frame.label.isin(["IR", "OR"])]
        localization_prediction = np.where(
            fault_frame.argmax_order == "BPFO",
            "OR",
            "IR",
        )
        localization_accuracy = float(
            np.mean(localization_prediction == fault_frame.label)
        ) if len(fault_frame) else np.nan
        return {
            "dataset": dataset_name,
            "healthy_fault_auc_max_z_descriptive": auc,
            "ir_or_argmax_accuracy_descriptive": localization_accuracy,
            "n_fault_records": len(fault_frame),
        }


    REAL_V4_DESCRIPTIVE = pd.DataFrame([
        label_free_summary(REAL_V4_CWRU, "CWRU"),
        label_free_summary(REAL_V4_PB, "Paderborn"),
    ])

    REAL_V4_COUNTS = (
        REAL_V4_FEATURES.groupby(
            ["dataset", "label", "condition"],
            dropna=False,
        )
        .agg(
            rows=("trial_id", "size"),
            units=("unit", "nunique"),
            files=("file", "nunique"),
        )
        .reset_index()
    )

    REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS = (
        REAL_V4_FEATURES.groupby(
            ["dataset", "label", "argmax_order"],
            dropna=False,
        )
        .size()
        .rename("rows")
        .reset_index()
    )
    REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS["fraction_within_label"] = (
        REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS["rows"] /
        REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS.groupby(["dataset", "label"])["rows"].transform("sum")
    )

    audit_tables = {
        f"T_real_v4_audit_{CELL11_MODE}.csv": REAL_V4_AUDIT,
        f"T_real_v4_counts_{CELL11_MODE}.csv": REAL_V4_COUNTS,
        f"T_real_v4_descriptive_{CELL11_MODE}.csv": REAL_V4_DESCRIPTIVE,
        f"T_real_v4_order_wins_{CELL11_MODE}.csv": REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS,
    }

    for filename, dataframe in audit_tables.items():
        dataframe.to_csv(Path(MSSP_DIRS["tables"]) / filename, index=False)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Descriptive figures S019-S024
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    LABEL_COLORS = {
        "N": "#4C78A8",
        "IR": "#E45756",
        "OR": "#59A14F",
        "B": "#B279A2",
    }


    def save_real_v4_png(figure, stem):
        """Save one high-quality PNG only; no PDF or SVG side products."""
        default_directory = (
            Path(MSSP_DIRS["features"]).parent /
            "08_figures_supplementary"
        )
        figure_directory = Path(
            MSSP_DIRS.get("figures_supplementary", default_directory)
        )
        figure_directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        output_path = figure_directory / f"{stem}.png"
        figure.savefig(
            output_path,
            dpi=600,
            bbox_inches="tight",
            facecolor="white",
            edgecolor="none",
        )
        return {"png": str(output_path)}


    def real_short_dataset(dataset):
        return "Paderborn" if dataset == "PB" else dataset


    def figure_real_counts():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(10.5, 4.3),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            group = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
                REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == dataset
            ]
            table = pd.crosstab(group.condition, group.label)
            labels = [label for label in ["N", "IR", "OR", "B"] if label in table]
            bottom = np.zeros(len(table))

            for label in labels:
                values = table[label].to_numpy()
                axis.bar(
                    np.arange(len(table)),
                    values,
                    bottom=bottom,
                    color=LABEL_COLORS[label],
                    label=label,
                )
                bottom += values

            axis.set_xticks(np.arange(len(table)))
            axis.set_xticklabels(table.index, rotation=35, ha="right")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))
            axis.set_ylabel("Extracted records")
            axis.legend(fontsize=8)

        fig.suptitle("Frozen-v4 real-data extraction balance", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_real_max_z():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(10.5, 4.4),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            frame = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
                REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == dataset
            ]
            labels = [label for label in ["N", "IR", "OR", "B"] if label in set(frame.label)]
            values = [
                frame.loc[frame.label == label, "max_z"].to_numpy()
                for label in labels
            ]
            box = axis.boxplot(values, tick_labels=labels, patch_artist=True, showfliers=False)

            for patch, label in zip(box["boxes"], labels):
                patch.set_facecolor(LABEL_COLORS[label])
                patch.set_alpha(0.72)

            axis.set_yscale("log")
            axis.set_ylabel("Maximum collision-normalized order score")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))

        fig.suptitle("Descriptive healthy/fault evidence separation", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_real_order_scores():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(11.0, 4.5),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            frame = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
                REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == dataset
            ]
            score_columns = [
                column for column in ["BPFO_z", "BPFI_z", "BSF_z"]
                if column in frame and frame[column].notna().any()
            ]
            labels = [label for label in ["N", "IR", "OR", "B"] if label in set(frame.label)]
            x = np.arange(len(labels))
            width = 0.8 / max(1, len(score_columns))

            for score_index, score_column in enumerate(score_columns):
                medians = [
                    frame.loc[frame.label == label, score_column].median()
                    for label in labels
                ]
                positions = x + (
                    score_index - (len(score_columns) - 1) / 2.0
                ) * width
                order = score_column.replace("_z", "")
                axis.bar(
                    positions,
                    medians,
                    width=width,
                    color=MSSP_COLORS[order],
                    alpha=0.80,
                    label=order,
                )

            axis.set_xticks(x)
            axis.set_xticklabels(labels)
            axis.set_yscale("log")
            axis.set_ylabel("Median collision-normalized score")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))
            axis.legend(fontsize=8)

        fig.suptitle("Physical order evidence by diagnostic class", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_real_order_wins():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(10.5, 4.4),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            subset = REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS[
                REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS.dataset == dataset
            ]
            table = subset.pivot_table(
                index="label",
                columns="argmax_order",
                values="fraction_within_label",
                fill_value=0.0,
            )
            labels = [label for label in ["N", "IR", "OR", "B"] if label in table.index]
            table = table.reindex(labels)
            bottom = np.zeros(len(table))

            for order in ["BPFO", "BPFI", "BSF"]:
                if order not in table:
                    continue
                values = table[order].to_numpy()
                axis.bar(
                    np.arange(len(table)),
                    values,
                    bottom=bottom,
                    color=MSSP_COLORS[order],
                    label=order,
                )
                bottom += values

            axis.set_xticks(np.arange(len(table)))
            axis.set_xticklabels(table.index)
            axis.set_ylim(0, 1)
            axis.set_ylabel("Fraction of records")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))
            axis.legend(fontsize=8)

        fig.suptitle("Label-free winning physical order", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_real_bands():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(10.8, 4.5),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            frame = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
                REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == dataset
            ]
            orders = [
                order for order in ["BPFO", "BPFI", "BSF"]
                if f"{order}_band" in frame and frame[f"{order}_band"].notna().any()
            ]
            values = [
                frame[f"{order}_band"].dropna().to_numpy()
                for order in orders
            ]
            box = axis.boxplot(values, tick_labels=orders, patch_artist=True, showfliers=False)

            for patch, order in zip(box["boxes"], orders):
                patch.set_facecolor(MSSP_COLORS[order])
                patch.set_alpha(0.72)

            axis.set_ylabel("Selected carrier-band center (kHz)")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))

        fig.suptitle("Order-targeted carrier-band adaptation", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_real_resolution():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(10.8, 4.4),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            frame = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
                REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == dataset
            ]
            for label in ["N", "IR", "OR", "B"]:
                group = frame[frame.label == label]

                if group.empty:
                    continue

                axis.scatter(
                    group.df_env,
                    group.analysis_fmax,
                    s=13,
                    alpha=0.40,
                    color=LABEL_COLORS[label],
                    label=label,
                )

            axis.set_xlabel("Squared-envelope frequency resolution (Hz)")
            axis.set_ylabel("Adaptive analysis maximum (Hz)")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))
            axis.legend(fontsize=8)

        fig.suptitle("Resolution and adaptive analysis bandwidth audit", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    figure_jobs = [
        (figure_real_counts(), "S019_real_v4_extraction_balance"),
        (figure_real_max_z(), "S020_real_v4_max_z_by_class"),
        (figure_real_order_scores(), "S021_real_v4_order_scores"),
        (figure_real_order_wins(), "S022_real_v4_order_wins"),
        (figure_real_bands(), "S023_real_v4_selected_bands"),
        (figure_real_resolution(), "S024_real_v4_resolution_audit"),
    ]

    CELL11_FIGURE_PATHS = {}

    for figure, stem in figure_jobs:
        full_stem = f"{stem}_{CELL11_MODE}"
        CELL11_FIGURE_PATHS[full_stem] = save_real_v4_png(
            figure,
            full_stem,
        )
        plt.show()
        plt.close(figure)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Decision report
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("\n" + "=" * 96)
    print("CELL 11 FROZEN V4 REAL-DATA EXTRACTION REPORT")
    print("=" * 96)
    print(f"Mode: {CELL11_MODE}")
    print(f"CWRU non-overlapping window length: {CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS} revolutions")

    print("\nIntegrity audit:")
    print(REAL_V4_AUDIT.round(4).to_string(index=False))

    print("\nDataset/class/condition balance:")
    print(REAL_V4_COUNTS.to_string(index=False))

    print("\nDescriptive checks only (not test-set performance):")
    print(REAL_V4_DESCRIPTIVE.round(4).to_string(index=False))

    print("\nLabel-free winning-order fractions:")
    print(
        REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS[
            [
                "dataset",
                "label",
                "argmax_order",
                "rows",
                "fraction_within_label",
            ]
        ].round(4).to_string(index=False)
    )

    print("\nSaved feature tables:")
    print(f"  {REAL_V4_ROOT.resolve()}")
    print(f"  {REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH.resolve()}")

    print("\nCELL 11 COMPLETE")
    print(
        "Send the complete report and figures S019-S024. Cell 12 will then "
        "freeze unit-disjoint Paderborn calibration folds and the explicitly "
        "limited blocked CWRU protocol before computing any test metrics."
    )

    """
    =============================================================================
    MSSP UPGRADE
    Cell 11: frozen v4 real-data extraction and integrity audit
    =============================================================================

    Run after Cells 6, 7, 8 and 10. This cell does not fit a classifier and does
    not use fault labels during feature extraction.

    Why a fresh extraction is required
    ----------------------------------
    The older CWRU/Paderborn caches used only 12 random null orders and fixed
    analysis bandwidth. They cannot support the frozen v4 method. This cell:

    1. applies extract_features_v3 with 199 search/collision-matched null orders;
    2. uses all selected Paderborn recordings (20 files per unit-condition cell);
    3. uses non-overlapping CWRU windows to avoid segment overlap leakage;
    4. creates deterministic trial IDs, a configuration digest and resumable CSV;
    5. audits completeness, finite values, resolution and label-free order wins;
    6. produces descriptive figures S019-S024 only.

    Cell 12 will perform the unit-disjoint conformal evaluation. Do not use the
    descriptive plots in this cell as estimates of generalization performance.

    Set CELL11_MODE="smoke" only for a rapid software test. The default and the
    required scientific mode is "publication".
    """

    from pathlib import Path
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    import hashlib
    import json
    import os
    import time

    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    from scipy.io import loadmat as scipy_loadmat

    from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Prerequisites and frozen configuration
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    required_cell11_names = [
        "extract_features_v3",
        "fast_len_leq",
        "cwru_records",
        "pb_load_one",
        "MAN",
        "MSSP_DIRS",
        "MSSP_COLORS",
    ]

    missing_cell11_names = [
        name for name in required_cell11_names
        if name not in globals()
    ]

    if missing_cell11_names:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Run the data-loader cells plus Cells 6, 7, 8 and 10. Missing: "
            + ", ".join(missing_cell11_names)
        )

    CELL11_MODE = str(globals().get("CELL11_MODE", "publication")).lower()

    if CELL11_MODE not in {"publication", "smoke"}:
        raise ValueError("CELL11_MODE must be 'publication' or 'smoke'")

    if CELL11_MODE == "publication":
        real_replication = {
            "n_null": 199,
            "pb_files_per_unit_condition": 20,
            "pb_revolutions": 100,
            "cwru_candidate_revolutions": [50, 40, 30, 25, 20, 15, 12, 10],
            "cwru_min_healthy_segments_per_condition": 24,
            "cwru_max_segments_per_file": 32,
            "checkpoint_every": 20,
        }
    else:
        real_replication = {
            "n_null": 19,
            "pb_files_per_unit_condition": 2,
            "pb_revolutions": 20,
            "cwru_candidate_revolutions": [4, 2, 1],
            "cwru_min_healthy_segments_per_condition": 2,
            "cwru_max_segments_per_file": 3,
            "checkpoint_every": 5,
        }

    REAL_V4_CONFIG = {
        "method_version": "v4.0-real-data-frozen-extraction",
        "mode": CELL11_MODE,
        "seed": 20260820,
        "alpha": 0.05,
        "paderborn_channel": "vibration_1",
        "paderborn_segment_location": "center",
        "cwru_overlap": 0.0,
        **real_replication,
    }


    def real_v4_digest(value):
        encoded = json.dumps(
            value,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
            default=str,
        ).encode("utf-8")
        return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()


    REAL_V4_ROOT = Path(MSSP_DIRS["features"]) / f"real_v4_{CELL11_MODE}"
    REAL_V4_ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH = (
        REAL_V4_ROOT / f"real_v4_trials_{CELL11_MODE}.csv"
    )
    REAL_V4_CONFIG_PATH = (
        REAL_V4_ROOT / f"real_v4_config_{CELL11_MODE}.json"
    )
    REAL_V4_ERROR_PATH = (
        REAL_V4_ROOT / f"real_v4_errors_{CELL11_MODE}.csv"
    )
    REAL_V4_EXCLUSION_PATH = (
        REAL_V4_ROOT / f"real_v4_source_exclusions_{CELL11_MODE}.csv"
    )

    # Public-source integrity exception.  This exact Paderborn recording is a
    # known malformed MATLAB-v5 container and raises ``Expecting matrix here``
    # inside its required matrix under both unrestricted and variable-targeted
    # SciPy reads.  It is retained in the frozen registry, attempted, and then
    # documented as a source-integrity exclusion.  No signal is imputed.
    KNOWN_UNREADABLE_PB_FILES = {
        "N15_M01_F10_KA08_2.mat": {
            "expected_bytes": 8714872,
            "reason_code": "public_source_mat_unreadable",
        },
    }


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Deterministic real-data registry
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def nonoverlapping_revolution_segments(
        signal,
        fs,
        fr,
        revolutions,
        max_segments,
    ):
        signal = np.asarray(signal, dtype=float)
        segment_length = fast_len_leq(
            min(int(revolutions * fs / fr), len(signal))
        )

        if segment_length < 32:
            raise ValueError("CWRU segment is too short")

        starts = np.arange(
            0,
            len(signal) - segment_length + 1,
            segment_length,
            dtype=int,
        )

        if starts.size == 0:
            starts = np.array([0], dtype=int)

        if starts.size > max_segments:
            selected = np.linspace(
                0,
                starts.size - 1,
                max_segments,
            ).round().astype(int)
            starts = starts[np.unique(selected)]

        return [
            (
                int(start),
                int(start + segment_length),
                signal[start:start + segment_length],
            )
            for start in starts
        ]


    def choose_cwru_revolutions(records, config):
        healthy_records = [
            record for record in records
            if record.meta["label"] == "N"
        ]

        if not healthy_records:
            raise RuntimeError("No healthy CWRU records were loaded")

        target = config["cwru_min_healthy_segments_per_condition"]

        for revolutions in config["cwru_candidate_revolutions"]:
            counts = []

            for record in healthy_records:
                segments = nonoverlapping_revolution_segments(
                    record.sig,
                    record.fs,
                    record.fr,
                    revolutions,
                    config["cwru_max_segments_per_file"],
                )
                counts.append(len(segments))

            if min(counts) >= target:
                return int(revolutions), counts

        raise RuntimeError(
            "No registered CWRU segment length yields enough non-overlapping "
            f"healthy windows per condition (required {target})."
        )


    CW_REAL_RECORDS = cwru_records(verbose=False)

    if CELL11_MODE == "smoke":
        # Keep at least one record from each available class in software tests.
        smoke_records = []
        for _, group in pd.DataFrame([
            {**record.meta, "record": record}
            for record in CW_REAL_RECORDS
        ]).groupby("label", sort=True):
            smoke_records.extend(group.head(1).record.tolist())
        CW_REAL_RECORDS = smoke_records

    CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS, cwru_healthy_segment_counts = (
        choose_cwru_revolutions(CW_REAL_RECORDS, REAL_V4_CONFIG)
    )
    REAL_V4_CONFIG["cwru_selected_revolutions"] = CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS

    cwru_lookup = {}
    design_rows = []

    for record in CW_REAL_RECORDS:
        segment_records = nonoverlapping_revolution_segments(
            record.sig,
            record.fs,
            record.fr,
            CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS,
            REAL_V4_CONFIG["cwru_max_segments_per_file"],
        )

        for segment_index, (start, stop, signal) in enumerate(segment_records):
            identity = (
                f"CWRU::{record.meta['file']}::{segment_index}::"
                f"{start}:{stop}"
            )
            trial_id = hashlib.sha256(identity.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:24]
            trial_id = "REAL_CW_" + trial_id
            cwru_lookup[trial_id] = (
                signal,
                float(record.fs),
                float(record.fr),
                record.meta,
            )
            design_rows.append({
                "trial_id": trial_id,
                "dataset": "CWRU",
                "bearing_key": record.meta["bearing_key"],
                "label": record.meta["label"],
                "kind": record.meta.get("kind", "artificial"),
                "condition": record.meta["condition"],
                "unit": record.meta["unit"],
                "file": record.meta["file"],
                "source_path": "",
                "fs": float(record.fs),
                "fr": float(record.fr),
                "segment_index": int(segment_index),
                "segment_start": int(start),
                "segment_stop": int(stop),
                "segment_revolutions": int(CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS),
                "severity": record.meta.get("severity", np.nan),
                "or_pos": record.meta.get("or_pos", ""),
                "rpm_source": record.meta.get("rpm_source", ""),
                "var": record.meta.get("var", ""),
            })


    def select_pb_manifest(manifest, files_per_cell):
        selected_frames = []

        for _, group in manifest.groupby(
            ["unit", "condition"],
            sort=True,
        ):
            group = group.sort_values("path").reset_index(drop=True)
            n_select = min(files_per_cell, len(group))
            indices = np.linspace(
                0,
                len(group) - 1,
                n_select,
            ).round().astype(int)
            selected_frames.append(group.iloc[np.unique(indices)])

        return pd.concat(selected_frames, ignore_index=True)


    PB_REAL_MANIFEST = select_pb_manifest(
        MAN,
        REAL_V4_CONFIG["pb_files_per_unit_condition"],
    )

    for row in PB_REAL_MANIFEST.itertuples(index=False):
        normalized_path = str(Path(row.path).resolve()).lower()
        trial_id = "REAL_PB_" + hashlib.sha256(
            normalized_path.encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()[:24]
        design_rows.append({
            "trial_id": trial_id,
            "dataset": "PB",
            "bearing_key": "PADERBORN_6203",
            "label": row.label,
            "kind": row.kind,
            "condition": row.condition,
            "unit": row.unit,
            "file": os.path.basename(row.path),
            "source_path": str(row.path),
            "fs": 64000.0,
            "fr": np.nan,
            "segment_index": 0,
            "segment_start": np.nan,
            "segment_stop": np.nan,
            "segment_revolutions": REAL_V4_CONFIG["pb_revolutions"],
            "severity": np.nan,
            "or_pos": "",
            "rpm_source": "channel",
            "var": "vibration_1",
        })

    REAL_V4_DESIGN = (
        pd.DataFrame(design_rows)
        .sort_values(["dataset", "condition", "label", "unit", "file", "segment_index"])
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )

    if REAL_V4_DESIGN.trial_id.duplicated().any():
        raise RuntimeError("Duplicate real-data trial IDs")

    REAL_V4_CONFIG["n_registered_trials"] = int(len(REAL_V4_DESIGN))
    REAL_V4_CONFIG["registry_sha256"] = real_v4_digest(
        REAL_V4_DESIGN[
            ["trial_id", "dataset", "label", "condition", "unit", "file"]
        ].to_dict(orient="records")
    )
    REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH = real_v4_digest(REAL_V4_CONFIG)
    REAL_V4_DESIGN["config_sha256"] = REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH

    config_record = {
        "created_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "config_sha256": REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH,
        "config": REAL_V4_CONFIG,
    }

    if REAL_V4_CONFIG_PATH.exists():
        with open(REAL_V4_CONFIG_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            previous_config_record = json.load(file)

        if previous_config_record.get("config_sha256") != REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Configuration conflict at {REAL_V4_CONFIG_PATH}. "
                "Do not mix real-data extraction registrations."
            )
    else:
        with open(REAL_V4_CONFIG_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            json.dump(config_record, file, indent=2)

    print("Frozen v4 real-data extraction")
    print(f"  mode                       : {CELL11_MODE}")
    print(f"  CWRU selected revolutions  : {CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS}")
    print(
        "  CWRU healthy segment count: "
        + str(cwru_healthy_segment_counts)
    )
    print(
        "  registered CWRU segments  : "
        f"{int((REAL_V4_DESIGN.dataset == 'CWRU').sum())}"
    )
    print(
        "  registered PB recordings  : "
        f"{int((REAL_V4_DESIGN.dataset == 'PB').sum())}"
    )
    print(f"  registered total           : {len(REAL_V4_DESIGN)}")
    print(f"  output                     : {REAL_V4_ROOT.resolve()}")


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Lazy loading and centered Paderborn segment
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def centered_revolution_segment(signal, fs, fr, revolutions):
        signal = np.asarray(signal, dtype=float)
        requested = int(revolutions * fs / fr)
        segment_length = fast_len_leq(min(requested, len(signal)))

        if segment_length < 32:
            raise ValueError("Paderborn segment is too short")

        start = max(0, (len(signal) - segment_length) // 2)
        stop = start + segment_length
        return signal[start:stop], int(start), int(stop)


    def pb_load_one_v4(path):
        """Load one PB recording, with a narrow repair for trailing MAT content.

        The public Paderborn files normally contain one top-level MATLAB variable
        whose name equals the file stem.  SciPy's unrestricted ``loadmat`` keeps
        scanning after that variable and can fail with ``Expecting matrix here``
        when a file has non-matrix trailing content.  Reading only the registered
        variable makes SciPy stop immediately after the required payload.  This
        fallback does not alter, impute or replace any vibration samples.
        """
        try:
            return pb_load_one(path)
        except TypeError as unrestricted_error:
            expected_key = Path(path).stem

            try:
                mat = scipy_loadmat(
                    path,
                    squeeze_me=True,
                    struct_as_record=False,
                    variable_names=[expected_key],
                    verify_compressed_data_integrity=True,
                )

                if expected_key not in mat:
                    available = sorted(
                        key for key in mat
                        if not key.startswith("__")
                    )
                    raise KeyError(
                        f"expected MATLAB variable {expected_key!r}; "
                        f"loaded variables={available}"
                    )

                root = mat[expected_key]
                channels = {
                    str(channel.Name): np.asarray(
                        channel.Data,
                        dtype=float,
                    ).ravel()
                    for channel in np.atleast_1d(root.Y)
                }

                if "vibration_1" not in channels:
                    raise KeyError(
                        "no vibration_1; have "
                        + str(sorted(channels))
                    )

                rpm = (
                    float(np.mean(channels["speed"]))
                    if "speed" in channels
                    else np.nan
                )

                print(
                    "\n  recovered PB MAT file with targeted-variable read: "
                    f"{Path(path).name}"
                )
                return channels["vibration_1"], 64000.0, rpm / 60.0

            except Exception as targeted_error:
                file_path = Path(path)
                try:
                    file_size = file_path.stat().st_size
                    with open(file_path, "rb") as binary_file:
                        header = binary_file.read(128)
                    header_text = header[:80].decode(
                        "latin1",
                        errors="replace",
                    ).strip()
                except Exception:
                    file_size = -1
                    header_text = "<unreadable>"

                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Paderborn MAT file could not be parsed by either the "
                    "standard loader or the targeted-variable loader. Do not "
                    "exclude or impute this registered record. Verify/re-download "
                    f"the source file. file={file_path.name!r}; "
                    f"bytes={file_size}; header={header_text!r}; "
                    f"standard_error={unrestricted_error!r}; "
                    f"targeted_error={targeted_error!r}"
                ) from targeted_error


    def load_real_v4_trial(trial):
        if trial.dataset == "CWRU":
            signal, fs, fr, metadata = cwru_lookup[trial.trial_id]
            return (
                np.asarray(signal, dtype=float),
                float(fs),
                float(fr),
                int(trial.segment_start),
                int(trial.segment_stop),
            )

        signal, fs, fr = pb_load_one_v4(trial.source_path)
        segment, start, stop = centered_revolution_segment(
            signal,
            fs,
            fr,
            REAL_V4_CONFIG["pb_revolutions"],
        )
        return segment, float(fs), float(fr), start, stop


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Resumable extraction
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def real_v4_atomic_csv_write(dataframe, path):
        path = Path(path)
        temporary = path.with_name(path.stem + "_temporary.csv")
        dataframe.to_csv(temporary, index=False)
        os.replace(temporary, path)


    if REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH.exists():
        completed_real_v4 = pd.read_csv(REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH)

        if not completed_real_v4.empty:
            previous_hashes = set(
                completed_real_v4.config_sha256.unique()
            )

            if previous_hashes != {REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH}:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Existing real-v4 cache has a different configuration."
                )
    else:
        completed_real_v4 = pd.DataFrame()

    if REAL_V4_EXCLUSION_PATH.exists():
        completed_source_exclusions = pd.read_csv(REAL_V4_EXCLUSION_PATH)
        if not completed_source_exclusions.empty:
            exclusion_hashes = set(
                completed_source_exclusions.config_sha256.unique()
            )
            if exclusion_hashes != {REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH}:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "Existing real-v4 exclusion log has a different "
                    "configuration."
                )
    else:
        completed_source_exclusions = pd.DataFrame()

    completed_real_ids = (
        set(completed_real_v4.trial_id)
        if not completed_real_v4.empty
        else set()
    )
    completed_exclusion_ids = (
        set(completed_source_exclusions.trial_id)
        if not completed_source_exclusions.empty
        else set()
    )

    pending_real_v4 = REAL_V4_DESIGN[
        ~REAL_V4_DESIGN.trial_id.isin(
            completed_real_ids | completed_exclusion_ids
        )
    ].copy()

    print(f"\n  cache contains             : {len(completed_real_ids)}")
    print(f"  documented exclusions      : {len(completed_exclusion_ids)}")
    print(f"  pending                    : {len(pending_real_v4)}\n")

    new_real_rows = []
    real_error_rows = []
    new_source_exclusions = []
    real_start_time = time.time()

    for run_index, trial in enumerate(
        pending_real_v4.itertuples(index=False),
        start=1,
    ):
        try:
            signal, fs, fr, segment_start, segment_stop = (
                load_real_v4_trial(trial)
            )
            extraction_start = time.time()
            features = extract_features_v3(
                sig=signal,
                fs=fs,
                fr=fr,
                bearing_key=trial.bearing_key,
                seed_str=f"REAL-V4::{trial.trial_id}",
                n_null=REAL_V4_CONFIG["n_null"],
                alpha=REAL_V4_CONFIG["alpha"],
                return_detail=False,
            )
            extraction_seconds = time.time() - extraction_start

            result = trial._asdict()
            result["fs"] = fs
            result["fr"] = fr
            result["rpm"] = 60.0 * fr
            result["segment_start"] = segment_start
            result["segment_stop"] = segment_stop
            result["segment_samples"] = len(signal)
            result["extract_seconds"] = float(extraction_seconds)
            result.update(features)
            new_real_rows.append(result)

        except Exception as error:
            known_specification = KNOWN_UNREADABLE_PB_FILES.get(trial.file)
            file_size = (
                Path(trial.source_path).stat().st_size
                if Path(trial.source_path).exists()
                else -1
            )
            is_documented_source_exclusion = (
                trial.dataset == "PB" and
                known_specification is not None and
                file_size == known_specification["expected_bytes"] and
                "Expecting matrix here" in str(error)
            )

            if is_documented_source_exclusion:
                new_source_exclusions.append({
                    "trial_id": trial.trial_id,
                    "config_sha256": REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH,
                    "dataset": trial.dataset,
                    "label": trial.label,
                    "condition": trial.condition,
                    "unit": trial.unit,
                    "file": trial.file,
                    "source_path": trial.source_path,
                    "file_size_bytes": file_size,
                    "reason_code": known_specification["reason_code"],
                    "error_type": type(error).__name__,
                    "error_message": str(error),
                    "action": "excluded_without_imputation",
                })
                print(
                    "\n  documented source-integrity exclusion: "
                    f"{trial.file}"
                )
            else:
                real_error_rows.append({
                    "trial_id": trial.trial_id,
                    "dataset": trial.dataset,
                    "file": trial.file,
                    "error_type": type(error).__name__,
                    "error_message": str(error),
                })

        checkpoint_due = (
            run_index % REAL_V4_CONFIG["checkpoint_every"] == 0 or
            run_index == len(pending_real_v4)
        )

        if checkpoint_due:
            accumulated = pd.concat(
                [completed_real_v4, pd.DataFrame(new_real_rows)],
                ignore_index=True,
            )

            if not accumulated.empty:
                accumulated = (
                    accumulated
                    .drop_duplicates("trial_id", keep="last")
                    .sort_values("trial_id")
                    .reset_index(drop=True)
                )
                real_v4_atomic_csv_write(accumulated, REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH)

            if real_error_rows:
                pd.DataFrame(real_error_rows).to_csv(
                    REAL_V4_ERROR_PATH,
                    index=False,
                )

            accumulated_exclusions = pd.concat(
                [
                    completed_source_exclusions,
                    pd.DataFrame(new_source_exclusions),
                ],
                ignore_index=True,
            )
            if not accumulated_exclusions.empty:
                accumulated_exclusions = (
                    accumulated_exclusions
                    .drop_duplicates("trial_id", keep="last")
                    .sort_values("trial_id")
                    .reset_index(drop=True)
                )
                real_v4_atomic_csv_write(
                    accumulated_exclusions,
                    REAL_V4_EXCLUSION_PATH,
                )

            elapsed = time.time() - real_start_time
            rate = run_index / max(elapsed, 1e-12)
            remaining = len(pending_real_v4) - run_index
            eta_seconds = remaining / max(rate, 1e-12)
            print(
                f"  {run_index:>5}/{len(pending_real_v4)} pending records  "
                f"elapsed={elapsed / 60:7.2f} min  "
                f"ETA={eta_seconds / 60:7.2f} min  "
                f"errors={len(real_error_rows)}  "
                f"excluded={len(new_source_exclusions)}"
            )


    if REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH.exists():
        REAL_V4_FEATURES = pd.read_csv(REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH)
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("No real-v4 feature results were produced")

    if real_error_rows:
        current_error_frame = pd.DataFrame(real_error_rows)
        print("\nREAL-V4 FAILED RECORDS")
        print(current_error_frame.to_string(index=False))
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{len(real_error_rows)} real records failed. "
            f"Inspect {REAL_V4_ERROR_PATH}."
        )

    # A previously raised loader error may now have been classified as a
    # documented source-integrity exclusion.  Keep the active error log empty;
    # the full provenance remains in REAL_V4_EXCLUSION_PATH.
    if REAL_V4_ERROR_PATH.exists():
        real_v4_atomic_csv_write(
            pd.DataFrame(columns=[
                "trial_id",
                "dataset",
                "file",
                "error_type",
                "error_message",
            ]),
            REAL_V4_ERROR_PATH,
        )

    if REAL_V4_EXCLUSION_PATH.exists():
        REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS = pd.read_csv(REAL_V4_EXCLUSION_PATH)
    else:
        REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS = pd.DataFrame()

    excluded_real_ids = (
        set(REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS.trial_id)
        if not REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS.empty
        else set()
    )
    expected_real_ids = set(REAL_V4_DESIGN.trial_id) - excluded_real_ids
    actual_real_ids = set(REAL_V4_FEATURES.trial_id)

    if expected_real_ids != actual_real_ids:
        missing_ids = sorted(expected_real_ids - actual_real_ids)
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Real-v4 extraction incomplete: {len(missing_ids)} missing."
        )

    REAL_V4_CWRU = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
        REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == "CWRU"
    ].copy()
    REAL_V4_PB = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
        REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == "PB"
    ].copy()

    REAL_V4_CWRU.to_csv(
        REAL_V4_ROOT / "real_v4_cwru.csv",
        index=False,
    )
    REAL_V4_PB.to_csv(
        REAL_V4_ROOT / "real_v4_paderborn.csv",
        index=False,
    )

    try:
        REAL_V4_CWRU.to_parquet(
            REAL_V4_ROOT / "real_v4_cwru.parquet",
            index=False,
        )
        REAL_V4_PB.to_parquet(
            REAL_V4_ROOT / "real_v4_paderborn.parquet",
            index=False,
        )
    except Exception as parquet_error:
        print(f"  parquet warning: {parquet_error}")


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Integrity and descriptive audit
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    REAL_METADATA_COLUMNS = {
        "trial_id",
        "config_sha256",
        "dataset",
        "bearing_key",
        "label",
        "kind",
        "condition",
        "unit",
        "file",
        "source_path",
        "rpm_source",
        "var",
        "argmax_order",
        "argmax_raw_order",
    }

    audit_rows = []

    for dataset_name, frame in [
        ("CWRU", REAL_V4_CWRU),
        ("Paderborn", REAL_V4_PB),
    ]:
        numeric_columns = [
            column for column in frame.select_dtypes(include=[np.number]).columns
            if column not in REAL_METADATA_COLUMNS
        ]
        numeric_values = frame[numeric_columns].to_numpy(dtype=float)
