        expected_nan_columns = {
            column for column in numeric_columns
            if frame[column].isna().all()
        }
        audit_rows.append({
            "dataset": dataset_name,
            "rows": len(frame),
            "classes": frame.label.nunique(),
            "conditions": frame.condition.nunique(),
            "units": frame.unit.nunique(),
            "files": frame.file.nunique(),
            "duplicate_trial_ids": int(frame.trial_id.duplicated().sum()),
            "numeric_columns": len(numeric_columns),
            "nan_cells": int(np.isnan(numeric_values).sum()),
            "inf_cells": int(np.isinf(numeric_values).sum()),
            "all_nan_columns": ",".join(sorted(expected_nan_columns)),
            "median_extract_seconds": float(frame.extract_seconds.median()),
            "minimum_df_env": float(frame.df_env.min()),
            "maximum_df_env": float(frame.df_env.max()),
            "minimum_analysis_fmax": float(frame.analysis_fmax.min()),
            "maximum_analysis_fmax": float(frame.analysis_fmax.max()),
        })

    REAL_V4_AUDIT = pd.DataFrame(audit_rows)
    REAL_V4_AUDIT["registered_rows"] = REAL_V4_AUDIT["dataset"].map({
        "CWRU": int((REAL_V4_DESIGN.dataset == "CWRU").sum()),
        "Paderborn": int((REAL_V4_DESIGN.dataset == "PB").sum()),
    })
    REAL_V4_AUDIT["source_integrity_exclusions"] = REAL_V4_AUDIT["dataset"].map({
        "CWRU": int(
            (REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS.dataset == "CWRU").sum()
        ) if not REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS.empty else 0,
        "Paderborn": int(
            (REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS.dataset == "PB").sum()
        ) if not REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS.empty else 0,
    })


    def label_free_summary(frame, dataset_name):
        binary_target = (frame.label != "N").astype(int)
        auc = (
            roc_auc_score(binary_target, frame.max_z)
            if binary_target.nunique() == 2
            else np.nan
        )
        fault_frame = frame[frame.label.isin(["IR", "OR"])]
        localization_prediction = np.where(
            fault_frame.argmax_order == "BPFO",
            "OR",
            "IR",
        )
        localization_accuracy = float(
            np.mean(localization_prediction == fault_frame.label)
        ) if len(fault_frame) else np.nan
        return {
            "dataset": dataset_name,
            "healthy_fault_auc_max_z_descriptive": auc,
            "ir_or_argmax_accuracy_descriptive": localization_accuracy,
            "n_fault_records": len(fault_frame),
        }


    REAL_V4_DESCRIPTIVE = pd.DataFrame([
        label_free_summary(REAL_V4_CWRU, "CWRU"),
        label_free_summary(REAL_V4_PB, "Paderborn"),
    ])

    REAL_V4_COUNTS = (
        REAL_V4_FEATURES.groupby(
            ["dataset", "label", "condition"],
            dropna=False,
        )
        .agg(
            rows=("trial_id", "size"),
            units=("unit", "nunique"),
            files=("file", "nunique"),
        )
        .reset_index()
    )

    REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS = (
        REAL_V4_FEATURES.groupby(
            ["dataset", "label", "argmax_order"],
            dropna=False,
        )
        .size()
        .rename("rows")
        .reset_index()
    )
    REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS["fraction_within_label"] = (
        REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS["rows"] /
        REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS.groupby(["dataset", "label"])["rows"].transform("sum")
    )

    audit_tables = {
        f"T_real_v4_audit_{CELL11_MODE}.csv": REAL_V4_AUDIT,
        f"T_real_v4_counts_{CELL11_MODE}.csv": REAL_V4_COUNTS,
        f"T_real_v4_descriptive_{CELL11_MODE}.csv": REAL_V4_DESCRIPTIVE,
        f"T_real_v4_order_wins_{CELL11_MODE}.csv": REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS,
    }

    if not REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS.empty:
        audit_tables[
            f"T_real_v4_source_exclusions_{CELL11_MODE}.csv"
        ] = REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS

    for filename, dataframe in audit_tables.items():
        dataframe.to_csv(Path(MSSP_DIRS["tables"]) / filename, index=False)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Descriptive figures S019-S024
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    LABEL_COLORS = {
        "N": "#4C78A8",
        "IR": "#E45756",
        "OR": "#59A14F",
        "B": "#B279A2",
    }


    def save_real_v4_png(figure, stem):
        """Save one high-quality PNG only; no PDF or SVG side products."""
        default_directory = (
            Path(MSSP_DIRS["features"]).parent /
            "08_figures_supplementary"
        )
        figure_directory = Path(
            MSSP_DIRS.get("figures_supplementary", default_directory)
        )
        figure_directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        output_path = figure_directory / f"{stem}.png"
        figure.savefig(
            output_path,
            dpi=600,
            bbox_inches="tight",
            facecolor="white",
            edgecolor="none",
        )
        return {"png": str(output_path)}


    def real_short_dataset(dataset):
        return "Paderborn" if dataset == "PB" else dataset


    def figure_real_counts():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(10.5, 4.3),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            group = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
                REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == dataset
            ]
            table = pd.crosstab(group.condition, group.label)
            labels = [label for label in ["N", "IR", "OR", "B"] if label in table]
            bottom = np.zeros(len(table))

            for label in labels:
                values = table[label].to_numpy()
                axis.bar(
                    np.arange(len(table)),
                    values,
                    bottom=bottom,
                    color=LABEL_COLORS[label],
                    label=label,
                )
                bottom += values

            axis.set_xticks(np.arange(len(table)))
            axis.set_xticklabels(table.index, rotation=35, ha="right")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))
            axis.set_ylabel("Extracted records")
            axis.legend(fontsize=8)

        fig.suptitle("Frozen-v4 real-data extraction balance", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_real_max_z():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(10.5, 4.4),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            frame = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
                REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == dataset
            ]
            labels = [label for label in ["N", "IR", "OR", "B"] if label in set(frame.label)]
            values = [
                frame.loc[frame.label == label, "max_z"].to_numpy()
                for label in labels
            ]
            box = axis.boxplot(values, tick_labels=labels, patch_artist=True, showfliers=False)

            for patch, label in zip(box["boxes"], labels):
                patch.set_facecolor(LABEL_COLORS[label])
                patch.set_alpha(0.72)

            axis.set_yscale("log")
            axis.set_ylabel("Maximum collision-normalized order score")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))

        fig.suptitle("Descriptive healthy/fault evidence separation", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_real_order_scores():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(11.0, 4.5),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            frame = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
                REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == dataset
            ]
            score_columns = [
                column for column in ["BPFO_z", "BPFI_z", "BSF_z"]
                if column in frame and frame[column].notna().any()
            ]
            labels = [label for label in ["N", "IR", "OR", "B"] if label in set(frame.label)]
            x = np.arange(len(labels))
            width = 0.8 / max(1, len(score_columns))

            for score_index, score_column in enumerate(score_columns):
                medians = [
                    frame.loc[frame.label == label, score_column].median()
                    for label in labels
                ]
                positions = x + (
                    score_index - (len(score_columns) - 1) / 2.0
                ) * width
                order = score_column.replace("_z", "")
                axis.bar(
                    positions,
                    medians,
                    width=width,
                    color=MSSP_COLORS[order],
                    alpha=0.80,
                    label=order,
                )

            axis.set_xticks(x)
            axis.set_xticklabels(labels)
            axis.set_yscale("log")
            axis.set_ylabel("Median collision-normalized score")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))
            axis.legend(fontsize=8)

        fig.suptitle("Physical order evidence by diagnostic class", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_real_order_wins():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(10.5, 4.4),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            subset = REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS[
                REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS.dataset == dataset
            ]
            table = subset.pivot_table(
                index="label",
                columns="argmax_order",
                values="fraction_within_label",
                fill_value=0.0,
            )
            labels = [label for label in ["N", "IR", "OR", "B"] if label in table.index]
            table = table.reindex(labels)
            bottom = np.zeros(len(table))

            for order in ["BPFO", "BPFI", "BSF"]:
                if order not in table:
                    continue
                values = table[order].to_numpy()
                axis.bar(
                    np.arange(len(table)),
                    values,
                    bottom=bottom,
                    color=MSSP_COLORS[order],
                    label=order,
                )
                bottom += values

            axis.set_xticks(np.arange(len(table)))
            axis.set_xticklabels(table.index)
            axis.set_ylim(0, 1)
            axis.set_ylabel("Fraction of records")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))
            axis.legend(fontsize=8)

        fig.suptitle("Label-free winning physical order", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_real_bands():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(10.8, 4.5),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            frame = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
                REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == dataset
            ]
            orders = [
                order for order in ["BPFO", "BPFI", "BSF"]
                if f"{order}_band" in frame and frame[f"{order}_band"].notna().any()
            ]
            values = [
                frame[f"{order}_band"].dropna().to_numpy()
                for order in orders
            ]
            box = axis.boxplot(values, tick_labels=orders, patch_artist=True, showfliers=False)

            for patch, order in zip(box["boxes"], orders):
                patch.set_facecolor(MSSP_COLORS[order])
                patch.set_alpha(0.72)

            axis.set_ylabel("Selected carrier-band center (kHz)")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))

        fig.suptitle("Order-targeted carrier-band adaptation", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_real_resolution():
        datasets = sorted(REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset.unique())
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(
            1,
            len(datasets),
            figsize=(10.8, 4.4),
            squeeze=False,
        )

        for axis, dataset in zip(axes.ravel(), datasets):
            frame = REAL_V4_FEATURES[
                REAL_V4_FEATURES.dataset == dataset
            ]
            for label in ["N", "IR", "OR", "B"]:
                group = frame[frame.label == label]

                if group.empty:
                    continue

                axis.scatter(
                    group.df_env,
                    group.analysis_fmax,
                    s=13,
                    alpha=0.40,
                    color=LABEL_COLORS[label],
                    label=label,
                )

            axis.set_xlabel("Squared-envelope frequency resolution (Hz)")
            axis.set_ylabel("Adaptive analysis maximum (Hz)")
            axis.set_title(real_short_dataset(dataset))
            axis.legend(fontsize=8)

        fig.suptitle("Resolution and adaptive analysis bandwidth audit", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    figure_jobs = [
        (figure_real_counts(), "S019_real_v4_extraction_balance"),
        (figure_real_max_z(), "S020_real_v4_max_z_by_class"),
        (figure_real_order_scores(), "S021_real_v4_order_scores"),
        (figure_real_order_wins(), "S022_real_v4_order_wins"),
        (figure_real_bands(), "S023_real_v4_selected_bands"),
        (figure_real_resolution(), "S024_real_v4_resolution_audit"),
    ]

    CELL11_FIGURE_PATHS = {}

    for figure, stem in figure_jobs:
        full_stem = f"{stem}_{CELL11_MODE}"
        CELL11_FIGURE_PATHS[full_stem] = save_real_v4_png(
            figure,
            full_stem,
        )
        plt.show()
        plt.close(figure)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Decision report
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("\n" + "=" * 96)
    print("CELL 11 FROZEN V4 REAL-DATA EXTRACTION REPORT")
    print("=" * 96)
    print(f"Mode: {CELL11_MODE}")
    print(f"CWRU non-overlapping window length: {CW_REAL_REVOLUTIONS} revolutions")

    print("\nIntegrity audit:")
    print(REAL_V4_AUDIT.round(4).to_string(index=False))

    if not REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS.empty:
        print("\nDocumented source-integrity exclusions:")
        print(
            REAL_V4_SOURCE_EXCLUSIONS[
                [
                    "dataset",
                    "condition",
                    "unit",
                    "file",
                    "reason_code",
                    "action",
                ]
            ].to_string(index=False)
        )

    print("\nDataset/class/condition balance:")
    print(REAL_V4_COUNTS.to_string(index=False))

    print("\nDescriptive checks only (not test-set performance):")
    print(REAL_V4_DESCRIPTIVE.round(4).to_string(index=False))

    print("\nLabel-free winning-order fractions:")
    print(
        REAL_V4_ORDER_WINS[
            [
                "dataset",
                "label",
                "argmax_order",
                "rows",
                "fraction_within_label",
            ]
        ].round(4).to_string(index=False)
    )

    print("\nSaved feature tables:")
    print(f"  {REAL_V4_ROOT.resolve()}")
    print(f"  {REAL_V4_TRIAL_PATH.resolve()}")

    print("\nCELL 11 COMPLETE")
    print(
        "Send the complete report and figures S019-S024. Cell 12 will then "
        "freeze unit-disjoint Paderborn calibration folds and the explicitly "
        "limited blocked CWRU protocol before computing any test metrics."
    )

    """
    =============================================================================
    MSSP UPGRADE
    Cell 12: frozen real-data conformal evaluation
    =============================================================================

    Run immediately after the publication-mode Cell 11 extraction.

    Primary real-data protocol
    --------------------------
    Paderborn is evaluated with three deterministic folds.  In every fold, two
    healthy bearing units are held out and the remaining four healthy units form
    the conformal reference banks.  Fault units are assigned once, within class,
    to a fold.  Thus every Paderborn test record is evaluated once and no physical
    unit occurs in both calibration and test within a fold.

    CWRU contains only one healthy source bearing/file per load.  Independent-unit
    calibration is impossible.  Its real-data result is therefore explicitly
    secondary: the earliest 80% of non-overlapping healthy segments form reference
    banks and the latest 20% form a blocked healthy test set.  Fault bearings are
    independent of the healthy reference, but CWRU healthy false-alarm estimates
    remain within-machine and must not be described as independent-unit results.

    For a test statistic T=max_z, the upper conformal probability in healthy bank
    g is

        p_g = (1 + number of calibration scores >= T_test) / (n_g + 1).

    The proposed environment-robust probability is the maximum across all
    speed-compatible healthy banks.  Operating speed is used; the test condition
    or nuisance-environment label is not used.  The physical order with largest z
    localizes the detected fault.

    All protocol choices and publication gates are written before metrics are
    computed.  Figures are saved as 600-dpi PNG only.
    """

    from pathlib import Path
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    import hashlib
    import json
    import os

    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    from scipy.stats import norm, binomtest


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Prerequisites and frozen protocol
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    required_cell12_names = [
        "REAL_V4_FEATURES",
        "REAL_V4_CWRU",
        "REAL_V4_PB",
        "REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH",
        "MSSP_DIRS",
    ]

    missing_cell12_names = [
        name for name in required_cell12_names
        if name not in globals()
    ]

    if missing_cell12_names:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Run publication-mode Cell 11 first. Missing: "
            + ", ".join(missing_cell12_names)
        )

    CELL12_MODE = str(globals().get("CELL12_MODE", "publication")).lower()
    if CELL12_MODE != "publication":
        raise ValueError(
            "Cell 12 is frozen for publication mode; set CELL12_MODE='publication'."
        )

    REAL12_CONFIG = {
        "method_version": "v4.1-real-evaluation-frozen",
        "mode": CELL12_MODE,
        "source_feature_config_sha256": str(REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH),
        "seed": 20260820,
        "alpha": 0.05,
        "paderborn_folds": 3,
        "paderborn_healthy_test_units_per_fold": 2,
        "paderborn_fault_assignment": "class-stratified deterministic hash round-robin",
        "paderborn_speed_bank_tolerance_hz": 1.5,
        "cwru_healthy_calibration_fraction": 0.80,
        "cwru_split_direction": "earliest calibration; latest blocked test",
        "cwru_speed_bank_tolerance_hz": 2.0,
        "cluster_bootstrap_replicates": 10000,
        "primary_dataset": "PB",
        "primary_method": "robust_environment",
        "paderborn_publication_gates": {
            "zero_calibration_test_unit_overlap": True,
            "overall_healthy_fwer_at_most": 0.075,
            "maximum_condition_healthy_fwer_at_most": 0.10,
            "minimum_class_detection_power": 0.75,
            "minimum_class_correct_diagnosis_power": 0.70,
            "minimum_class_localization_accuracy": 0.80,
        },
        "cwru_inference_scope": (
            "secondary within-machine blocked analysis; no independent-unit "
            "healthy calibration claim"
        ),
    }


    def real12_digest(value):
        encoded = json.dumps(
            value,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
            default=str,
        ).encode("utf-8")
        return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()


    REAL12_CONFIG_HASH = real12_digest(REAL12_CONFIG)
    REAL12_ROOT = (
        Path(MSSP_DIRS["statistics"]) /
        f"real_v4_evaluation_{CELL12_MODE}"
    )
    REAL12_ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    REAL12_CONFIG_PATH = REAL12_ROOT / "real_v4_evaluation_config.json"
    REAL12_REGISTRY_PATH = REAL12_ROOT / "real_v4_evaluation_registry.csv"

    config_record = {
        "created_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "config_sha256": REAL12_CONFIG_HASH,
        "config": REAL12_CONFIG,
    }

    if REAL12_CONFIG_PATH.exists():
        with open(REAL12_CONFIG_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            previous_config = json.load(file)
        if previous_config.get("config_sha256") != REAL12_CONFIG_HASH:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Cell 12 configuration conflict at {REAL12_CONFIG_PATH}. "
                "Do not mix real-data evaluation protocols."
            )
    else:
        with open(REAL12_CONFIG_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
            json.dump(config_record, file, indent=2)

    print("Frozen v4 real-data evaluation")
    print(f"  mode                : {CELL12_MODE}")
    print(f"  source feature hash : {REAL_V4_CONFIG_HASH}")
    print(f"  evaluation hash     : {REAL12_CONFIG_HASH}")
    print(f"  output              : {REAL12_ROOT.resolve()}")


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Deterministic unit and blocked registries
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def deterministic_unit_order(units, salt):
        units = [str(unit) for unit in sorted(set(units))]
        return sorted(
            units,
            key=lambda unit: hashlib.sha256(
                f"{REAL12_CONFIG['seed']}::{salt}::{unit}".encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest(),
        )


    pb = REAL_V4_PB.copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    cwru = REAL_V4_CWRU.copy().reset_index(drop=True)

    if len(pb) != 2319:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Expected 2319 analyzable Paderborn recordings, found {len(pb)}."
        )
    if len(cwru) != 533:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Expected 533 CWRU segments, found {len(cwru)}."
        )

    pb_healthy_units = deterministic_unit_order(
        pb.loc[pb.label == "N", "unit"],
        "PB-HEALTHY",
    )
    if len(pb_healthy_units) != 6:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Expected six Paderborn healthy units, found {pb_healthy_units}."
        )

    pb_healthy_test_folds = [
        list(chunk)
        for chunk in np.array_split(
            np.asarray(pb_healthy_units, dtype=object),
            REAL12_CONFIG["paderborn_folds"],
        )
    ]

    registry_rows = []
    pb_fault_fold = {}

    for label in ["IR", "OR"]:
        ordered_units = deterministic_unit_order(
            pb.loc[pb.label == label, "unit"],
            f"PB-FAULT-{label}",
        )
        for index, unit in enumerate(ordered_units):
            pb_fault_fold[(label, unit)] = (
                index % REAL12_CONFIG["paderborn_folds"]
            )

    for fold, healthy_test_units in enumerate(pb_healthy_test_folds):
        healthy_test_units = set(healthy_test_units)
        healthy_calibration_units = set(pb_healthy_units) - healthy_test_units

        for row in pb[pb.label == "N"].itertuples(index=False):
            role = (
                "healthy_test"
                if row.unit in healthy_test_units
                else "healthy_calibration"
            )
            registry_rows.append({
                "trial_id": row.trial_id,
                "dataset": "PB",
                "fold": fold,
                "role": role,
                "label": row.label,
                "condition": row.condition,
                "unit": row.unit,
                "file": row.file,
            })

        for row in pb[pb.label != "N"].itertuples(index=False):
            if pb_fault_fold[(row.label, row.unit)] == fold:
                registry_rows.append({
                    "trial_id": row.trial_id,
                    "dataset": "PB",
                    "fold": fold,
                    "role": "fault_test",
                    "label": row.label,
                    "condition": row.condition,
                    "unit": row.unit,
                    "file": row.file,
                })

    cwru_healthy_calibration_ids = set()
    cwru_healthy_test_ids = set()

    for condition, group in cwru[cwru.label == "N"].groupby(
        "condition",
        sort=True,
    ):
        group = group.sort_values(
            ["file", "segment_start", "trial_id"]
        ).reset_index(drop=True)
        n_total = len(group)
        n_calibration = int(np.floor(
            REAL12_CONFIG["cwru_healthy_calibration_fraction"] * n_total
        ))
        n_calibration = min(max(19, n_calibration), n_total - 1)

        calibration_ids = set(group.iloc[:n_calibration].trial_id)
        test_ids = set(group.iloc[n_calibration:].trial_id)
        cwru_healthy_calibration_ids |= calibration_ids
        cwru_healthy_test_ids |= test_ids

        for row in group.itertuples(index=False):
            registry_rows.append({
                "trial_id": row.trial_id,
                "dataset": "CWRU",
                "fold": 0,
                "role": (
                    "healthy_calibration"
                    if row.trial_id in calibration_ids
                    else "healthy_test"
                ),
                "label": row.label,
                "condition": row.condition,
                "unit": row.unit,
                "file": row.file,
            })

    for row in cwru[cwru.label != "N"].itertuples(index=False):
        registry_rows.append({
            "trial_id": row.trial_id,
            "dataset": "CWRU",
            "fold": 0,
            "role": "fault_test",
            "label": row.label,
            "condition": row.condition,
            "unit": row.unit,
            "file": row.file,
        })

    REAL12_REGISTRY = (
        pd.DataFrame(registry_rows)
        .sort_values(["dataset", "fold", "role", "trial_id"])
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )
    REAL12_REGISTRY["evaluation_config_sha256"] = REAL12_CONFIG_HASH
    REAL12_REGISTRY.to_csv(REAL12_REGISTRY_PATH, index=False)

    # Every PB healthy/fault test record appears exactly once.  Calibration rows
    # intentionally appear in two folds because four of six healthy units form the
    # reference set for each held-out pair.
    pb_test_registry = REAL12_REGISTRY[
        (REAL12_REGISTRY.dataset == "PB") &
        (REAL12_REGISTRY.role.isin(["healthy_test", "fault_test"]))
    ]
    if set(pb_test_registry.trial_id) != set(pb.trial_id):
        raise RuntimeError("The Paderborn test registry is incomplete")
    if pb_test_registry.trial_id.duplicated().any():
        raise RuntimeError("A Paderborn test recording occurs in multiple folds")


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Leakage and attainable-probability audit
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    leakage_rows = []

    for fold in range(REAL12_CONFIG["paderborn_folds"]):
        fold_registry = REAL12_REGISTRY[
            (REAL12_REGISTRY.dataset == "PB") &
            (REAL12_REGISTRY.fold == fold)
        ]
        calibration = fold_registry[
            fold_registry.role == "healthy_calibration"
        ]
        test = fold_registry[fold_registry.role != "healthy_calibration"]
        calibration_units = set(calibration.unit)
        test_units = set(test.unit)
        leakage_rows.append({
            "dataset": "PB",
            "fold": fold,
            "protocol": "unit_disjoint",
            "calibration_rows": len(calibration),
            "test_rows": len(test),
            "calibration_units": len(calibration_units),
            "test_units": len(test_units),
            "shared_trial_ids": len(
                set(calibration.trial_id) & set(test.trial_id)
            ),
            "shared_units": len(calibration_units & test_units),
            "independent_healthy_units": True,
            "primary_inference": True,
        })

    cwru_registry = REAL12_REGISTRY[REAL12_REGISTRY.dataset == "CWRU"]
    cwru_cal_registry = cwru_registry[
        cwru_registry.role == "healthy_calibration"
    ]
    cwru_test_registry = cwru_registry[
        cwru_registry.role != "healthy_calibration"
    ]
    leakage_rows.append({
        "dataset": "CWRU",
        "fold": 0,
        "protocol": "within_machine_blocked",
        "calibration_rows": len(cwru_cal_registry),
        "test_rows": len(cwru_test_registry),
        "calibration_units": cwru_cal_registry.unit.nunique(),
        "test_units": cwru_test_registry.unit.nunique(),
        "shared_trial_ids": len(
            set(cwru_cal_registry.trial_id) & set(cwru_test_registry.trial_id)
        ),
        "shared_units": len(
            set(cwru_cal_registry.unit) & set(cwru_test_registry.unit)
        ),
        "independent_healthy_units": False,
        "primary_inference": False,
    })

    REAL12_LEAKAGE_AUDIT = pd.DataFrame(leakage_rows)

    pb_overlap_ok = bool(
        (
            REAL12_LEAKAGE_AUDIT.loc[
                REAL12_LEAKAGE_AUDIT.dataset == "PB",
                ["shared_trial_ids", "shared_units"],
            ] == 0
        ).all().all()
    )
    if not pb_overlap_ok:
        raise RuntimeError("Paderborn unit-disjoint leakage audit failed")


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Conformal probabilities and label-free decisions
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    METHODS = {
        "order_null": "Order-null Holm",
        "pooled_condition": "Pooled conformal",
        "oracle_environment": "Oracle condition",
        "robust_environment": "Robust max-p conformal",
    }

    METHOD_COLORS = {
        "order_null": "#E45756",
        "pooled_condition": "#F2CF5B",
        "oracle_environment": "#59A14F",
        "robust_environment": "#4C78A8",
    }

    LABEL_COLORS = {
        "N": "#4C78A8",
        "IR": "#E45756",
        "OR": "#59A14F",
        "B": "#B279A2",
    }

    TARGET_ORDER = {
        "IR": "BPFI",
        "OR": "BPFO",
        "B": "BSF",
    }


    def conformal_upper_probability(calibration_scores, test_scores):
        calibration_scores = np.asarray(calibration_scores, dtype=float)
        calibration_scores = calibration_scores[np.isfinite(calibration_scores)]
        test_scores = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(test_scores, dtype=float))
        if calibration_scores.size == 0:
            raise ValueError("Empty healthy calibration bank")
        exceedances = (
            calibration_scores[None, :] >= test_scores[:, None]
        ).sum(axis=1)
        return (1.0 + exceedances) / (calibration_scores.size + 1.0)


    def attach_probabilities_for_fold(
        feature_frame,
        registry_frame,
        dataset,
        fold,
        speed_tolerance_hz,
    ):
        calibration_ids = set(
            registry_frame.loc[
                registry_frame.role == "healthy_calibration",
                "trial_id",
            ]
        )
        test_ids = set(
            registry_frame.loc[
                registry_frame.role != "healthy_calibration",
                "trial_id",
            ]
        )
        calibration = feature_frame[
            feature_frame.trial_id.isin(calibration_ids)
        ].copy()
        test = feature_frame[feature_frame.trial_id.isin(test_ids)].copy()

        if calibration.empty or test.empty:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Empty calibration or test data for {dataset}, fold={fold}"
            )

        banks = {
            condition: bank.copy()
            for condition, bank in calibration.groupby("condition", sort=True)
        }
        bank_speeds = {
            condition: float(bank.fr.median())
            for condition, bank in banks.items()
        }
        pooled_scores = calibration.max_z.to_numpy(dtype=float)
        output_rows = []

        for row in test.itertuples(index=False):
            compatible_conditions = [
                condition
                for condition, bank_speed in bank_speeds.items()
                if abs(float(row.fr) - bank_speed) <= speed_tolerance_hz
            ]
            if not compatible_conditions:
                nearest_distance = min(
                    abs(float(row.fr) - bank_speed)
                    for bank_speed in bank_speeds.values()
                )
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"No speed-compatible bank for {dataset}, fold={fold}, "
                    f"fr={row.fr:.4f}; nearest distance={nearest_distance:.4f} Hz"
                )

            score = float(row.max_z)
            p_by_bank = {
                condition: float(conformal_upper_probability(
                    banks[condition].max_z.to_numpy(dtype=float),
                    [score],
                )[0])
                for condition in compatible_conditions
            }
            if row.condition not in banks:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"Oracle bank absent for {dataset}, fold={fold}, "
                    f"condition={row.condition}"
                )

            result = row._asdict()
            result.update({
                "fold": int(fold),
                "split_role": (
                    "healthy_test" if row.label == "N" else "fault_test"
                ),
                "p_pooled_condition": float(
                    conformal_upper_probability(pooled_scores, [score])[0]
                ),
                "p_oracle_environment": float(
                    conformal_upper_probability(
                        banks[row.condition].max_z.to_numpy(dtype=float),
                        [score],
                    )[0]
                ),
                "p_robust_environment": float(max(p_by_bank.values())),
                "n_compatible_banks": len(compatible_conditions),
                "compatible_banks": "|".join(sorted(compatible_conditions)),
            })
            output_rows.append(result)

        output = pd.DataFrame(output_rows)
        alpha = REAL12_CONFIG["alpha"]
        output["alarm_order_null"] = (
            output.min_p_holm <= alpha
        ).astype(int)
        output["alarm_pooled_condition"] = (
            output.p_pooled_condition <= alpha
        ).astype(int)
        output["alarm_oracle_environment"] = (
            output.p_oracle_environment <= alpha
        ).astype(int)
        output["alarm_robust_environment"] = (
            output.p_robust_environment <= alpha
        ).astype(int)
        output["target_order"] = output.label.map(TARGET_ORDER)
        output["localization_correct"] = (
            (output.label != "N") &
            (output.argmax_order == output.target_order)
        ).astype(int)

        for method in METHODS:
            output[f"correct_diagnosis_{method}"] = (
                output[f"alarm_{method}"].astype(bool) &
                output.localization_correct.astype(bool)
            ).astype(int)

        return output


    result_frames = []

    for fold in range(REAL12_CONFIG["paderborn_folds"]):
        registry = REAL12_REGISTRY[
            (REAL12_REGISTRY.dataset == "PB") &
            (REAL12_REGISTRY.fold == fold)
        ]
        result_frames.append(
            attach_probabilities_for_fold(
                pb,
                registry,
                dataset="PB",
                fold=fold,
                speed_tolerance_hz=REAL12_CONFIG[
                    "paderborn_speed_bank_tolerance_hz"
                ],
            )
        )

    result_frames.append(
        attach_probabilities_for_fold(
            cwru,
            cwru_registry,
            dataset="CWRU",
            fold=0,
            speed_tolerance_hz=REAL12_CONFIG[
                "cwru_speed_bank_tolerance_hz"
            ],
        )
    )

    REAL12_RESULTS = (
        pd.concat(result_frames, ignore_index=True)
        .sort_values(["dataset", "fold", "label", "condition", "unit", "trial_id"])
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )

    if REAL12_RESULTS.trial_id.duplicated().any():
        duplicates = REAL12_RESULTS.loc[
            REAL12_RESULTS.trial_id.duplicated(False),
            ["trial_id", "dataset", "fold"],
        ]
        raise RuntimeError(
            "A test record was evaluated more than once:\n"
            + duplicates.to_string(index=False)
        )

    REAL12_RESULTS.to_csv(
        REAL12_ROOT / "real_v4_test_probabilities.csv",
        index=False,
    )


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Calibration-bank audit
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def conformal_alarm_threshold(calibration_scores, alpha):
        scores = np.sort(np.asarray(calibration_scores, dtype=float))
        scores = scores[np.isfinite(scores)]
        n_scores = len(scores)
        if n_scores == 0:
            return np.nan
        allowed_exceedances = int(np.floor(
            alpha * (n_scores + 1.0) - 1.0 + 1e-12
        ))
        if allowed_exceedances < 0:
            return np.inf
        index = max(0, n_scores - 1 - allowed_exceedances)
        return float(np.nextafter(scores[index], np.inf))


    bank_rows = []

    for dataset, feature_frame, folds in [
        ("PB", pb, range(REAL12_CONFIG["paderborn_folds"])),
        ("CWRU", cwru, [0]),
    ]:
        for fold in folds:
            registry = REAL12_REGISTRY[
                (REAL12_REGISTRY.dataset == dataset) &
                (REAL12_REGISTRY.fold == fold) &
                (REAL12_REGISTRY.role == "healthy_calibration")
            ]
            calibration = feature_frame[
                feature_frame.trial_id.isin(set(registry.trial_id))
            ]
            for condition, bank in calibration.groupby("condition", sort=True):
                scores = bank.max_z.to_numpy(dtype=float)
                bank_rows.append({
                    "dataset": dataset,
                    "fold": fold,
                    "condition": condition,
                    "units": bank.unit.nunique(),
                    "files": bank.file.nunique(),
                    "n_calibration": len(bank),
                    "median_fr": float(bank.fr.median()),
                    "minimum_p": 1.0 / (len(bank) + 1.0),
                    "max_z_median": float(np.median(scores)),
                    "max_z_q95": float(np.quantile(scores, 0.95)),
                    "max_z_maximum": float(np.max(scores)),
                    "alarm_threshold": conformal_alarm_threshold(
                        scores,
                        REAL12_CONFIG["alpha"],
                    ),
                })

    REAL12_BANKS = pd.DataFrame(bank_rows)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Cluster-aware statistical summaries
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def wilson_interval(successes, total, confidence=0.95):
        if total <= 0:
            return np.nan, np.nan
        z_value = norm.ppf(0.5 + confidence / 2.0)
        proportion = successes / total
        denominator = 1.0 + z_value ** 2 / total
        centre = (
            proportion + z_value ** 2 / (2.0 * total)
        ) / denominator
        half_width = (
            z_value / denominator *
            np.sqrt(
                proportion * (1.0 - proportion) / total +
                z_value ** 2 / (4.0 * total ** 2)
            )
        )
        return centre - half_width, centre + half_width


    def cluster_bootstrap_summary(
        frame,
        indicator_column,
        cluster_column="unit",
        replicates=None,
        seed_salt="",
    ):
        replicates = (
            REAL12_CONFIG["cluster_bootstrap_replicates"]
            if replicates is None
            else int(replicates)
        )
        cluster_means = (
            frame.groupby(cluster_column, sort=True)[indicator_column]
            .mean()
            .to_numpy(dtype=float)
        )
        n_clusters = len(cluster_means)
        if n_clusters < 2:
            return float(cluster_means.mean()), np.nan, np.nan, n_clusters

        seed = int(
            hashlib.sha256(
                f"{REAL12_CONFIG['seed']}::{seed_salt}".encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()[:8],
            16,
        )
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        indices = rng.integers(
            0,
            n_clusters,
            size=(replicates, n_clusters),
        )
        bootstrap_rates = cluster_means[indices].mean(axis=1)
        return (
            float(cluster_means.mean()),
            float(np.quantile(bootstrap_rates, 0.025)),
            float(np.quantile(bootstrap_rates, 0.975)),
            n_clusters,
        )


    def binary_summary(
        dataframe,
        group_columns,
        indicator_column,
        metric,
        method,
    ):
        rows = []
        grouper = group_columns[0] if len(group_columns) == 1 else group_columns
        for keys, group in dataframe.groupby(grouper, dropna=False, sort=True):
            if not isinstance(keys, tuple):
                keys = (keys,)
            successes = int(group[indicator_column].sum())
            total = int(len(group))
            ci_low, ci_high = wilson_interval(successes, total)
            salt = "::".join(map(str, (*keys, metric, method)))
            cluster_rate, cluster_low, cluster_high, n_clusters = (
                cluster_bootstrap_summary(
                    group,
                    indicator_column,
                    seed_salt=salt,
                )
            )
            row = dict(zip(group_columns, keys))
            row.update({
                "successes": successes,
                "n": total,
                "rate": successes / total,
                "ci_low": ci_low,
                "ci_high": ci_high,
                "cluster_rate": cluster_rate,
                "cluster_ci_low": cluster_low,
                "cluster_ci_high": cluster_high,
                "n_clusters": n_clusters,
                "metric": metric,
                "method": method,
                "method_label": METHODS.get(method, method),
            })
            rows.append(row)
        return pd.DataFrame(rows)


    h0 = REAL12_RESULTS[REAL12_RESULTS.label == "N"].copy()
    h1 = REAL12_RESULTS[REAL12_RESULTS.label != "N"].copy()

    fwer_overall_frames = []
    fwer_condition_frames = []
    detection_frames = []
    diagnosis_frames = []

    for method in METHODS:
        alarm = f"alarm_{method}"
        diagnosis = f"correct_diagnosis_{method}"
        fwer_overall_frames.append(binary_summary(
            h0,
            ["dataset"],
            alarm,
            "Healthy family-wise false-positive rate",
            method,
        ))
        fwer_condition_frames.append(binary_summary(
            h0,
            ["dataset", "condition"],
            alarm,
            "Condition-specific healthy false-positive rate",
            method,
        ))
        detection_frames.append(binary_summary(
            h1,
            ["dataset", "label"],
            alarm,
            "Fault-detection power",
            method,
        ))
        diagnosis_frames.append(binary_summary(
            h1,
            ["dataset", "label"],
            diagnosis,
            "Detection with correct fault localization",
            method,
        ))

    REAL12_FWER_OVERALL = pd.concat(fwer_overall_frames, ignore_index=True)
    REAL12_FWER_CONDITION = pd.concat(
        fwer_condition_frames,
        ignore_index=True,
    )
    REAL12_DETECTION = pd.concat(detection_frames, ignore_index=True)
    REAL12_CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS = pd.concat(
        diagnosis_frames,
        ignore_index=True,
    )
    REAL12_LOCALIZATION = binary_summary(
        h1,
        ["dataset", "label"],
        "localization_correct",
        "Argmax-z fault-order localization",
        "argmax_z",
    )


    def holm_adjust(probabilities):
        probabilities = np.asarray(probabilities, dtype=float)
        order = np.argsort(probabilities)
        sorted_probabilities = probabilities[order]
        adjusted_sorted = np.maximum.accumulate(
            (len(probabilities) - np.arange(len(probabilities))) *
            sorted_probabilities
        )
        adjusted_sorted = np.minimum(adjusted_sorted, 1.0)
        adjusted = np.empty(len(probabilities), dtype=float)
        adjusted[order] = adjusted_sorted
        return adjusted


    robust_condition_mask = (
        REAL12_FWER_CONDITION.method == "robust_environment"
    )
    robust_condition = REAL12_FWER_CONDITION.loc[
        robust_condition_mask
    ].copy()
    robust_condition["p_excess_nominal_record_level"] = [
        binomtest(
            int(successes),
            int(total),
            REAL12_CONFIG["alpha"],
            alternative="greater",
        ).pvalue
        for successes, total in zip(
            robust_condition.successes,
            robust_condition.n,
        )
    ]
    robust_condition["p_excess_holm_record_level"] = holm_adjust(
        robust_condition.p_excess_nominal_record_level
    )
    REAL12_FWER_CONDITION.loc[
        robust_condition.index,
        "p_excess_nominal_record_level",
    ] = robust_condition.p_excess_nominal_record_level
    REAL12_FWER_CONDITION.loc[
        robust_condition.index,
        "p_excess_holm_record_level",
    ] = robust_condition.p_excess_holm_record_level


    # Unit-level detail for clustered inspection.
    unit_rows = []
    for (dataset, unit, label, condition), group in REAL12_RESULTS.groupby(
        ["dataset", "unit", "label", "condition"],
        sort=True,
    ):
        row = {
            "dataset": dataset,
            "unit": unit,
            "label": label,
            "condition": condition,
            "n": len(group),
            "localization_rate": (
                float(group.localization_correct.mean())
                if label != "N" else np.nan
            ),
        }
        for method in METHODS:
            row[f"alarm_rate_{method}"] = float(
                group[f"alarm_{method}"].mean()
            )
            row[f"correct_diagnosis_rate_{method}"] = (
                float(group[f"correct_diagnosis_{method}"].mean())
                if label != "N" else np.nan
            )
        unit_rows.append(row)
    REAL12_UNIT_DETAIL = pd.DataFrame(unit_rows)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Registered Paderborn decision gate
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    pb_robust_fwer = REAL12_FWER_OVERALL[
        (REAL12_FWER_OVERALL.dataset == "PB") &
        (REAL12_FWER_OVERALL.method == "robust_environment")
    ].iloc[0]
    pb_robust_condition = REAL12_FWER_CONDITION[
        (REAL12_FWER_CONDITION.dataset == "PB") &
        (REAL12_FWER_CONDITION.method == "robust_environment")
    ]
    pb_robust_detection = REAL12_DETECTION[
        (REAL12_DETECTION.dataset == "PB") &
        (REAL12_DETECTION.method == "robust_environment")
    ]
    pb_robust_diagnosis = REAL12_CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS[
        (REAL12_CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS.dataset == "PB") &
        (REAL12_CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS.method == "robust_environment")
    ]
    pb_localization = REAL12_LOCALIZATION[
        REAL12_LOCALIZATION.dataset == "PB"
    ]

    gates = REAL12_CONFIG["paderborn_publication_gates"]
    REAL12_PUBLICATION_GATE = pd.DataFrame({
        "criterion": [
            "zero_calibration_test_unit_overlap",
            "overall_healthy_fwer_at_most_0.075",
            "maximum_condition_healthy_fwer_at_most_0.10",
            "minimum_class_detection_power_0.75",
            "minimum_class_correct_diagnosis_power_0.70",
            "minimum_class_localization_accuracy_0.80",
        ],
        "passed": [
            pb_overlap_ok,
            bool(
                pb_robust_fwer.cluster_rate <=
                gates["overall_healthy_fwer_at_most"]
            ),
            bool(
                pb_robust_condition.cluster_rate.max() <=
                gates["maximum_condition_healthy_fwer_at_most"]
            ),
            bool(
                pb_robust_detection.cluster_rate.min() >=
                gates["minimum_class_detection_power"]
            ),
            bool(
                pb_robust_diagnosis.cluster_rate.min() >=
                gates["minimum_class_correct_diagnosis_power"]
            ),
            bool(
                pb_localization.cluster_rate.min() >=
                gates["minimum_class_localization_accuracy"]
            ),
        ],
    })
    REAL12_GATE_PASS = bool(REAL12_PUBLICATION_GATE.passed.all())


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 8. Tables
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    table_outputs = {
        f"T_real_v4_protocol_audit_{CELL12_MODE}.csv": REAL12_LEAKAGE_AUDIT,
        f"T_real_v4_calibration_banks_{CELL12_MODE}.csv": REAL12_BANKS,
        f"T_real_v4_fwer_overall_{CELL12_MODE}.csv": REAL12_FWER_OVERALL,
        f"T_real_v4_fwer_condition_{CELL12_MODE}.csv": REAL12_FWER_CONDITION,
        f"T_real_v4_detection_{CELL12_MODE}.csv": REAL12_DETECTION,
        f"T_real_v4_correct_diagnosis_{CELL12_MODE}.csv": REAL12_CORRECT_DIAGNOSIS,
        f"T_real_v4_localization_{CELL12_MODE}.csv": REAL12_LOCALIZATION,
        f"T_real_v4_unit_detail_{CELL12_MODE}.csv": REAL12_UNIT_DETAIL,
        f"T_real_v4_publication_gate_{CELL12_MODE}.csv": REAL12_PUBLICATION_GATE,
    }

    for filename, dataframe in table_outputs.items():
        dataframe.to_csv(Path(MSSP_DIRS["tables"]) / filename, index=False)
        dataframe.to_csv(REAL12_ROOT / filename, index=False)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 9. Publication PNG figures S025-S036
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def save_real12_png(figure, stem):
        default_directory = (
            Path(MSSP_DIRS["statistics"]).parent /
            "08_figures_supplementary"
        )
        figure_directory = Path(
            MSSP_DIRS.get("figures_supplementary", default_directory)
        )
        figure_directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path = figure_directory / f"{stem}_{CELL12_MODE}.png"
        figure.savefig(
            path,
            dpi=600,
            bbox_inches="tight",
            facecolor="white",
            edgecolor="none",
        )
        return str(path)


    def clustered_error(summary):
        lower = summary.cluster_rate - summary.cluster_ci_low
        upper = summary.cluster_ci_high - summary.cluster_rate
        fallback_lower = summary.rate - summary.ci_low
        fallback_upper = summary.ci_high - summary.rate
        lower = lower.where(np.isfinite(lower), fallback_lower)
        upper = upper.where(np.isfinite(upper), fallback_upper)
        return np.vstack([lower.to_numpy(), upper.to_numpy()])


    def figure_protocol():
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(12.5, 4.8))
        panels = [
            (
                axes[0],
                "Paderborn: primary unit-disjoint protocol",
                [
                    (0.06, 0.68, 0.25, 0.20, "4 healthy units\ncalibration banks", "#9ECAE1"),
                    (0.39, 0.68, 0.25, 0.20, "2 healthy units\nindependent test", "#A1D99B"),
                    (0.72, 0.68, 0.22, 0.20, "Fault units\nassigned once", "#FC9272"),
                    (0.22, 0.25, 0.25, 0.20, "Speed-compatible\ncondition banks", "#FDD0A2"),
                    (0.57, 0.25, 0.25, 0.20, "Robust max-p\ndetect + argmax-z", "#BCBDDC"),
                ],
            ),
            (
                axes[1],
                "CWRU: secondary within-machine protocol",
                [
                    (0.06, 0.68, 0.25, 0.20, "Earliest 80%\nhealthy segments", "#9ECAE1"),
                    (0.39, 0.68, 0.25, 0.20, "Latest 20%\nblocked test", "#A1D99B"),
                    (0.72, 0.68, 0.22, 0.20, "Independent\nfault bearings", "#FC9272"),
                    (0.22, 0.25, 0.25, 0.20, "Load-specific\nhealthy banks", "#FDD0A2"),
                    (0.57, 0.25, 0.25, 0.20, "Exploratory\nrobust max-p", "#BCBDDC"),
                ],
            ),
        ]
        for axis, title, boxes in panels:
            axis.set_xlim(0, 1)
            axis.set_ylim(0, 1)
            axis.axis("off")
            axis.set_title(title, fontweight="bold")
            for x, y, width, height, text_value, color in boxes:
                rectangle = plt.Rectangle(
                    (x, y), width, height,
                    facecolor=color, edgecolor="#333333", linewidth=1.2,
                )
                axis.add_patch(rectangle)
                axis.text(
                    x + width / 2,
                    y + height / 2,
                    text_value,
                    ha="center",
                    va="center",
                    fontsize=10,
                )
            for start, stop in [
                ((0.31, 0.78), (0.39, 0.78)),
                ((0.64, 0.78), (0.72, 0.78)),
                ((0.50, 0.68), (0.39, 0.45)),
                ((0.72, 0.68), (0.69, 0.45)),
                ((0.47, 0.35), (0.57, 0.35)),
            ]:
                axis.annotate(
                    "",
                    xy=stop,
                    xytext=start,
                    arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle="->", lw=1.3, color="#444444"),
                )
        fig.suptitle("Frozen real-data evaluation design", y=1.02, fontsize=16)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_fwer_overall():
        fig, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(10.5, 5.4))
        datasets = ["PB", "CWRU"]
        methods = list(METHODS)
        width = 0.18
        x = np.arange(len(datasets))
        for index, method in enumerate(methods):
            summary = (
                REAL12_FWER_OVERALL[
                    REAL12_FWER_OVERALL.method == method
                ].set_index("dataset").loc[datasets].reset_index()
            )
            offset = (index - (len(methods) - 1) / 2) * width
            axis.bar(
                x + offset,
                summary.cluster_rate,
                width=width,
                color=METHOD_COLORS[method],
                label=METHODS[method],
                alpha=0.90,
                yerr=clustered_error(summary),
                capsize=3,
            )
        axis.axhline(
            REAL12_CONFIG["alpha"],
            color="black",
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=1.4,
            label="Nominal 0.05",
        )
        axis.set_xticks(x)
        axis.set_xticklabels(["Paderborn\nunit-disjoint", "CWRU\nblocked exploratory"])
        axis.set_ylabel("Healthy family-wise false-positive rate")
        axis.set_ylim(bottom=0)
        axis.set_title("Independent-test calibration by method")
        axis.legend(ncol=3, fontsize=9)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_pb_conditional_fwer():
        frame = REAL12_UNIT_DETAIL[
            (REAL12_UNIT_DETAIL.dataset == "PB") &
            (REAL12_UNIT_DETAIL.label == "N")
        ].copy()
        matrix = frame.pivot_table(
            index="unit",
            columns="condition",
            values="alarm_rate_robust_environment",
        )
        fig, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(9.5, 5.3))
        image = axis.imshow(matrix.to_numpy(), cmap="Blues", vmin=0, vmax=0.15, aspect="auto")
        axis.set_xticks(np.arange(len(matrix.columns)))
        axis.set_xticklabels(matrix.columns, rotation=25, ha="right")
        axis.set_yticks(np.arange(len(matrix.index)))
        axis.set_yticklabels(matrix.index)
        for i in range(matrix.shape[0]):
            for j in range(matrix.shape[1]):
                value = matrix.iloc[i, j]
                axis.text(j, i, f"{value:.2f}", ha="center", va="center", fontsize=9)
        fig.colorbar(image, ax=axis, label="Robust false-positive rate")
        axis.set_xlabel("Operating condition")
        axis.set_ylabel("Held-out healthy unit")
        axis.set_title("Paderborn unit-level robust calibration")
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def grouped_metric_figure(summary, title, ylabel):
        labels = [
            (dataset, label)
            for dataset in ["PB", "CWRU"]
            for label in sorted(summary.loc[summary.dataset == dataset, "label"].unique())
        ]
        fig, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(12.5, 5.5))
        methods = list(METHODS)
        width = 0.18
        x = np.arange(len(labels))
        for index, method in enumerate(methods):
            rows = []
            for dataset, label in labels:
                rows.append(summary[
                    (summary.dataset == dataset) &
                    (summary.label == label) &
                    (summary.method == method)
                ].iloc[0])
            method_frame = pd.DataFrame(rows)
            offset = (index - (len(methods) - 1) / 2) * width
            axis.bar(
                x + offset,
                method_frame.cluster_rate,
                width=width,
                color=METHOD_COLORS[method],
                alpha=0.90,
                label=METHODS[method],
                yerr=clustered_error(method_frame),
                capsize=2.5,
            )
        axis.set_xticks(x)
        axis.set_xticklabels([f"{d}\n{l}" for d, l in labels])
        axis.set_ylim(0, 1.05)
        axis.set_ylabel(ylabel)
        axis.set_title(title)
        axis.legend(ncol=4, fontsize=9, loc="lower right")
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    def figure_localization():
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(11.5, 4.8))
        for axis, dataset in zip(axes, ["PB", "CWRU"]):
            frame = h1[h1.dataset == dataset]
            labels = sorted(frame.label.unique())
            orders = [
                order for order in ["BPFO", "BPFI", "BSF"]
                if order in set(frame.argmax_order)
            ]
            table = pd.crosstab(
                frame.label,
                frame.argmax_order,
                normalize="index",
            ).reindex(index=labels, columns=orders, fill_value=0.0)
            image = axis.imshow(table.to_numpy(), cmap="YlGnBu", vmin=0, vmax=1, aspect="auto")
            axis.set_xticks(np.arange(len(orders)))
            axis.set_xticklabels(orders)
            axis.set_yticks(np.arange(len(labels)))
            axis.set_yticklabels(labels)
            for i in range(table.shape[0]):
                for j in range(table.shape[1]):
                    value = table.iloc[i, j]
                    axis.text(
                        j, i, f"{value:.2f}", ha="center", va="center",
                        color="white" if value > 0.55 else "black",
                    )
            axis.set_xlabel("Winning calibrated order")
            axis.set_ylabel("True fault class")
            axis.set_title("Paderborn" if dataset == "PB" else "CWRU")
        fig.colorbar(image, ax=axes.ravel().tolist(), label="Row-normalized fraction", shrink=0.82)
        fig.suptitle("Physics-guided fault-order localization", y=1.02)
        fig.subplots_adjust(wspace=0.30, top=0.86)
        return fig


    def figure_healthy_p_ecdf():

    def figure_method_tradeoff():
        fig, axis = plt.subplots(figsize=(8.8, 6.2))
        for dataset, marker in [("PB", "o"), ("CWRU", "s")]:
            for method in METHODS:
                fwer = REAL12_FWER_OVERALL[
                    (REAL12_FWER_OVERALL.dataset == dataset) &
                    (REAL12_FWER_OVERALL.method == method)
                ].cluster_rate.iloc[0]
                power = REAL12_DETECTION[
                    (REAL12_DETECTION.dataset == dataset) &
                    (REAL12_DETECTION.method == method)
                ].cluster_rate.mean()
                axis.scatter(
                    fwer,
                    power,
                    s=110,
                    marker=marker,
                    color=METHOD_COLORS[method],
                    edgecolor="black",
                    linewidth=0.7,
                )
                axis.annotate(
                    f"{dataset}: {METHODS[method]}",
                    (fwer, power),
                    xytext=(5, 5),
                    textcoords="offset points",
                    fontsize=8,
                )
        axis.axvline(REAL12_CONFIG["alpha"], color="black", linestyle="--", linewidth=1.2)
        axis.set_xlabel("Healthy false-positive rate")
        axis.set_ylabel("Mean class-balanced detection power")
        axis.set_xlim(left=0)
        axis.set_ylim(0, 1.03)
        axis.set_title("Calibration–detection operating trade-off")
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig

    def figure_condition_diagnosis():
        frame = h1.copy()
        summary = (
            frame.groupby(["dataset", "label", "condition"], sort=True)[
                "correct_diagnosis_robust_environment"

        )
        fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(12.2, 4.8), sharey=True)
        for axis, dataset in zip(axes, ["PB", "CWRU"]):
            subset = summary[summary.dataset == dataset]
            conditions = sorted(subset.condition.unique())
            labels = sorted(subset.label.unique())
            x = np.arange(len(conditions))
            width = 0.8 / len(labels)
            for index, label in enumerate(labels):
                values = (
                    subset[subset.label == label]
                    .set_index("condition")
                    .reindex(conditions)["correct_diagnosis_robust_environment"]
                    .to_numpy()
                )
                axis.bar(
                    x + (index - (len(labels) - 1) / 2) * width,
                    values,
                    width=width,
                    color=LABEL_COLORS[label],
                    alpha=0.85,
                    label=label,
                )
            axis.set_xticks(x)
            axis.set_xticklabels(conditions, rotation=25, ha="right")
            axis.set_ylim(0, 1.05)
            axis.set_xlabel("Operating condition")
            axis.set_title("Paderborn" if dataset == "PB" else "CWRU")
            axis.legend(title="Fault", fontsize=8)
        axes[0].set_ylabel("Correct-diagnosis probability")
        fig.suptitle("Environment-wise robust diagnosis", y=1.02)
        fig.tight_layout()
        return fig


    figure_jobs = [

    # 10. Decision report

    def compact_metric_table(frame, dataset, method):
        columns = [
            "label",
            "successes",
            "n",
            "rate",
            "cluster_rate",
            "cluster_ci_low",
            "cluster_ci_high",
            "n_clusters",
        ]
        return frame[
            (frame.dataset == dataset) &
            (frame.method == method)
        ][columns]

                "median_fr",

        ].round(4).to_string(index=False)
    )

    print("\nHealthy family-wise false-positive rates:")
    print(
        REAL12_FWER_OVERALL[
            [
                "dataset",
                "method_label",
                "successes",
                "n",
                "rate",

                "ci_high",

                "cluster_ci_high",

    )

        pb_robust_condition[

    print(
        compact_metric_table(

    print(cwru_report.round(4).to_string(index=False))

    print("\nRegistered Paderborn publication gate:")
    print(REAL12_PUBLICATION_GATE.to_string(index=False))
    print(f"\nREAL-DATA PUBLICATION GATE: {'PASS' if REAL12_GATE_PASS else 'REVISE'}")

    print(f"  {REAL12_REGISTRY_PATH.resolve()}")

        "the PNG-only publication atlas and select the final main-text panels."
