                "trial_seed": int(master_rng.integers(0, 2**32 - 1)),
                "interference_seed": int(master_rng.integers(0, 2**32 - 1)),
                "experiment": experiment,
                "level_value": float(level_value),
                "calibration_key": calibration_key,
                "split_role": split_role,
                "hypothesis": hypothesis,
                "bearing_key": config["bearing_key"],
                "fs": float(config["fs"]),
                "true_fr": float(config["true_fr_hz"]),
                "analysis_fr": float(analysis_fr),
                "revolutions": int(revolutions),
                "noise_profile": noise_profile,
                "target_order": target_order,
                "fault_name": fault_name,
                "snr_db": (
                    np.nan if hypothesis == "H0"
                    else float(config["fault_snr_db"])
                ),
                "added_impulse_rms": float(added_impulse_rms),
                "repetition": int(repetition),
                "configuration_sha256": CELL10B_CONFIG_HASH,
            })
            counter += 1

        def add_matched_condition(
            experiment,
            level_value,
            calibration_key,
            revolutions,
            analysis_fr,
        ):
            for environment in config["healthy_environments"]:
                for repetition in range(
                    config["n_calibration_per_environment"]
                ):
                    add_row(
                        experiment, level_value, calibration_key,
                        "calibration", "H0", environment, repetition,
                        revolutions, analysis_fr,
                    )
                for repetition in range(
                    config["n_test_healthy_per_environment"]
                ):
                    add_row(
                        experiment, level_value, calibration_key,
                        "test", "H0", environment, repetition,
                        revolutions, analysis_fr,
                    )

            for target_order in config["target_orders"]:
                for environment in config["fault_environments"]:
                    for repetition in range(
                        config["n_fault_per_order_environment"]
                    ):
                        add_row(
                            experiment, level_value, calibration_key,
                            "test", "H1", environment, repetition,
                            revolutions, analysis_fr,
                            target_order=target_order,
                        )

        # A. Calibration and testing share the same analysis-speed error. The
        # signal itself is always generated at the true 25-Hz shaft speed.
        for error_percent in config["speed_error_percent"]:
            analysis_fr = config["true_fr_hz"] * (
                1.0 + error_percent / 100.0
            )
            add_matched_condition(
                experiment="speed_error",
                level_value=error_percent,
                calibration_key=f"speed_error::{error_percent:+.3f}",
                revolutions=config["reference_revolutions"],
                analysis_fr=analysis_fr,
            )

        # B. Calibration and testing are duration matched at every segment length.
        for revolutions in config["segment_revolutions"]:
            add_matched_condition(
                experiment="segment_length",
                level_value=revolutions,
                calibration_key=f"segment_length::{int(revolutions)}",
                revolutions=revolutions,
                analysis_fr=config["true_fr_hz"],
            )

        # C. One uncontaminated, four-environment bank is used for all levels of
        # an unrepresented resonant-impulse shift. Test signals use colored noise
        # plus the registered added-interference RMS.
        impulse_key = "impulse_shift::reference"
        for environment in config["healthy_environments"]:
            for repetition in range(
                config["n_calibration_per_environment"]
            ):
                add_row(
                    "impulse_shift", 0.0, impulse_key,
                    "calibration", "H0", environment, repetition,
                    config["reference_revolutions"],
                    config["true_fr_hz"],
                    added_impulse_rms=0.0,
                )

        for impulse_rms in config["added_impulse_rms"]:
            for repetition in range(
                config["n_test_healthy_per_environment"]
            ):
                add_row(
                    "impulse_shift", impulse_rms, impulse_key,
                    "test", "H0", "colored", repetition,
                    config["reference_revolutions"],
                    config["true_fr_hz"],
                    added_impulse_rms=impulse_rms,
                )
            for target_order in config["target_orders"]:
                for repetition in range(
                    config["n_fault_per_order_environment"]
                ):
                    add_row(
                        "impulse_shift", impulse_rms, impulse_key,
                        "test", "H1", "colored", repetition,
                        config["reference_revolutions"],
                        config["true_fr_hz"],
                        target_order=target_order,
                        added_impulse_rms=impulse_rms,
                    )

        return pd.DataFrame(rows)


    CELL10B_DESIGN = make_cell10b_design(CELL10B_CONFIG)
    CELL10B_DESIGN.to_csv(DESIGN_PATH, index=False)

    print(f"  registered new trials: {len(CELL10B_DESIGN)}")
    print(
        CELL10B_DESIGN.groupby(
            ["experiment", "split_role", "hypothesis"]
        ).size().rename("rows").to_string()
    )


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Resolution audit and resumable feature extraction
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def cell10b_atomic_csv_write(dataframe, path):
        path = Path(path)
        temporary = path.with_name(path.stem + "_temporary.csv")
        dataframe.to_csv(temporary, index=False)
        os.replace(temporary, path)


    def cell10b_order_audit(revolutions, analysis_fr):
        n_samples = fast_len_leq(
            int(
                float(revolutions)
                * CELL10B_CONFIG["fs"]
                / CELL10B_CONFIG["true_fr_hz"]
            )
        )
        df_resolution = CELL10B_CONFIG["fs"] / n_samples
        kept, dropped = usable_orders_v3(
            bearing_key=CELL10B_RUNTIME_BEARING_KEY,
            fr=float(analysis_fr),
            df_resolution=float(df_resolution),
            order_tolerance=CELL10B_CONFIG["order_tolerance"],
            min_collision_bins=CELL10B_CONFIG["min_collision_bins"],
            collision_guard_bins=CELL10B_CONFIG["collision_guard_bins"],
        )
        return n_samples, df_resolution, kept, dropped


    admissibility_rows = []
    for revolutions in CELL10B_CONFIG["segment_revolutions"]:
        n_samples, df_resolution, kept, dropped = cell10b_order_audit(
            revolutions, CELL10B_CONFIG["true_fr_hz"]
        )
        for target_order in CELL10B_CONFIG["target_orders"]:
            diagnostics = dropped.get(target_order, {})
            admissibility_rows.append({
                "revolutions": int(revolutions),
                "n_samples": int(n_samples),
                "df_hz": float(df_resolution),
                "target_order": target_order,
                "admissible": bool(target_order in kept),
                "separation_bins": diagnostics.get(
                    "separation_bins", np.nan
                ),
                "required_bins": diagnostics.get(
                    "required_bins", np.nan
                ),
            })

    CELL10B_ADMISSIBILITY = pd.DataFrame(admissibility_rows)

    if CACHE_PATH.exists():
        completed = pd.read_csv(CACHE_PATH)
        if not completed.empty:
            cached_hashes = set(completed["configuration_sha256"].unique())
            if cached_hashes != {CELL10B_CONFIG_HASH}:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "The Cell 10B cache belongs to a different registration."
                )
    else:
        completed = pd.DataFrame()

    completed_ids = (
        set(completed["trial_id"]) if not completed.empty else set()
    )
    pending = CELL10B_DESIGN[
        ~CELL10B_DESIGN.trial_id.isin(completed_ids)
    ].copy()

    print(f"  cache contains       : {len(completed_ids)}")
    print(f"  pending              : {len(pending)}")

    new_rows = []
    error_rows = []
    start_time = time.time()

    for run_index, trial in enumerate(
        pending.itertuples(index=False), start=1
    ):
        try:
            n_samples, df_resolution, kept, dropped = cell10b_order_audit(
                trial.revolutions, trial.analysis_fr
            )
            primary_usable = [
                order for order in CELL10B_CONFIG["target_orders"]
                if order in kept
            ]

            result = trial._asdict()
            result.update({
                "n_samples_expected": int(n_samples),
                "df_hz": float(df_resolution),
                "BPFI_admissible": int("BPFI" in kept),
                "BPFO_admissible": int("BPFO" in kept),
                "status": "pending",
            })

            if not primary_usable:
                result["status"] = "no_primary_order_admissible"
                result["max_z"] = np.nan
                result["argmax_order"] = np.nan
                result["correct_localization"] = np.nan
                result["extract_seconds"] = 0.0
                new_rows.append(result)
                continue

            if (
                trial.hypothesis == "H1"
                and trial.target_order not in primary_usable
            ):
                result["status"] = "target_order_not_admissible"
                result["max_z"] = np.nan
                result["argmax_order"] = np.nan
                result["correct_localization"] = np.nan
                result["extract_seconds"] = 0.0
                new_rows.append(result)
                continue

            fault_target = (
                None if trial.hypothesis == "H0" else trial.target_order
            )
            fault_snr = (
                None if trial.hypothesis == "H0" else trial.snr_db
            )

            signal, metadata = simulate_bearing_record(
                seed=trial.trial_seed,
                bearing_key=CELL10B_RUNTIME_BEARING_KEY,
                fs=trial.fs,
                revolutions=trial.revolutions,
                fr=trial.true_fr,
                noise_profile=trial.noise_profile,
                fault_name=fault_target,
                snr_db=fault_snr,
            )

            if trial.added_impulse_rms > 0:
                interference_rng = np.random.default_rng(
                    int(trial.interference_seed)
                )
                interference = random_resonant_impulses(
                    rng=interference_rng,
                    n=len(signal),
                    fs=trial.fs,
                    event_rate_hz=0.30 * trial.true_fr,
                    resonance_hz=metadata["resonance_hz"],
                    decay_rate=metadata["decay_rate"],
                )
                signal = (
                    signal
                    + trial.added_impulse_rms
                    * metadata["background_rms"]
                    * unit_rms(interference)
                )
                signal = signal - np.mean(signal)

            extraction_start = time.time()
            features = extract_features_v3(
                sig=signal,
                fs=trial.fs,
                fr=trial.analysis_fr,
                bearing_key=CELL10B_RUNTIME_BEARING_KEY,
                seed_str=f"V4R::{trial.trial_id}",
                n_null=CELL10B_CONFIG["n_null"],
                order_tolerance=CELL10B_CONFIG["order_tolerance"],
                alpha=CELL10B_CONFIG["alpha"],
                min_collision_bins=CELL10B_CONFIG["min_collision_bins"],
                collision_guard_bins=CELL10B_CONFIG[
                    "collision_guard_bins"
                ],
                return_detail=False,
            )
            extraction_seconds = time.time() - extraction_start

            result.update(metadata)
            result["bearing_key"] = trial.bearing_key
            result["runtime_bearing_key"] = (
                CELL10B_RUNTIME_BEARING_KEY
            )
            result["status"] = "ok"
            result["extract_seconds"] = float(extraction_seconds)
            result["max_z"] = features.get("max_z", np.nan)
            result["argmax_order"] = features.get("argmax_order", np.nan)
            result["margin"] = features.get("margin", np.nan)

            for order_name in CELL10B_CONFIG["target_orders"]:
                for suffix in ("z", "p2f", "p_emp", "p_holm"):
                    key = f"{order_name}_{suffix}"
                    result[key] = features.get(key, np.nan)

            result["correct_localization"] = (
                np.nan if trial.hypothesis == "H0"
                else int(features.get("argmax_order") == trial.target_order)
            )
            new_rows.append(result)

        except Exception as error:
            error_rows.append({
                "trial_id": trial.trial_id,
                "experiment": trial.experiment,
                "level_value": trial.level_value,
                "error_type": type(error).__name__,
                "error_message": str(error),
            })

        checkpoint_due = (
            run_index % CELL10B_CONFIG["checkpoint_every"] == 0
            or run_index == len(pending)
        )

        if checkpoint_due:
            accumulated = pd.concat(
                [completed, pd.DataFrame(new_rows)], ignore_index=True
            )
            if not accumulated.empty:
                accumulated = (
                    accumulated.drop_duplicates("trial_id", keep="last")
                    .sort_values("trial_id")
                    .reset_index(drop=True)
                )
                cell10b_atomic_csv_write(accumulated, CACHE_PATH)

            if error_rows:
                pd.DataFrame(error_rows).to_csv(ERROR_PATH, index=False)

            elapsed = time.time() - start_time
            rate = run_index / max(elapsed, 1e-12)
            eta_seconds = (len(pending) - run_index) / max(rate, 1e-12)
            print(
                f"  {run_index:>5}/{len(pending)} pending trials  "
                f"elapsed={elapsed / 60:7.2f} min  "
                f"ETA={eta_seconds / 60:7.2f} min  "
                f"errors={len(error_rows)}"
            )

    if not CACHE_PATH.exists():
        raise RuntimeError("No Cell 10B feature cache was produced")

    CELL10B_TRIALS = pd.read_csv(CACHE_PATH)

    if error_rows:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{len(error_rows)} Cell 10B trials failed. Inspect {ERROR_PATH}."
        )

    expected_ids = set(CELL10B_DESIGN.trial_id)
    actual_ids = set(CELL10B_TRIALS.trial_id)
    if expected_ids != actual_ids:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Cell 10B is incomplete: {len(expected_ids - actual_ids)} "
            "registered rows are missing."
        )


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Robust conformal probabilities for the new sensitivity trials
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def attach_cell10b_probabilities(trials):
        output = []
        calibration = trials[
            (trials.split_role == "calibration")
            & (trials.status == "ok")
            & np.isfinite(pd.to_numeric(trials.max_z, errors="coerce"))
        ].copy()
        tests = trials[trials.split_role == "test"].copy()

        for calibration_key, test_group in tests.groupby(
            "calibration_key", sort=True
        ):
            test_group = test_group.copy()
            banks = calibration[
                calibration.calibration_key == calibration_key
            ]
            if banks.empty:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"No calibration rows for {calibration_key}"
                )

            finite_test = np.isfinite(
                pd.to_numeric(test_group.max_z, errors="coerce")
            ).to_numpy()
            p_robust = np.full(len(test_group), np.nan)

            if finite_test.any():
                test_scores = test_group.loc[
                    finite_test, "max_z"
                ].to_numpy(dtype=float)
                bank_probabilities = []

                for environment in CELL10B_CONFIG["healthy_environments"]:
                    bank_scores = banks.loc[
                        banks.noise_profile == environment, "max_z"
                    ].to_numpy(dtype=float)
                    if len(bank_scores) != CELL10B_CONFIG[
                        "n_calibration_per_environment"
                    ]:
                        raise RuntimeError(
                            f"Calibration bank {calibration_key}/{environment} "
                            f"has {len(bank_scores)} rows."
                        )
                    bank_probabilities.append(
                        v4_conformal_upper_probability(
                            bank_scores, test_scores
                        )
                    )

                p_robust[finite_test] = np.max(
                    np.column_stack(bank_probabilities), axis=1
                )

            test_group["p_robust_environment"] = p_robust
            test_group["alarm_robust_environment"] = np.where(
                np.isfinite(p_robust),
                (p_robust <= CELL10B_CONFIG["alpha"]).astype(float),
                np.nan,
            )
            is_fault = test_group.hypothesis.eq("H1")
            test_group["correct_diagnosis"] = np.where(
                is_fault
                & np.isfinite(test_group["alarm_robust_environment"])
                & np.isfinite(
                    pd.to_numeric(
                        test_group["correct_localization"], errors="coerce"
                    )
                ),
                (
                    test_group["alarm_robust_environment"].eq(1)
                    & test_group["correct_localization"].eq(1)
                ).astype(float),
                np.nan,
            )
            output.append(test_group)

        return pd.concat(output, ignore_index=True)


    CELL10B_RESULTS = attach_cell10b_probabilities(CELL10B_TRIALS)
    CELL10B_RESULTS.to_csv(RESULT_PATH, index=False)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Statistical summaries with Wilson intervals
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def cell10b_wilson_interval(successes, total, confidence=0.95):
        if total <= 0:
            return np.nan, np.nan
        z_value = norm.ppf(0.5 + confidence / 2.0)
        rate = successes / total
        denominator = 1.0 + z_value**2 / total
        centre = (rate + z_value**2 / (2 * total)) / denominator
        half_width = (
            z_value
            / denominator
            * np.sqrt(
                rate * (1 - rate) / total
                + z_value**2 / (4 * total**2)
            )
        )
        return centre - half_width, centre + half_width


    def summarize_binary(dataframe, group_columns, indicator, metric):
        data = dataframe.copy()
        data[indicator] = pd.to_numeric(data[indicator], errors="coerce")
        data = data[np.isfinite(data[indicator])]
        if data.empty:
            return pd.DataFrame()
        summary = (
            data.groupby(group_columns, dropna=False)[indicator]
            .agg(successes="sum", n="count", rate="mean")
            .reset_index()
        )
        intervals = [
            cell10b_wilson_interval(int(s), int(n))
            for s, n in zip(summary.successes, summary.n)
        ]
        summary["ci_low"] = [item[0] for item in intervals]
        summary["ci_high"] = [item[1] for item in intervals]
        summary["metric"] = metric
        return summary


    summary_frames = []
    for experiment in ("speed_error", "segment_length", "impulse_shift"):
        subset = CELL10B_RESULTS[
            CELL10B_RESULTS.experiment == experiment
        ]
        h0 = subset[subset.hypothesis == "H0"]
        h1 = subset[subset.hypothesis == "H1"]

        fwer = summarize_binary(
            h0,
            ["experiment", "level_value"],
            "alarm_robust_environment",
            "Healthy FWER",
        )
        if not fwer.empty:
            fwer["target_order"] = "ALL"
            summary_frames.append(fwer)

        for indicator, metric in [
            ("alarm_robust_environment", "Detection"),
            ("correct_localization", "Localization"),
            ("correct_diagnosis", "Correct diagnosis"),
        ]:
            result = summarize_binary(
                h1,
                ["experiment", "level_value", "target_order"],
                indicator,
                metric,
            )
            if not result.empty:
                summary_frames.append(result)

    CELL10B_SUMMARY = pd.concat(summary_frames, ignore_index=True)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Frozen-bank-size resampling using independent Cell 10 test trials
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    bank_calibration = V4_CALIBRATION[
        (V4_CALIBRATION.bearing_key == CELL10B_CONFIG["bearing_key"])
        & np.isclose(V4_CALIBRATION.fr, CELL10B_CONFIG["true_fr_hz"])
    ].copy()

    bank_h0 = V4_H0_RESULTS[
        (V4_H0_RESULTS.bearing_key == CELL10B_CONFIG["bearing_key"])
        & np.isclose(V4_H0_RESULTS.fr, CELL10B_CONFIG["true_fr_hz"])
    ].copy()

    bank_h1 = V4_H1_RESULTS[
        (V4_H1_RESULTS.bearing_key == CELL10B_CONFIG["bearing_key"])
        & np.isclose(V4_H1_RESULTS.fr, CELL10B_CONFIG["true_fr_hz"])
        & np.isclose(V4_H1_RESULTS.snr_db, CELL10B_CONFIG["fault_snr_db"])
        & V4_H1_RESULTS.target_order.isin(CELL10B_CONFIG["target_orders"])
    ].copy()

    minimum_available_bank = int(
        bank_calibration.groupby("noise_profile").size().min()
    )
    requested_sizes = CELL10B_CONFIG["calibration_bank_sizes"]
    if max(requested_sizes) > minimum_available_bank:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Requested bank size {max(requested_sizes)} exceeds the "
            f"available frozen bank size {minimum_available_bank}."
        )

    bank_rng = np.random.default_rng(CELL10B_CONFIG["seed"] + 9191)
    bank_rows = []

    for bank_size in requested_sizes:
        for resample in range(CELL10B_CONFIG["n_bank_resamples"]):
            all_tests = pd.concat(
                [
                    bank_h0.assign(_source="H0"),
                    bank_h1.assign(_source="H1"),
                ],
                ignore_index=True,
            )
            test_scores = all_tests.max_z.to_numpy(dtype=float)
            probabilities = []

            for environment in CELL10B_CONFIG["healthy_environments"]:
                candidates = bank_calibration[
                    bank_calibration.noise_profile == environment
                ].max_z.to_numpy(dtype=float)
                selected = bank_rng.choice(
                    candidates, size=int(bank_size), replace=False
                )
                probabilities.append(
                    v4_conformal_upper_probability(selected, test_scores)
                )

            p_robust = np.max(np.column_stack(probabilities), axis=1)
            all_tests["alarm"] = (
                p_robust <= CELL10B_CONFIG["alpha"]
            ).astype(int)

            h0_rate = float(
                all_tests.loc[all_tests._source == "H0", "alarm"].mean()
            )

            target_detection = []
            target_diagnosis = []
            for target_order in CELL10B_CONFIG["target_orders"]:
                group = all_tests[
                    (all_tests._source == "H1")
                    & (all_tests.target_order == target_order)
                ]
                target_detection.append(float(group.alarm.mean()))
                target_diagnosis.append(float(
                    (
                        group.alarm.eq(1)
                        & group.argmax_order.eq(target_order)
                    ).mean()
                ))

            bank_rows.append({
                "bank_size": int(bank_size),
                "resample": int(resample),
                "minimum_p": 1.0 / (bank_size + 1.0),
                "healthy_fwer": h0_rate,
                "minimum_class_detection": min(target_detection),
                "minimum_class_correct_diagnosis": min(target_diagnosis),
            })

    CELL10B_BANK_RESAMPLES = pd.DataFrame(bank_rows)

    bank_summary_rows = []
    for bank_size, group in CELL10B_BANK_RESAMPLES.groupby("bank_size"):
        for column, metric in [
            ("healthy_fwer", "Healthy FWER"),
            ("minimum_class_detection", "Minimum-class detection"),
            (
                "minimum_class_correct_diagnosis",
                "Minimum-class correct diagnosis",
            ),
        ]:
            values = group[column].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            bank_summary_rows.append({
                "bank_size": int(bank_size),
                "minimum_p": 1.0 / (bank_size + 1.0),
                "metric": metric,
                "rate": float(np.mean(values)),
                "ci_low": float(np.quantile(values, 0.025)),
                "ci_high": float(np.quantile(values, 0.975)),
                "n_resamples": int(len(values)),
            })

    CELL10B_BANK_SUMMARY = pd.DataFrame(bank_summary_rows)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Save concise publication tables
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    speed_table = CELL10B_SUMMARY[
        CELL10B_SUMMARY.experiment == "speed_error"
    ].copy()
    length_table = CELL10B_SUMMARY[
        CELL10B_SUMMARY.experiment == "segment_length"
    ].copy()
    impulse_table = CELL10B_SUMMARY[
        CELL10B_SUMMARY.experiment == "impulse_shift"
    ].copy()

    table_outputs = {
        "T_S057_speed_error_sensitivity.csv": speed_table,
        "T_S057_segment_length_sensitivity.csv": length_table,
        "T_S057_segment_admissibility.csv": CELL10B_ADMISSIBILITY,
        "T_S057_calibration_bank_resamples.csv": CELL10B_BANK_RESAMPLES,
        "T_S057_calibration_bank_size.csv": CELL10B_BANK_SUMMARY,
        "T_S057_impulsive_shift.csv": impulse_table,
    }

    for filename, dataframe in table_outputs.items():
        dataframe.to_csv(TABLE_ROOT / filename, index=False)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 8. Four-panel supplementary figure
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    mpl.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "DejaVu Sans",
        "font.weight": "bold",
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "font.size": 10.5,
        "axes.labelsize": 11.5,
        "xtick.labelsize": 10,
        "ytick.labelsize": 10,
        "legend.fontsize": 9,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.4,
        "savefig.dpi": 600,
    })

    COLORS = {
        "Healthy FWER": "#4C78A8",
        "BPFI": "#E45756",
        "BPFO": "#59A14F",
        "Minimum-class detection": "#B279A2",
        "Minimum-class correct diagnosis": "#F2C14E",
    }


    def style_axis(axis):
        for spine in axis.spines.values():
            spine.set_visible(True)
            spine.set_linewidth(1.4)
            spine.set_color("black")
        for label in axis.get_xticklabels() + axis.get_yticklabels():
            label.set_fontweight("bold")
        axis.grid(axis="y", color="0.86", linestyle=":", linewidth=0.9)


    def plot_summary_curve(axis, data, metric, target, color, label):
        group = data[
            (data.metric == metric) & (data.target_order == target)
        ].sort_values("level_value")
        if group.empty:
            return
        x = group.level_value.to_numpy(dtype=float)
        y = group.rate.to_numpy(dtype=float)
        lo = group.ci_low.to_numpy(dtype=float)
        hi = group.ci_high.to_numpy(dtype=float)
        axis.fill_between(x, lo, hi, color=color, alpha=0.13, linewidth=0)
        axis.plot(
            x, y, marker="o", markersize=5.5,
            linewidth=2.0, color=color, label=label
        )


    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(12.6, 8.8))
    ax_a, ax_b, ax_c, ax_d = axes.ravel()

    # (a) Speed error
    speed = speed_table.copy()
    plot_summary_curve(
        ax_a, speed, "Healthy FWER", "ALL",
        COLORS["Healthy FWER"], "Healthy FWER"
    )
    for target in CELL10B_CONFIG["target_orders"]:
        plot_summary_curve(
            ax_a, speed, "Correct diagnosis", target,
            COLORS[target], f"{target} diagnosis"
        )

    orders = char_orders(CELL10B_CONFIG["bearing_key"])
    for target in CELL10B_CONFIG["target_orders"]:
        tolerance = (
            CELL10B_CONFIG["order_tolerance"] / orders[target] * 100.0
        )
        ax_a.axvline(+tolerance, color=COLORS[target], linestyle=":", linewidth=1.2)
        ax_a.axvline(-tolerance, color=COLORS[target], linestyle=":", linewidth=1.2)

    ax_a.axhline(0.05, color="black", linestyle="--", linewidth=1.2)
    ax_a.set_xlabel("Shaft-speed error (%)")
    ax_a.set_ylabel("Probability")
    ax_a.set_ylim(-0.02, 1.05)
    ax_a.legend(loc="upper center", bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.20), ncol=3)
    style_axis(ax_a)

    # (b) Segment duration and admissibility
    length = length_table.copy()
    for target in CELL10B_CONFIG["target_orders"]:
        plot_summary_curve(
            ax_b, length, "Correct diagnosis", target,
            COLORS[target], f"{target} diagnosis"
        )
        audit = CELL10B_ADMISSIBILITY[
            CELL10B_ADMISSIBILITY.target_order == target
        ].sort_values("revolutions")
        ax_b.scatter(
            audit.revolutions,
            np.full(len(audit), 1.015 if target == "BPFI" else 0.965),
            marker="s", s=34,
            facecolor=np.where(audit.admissible, COLORS[target], "white"),
            edgecolor=COLORS[target], linewidth=1.2,
            clip_on=False,
        )

    ax_b.set_xlabel("Segment duration (revolutions)")
    ax_b.set_ylabel("Correct-diagnosis probability")
    ax_b.set_xticks(CELL10B_CONFIG["segment_revolutions"])
    ax_b.set_ylim(-0.02, 1.08)
    ax_b.legend(loc="upper center", bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.20), ncol=2)
    style_axis(ax_b)

    # (c) Calibration-bank size
    for metric in [
        "Healthy FWER",
        "Minimum-class correct diagnosis",
    ]:
        group = CELL10B_BANK_SUMMARY[
            CELL10B_BANK_SUMMARY.metric == metric
        ].sort_values("bank_size")
        color = COLORS[metric]
        ax_c.fill_between(
            group.bank_size.to_numpy(dtype=float),
            group.ci_low.to_numpy(dtype=float),
            group.ci_high.to_numpy(dtype=float),
            color=color, alpha=0.13, linewidth=0,
        )
        ax_c.plot(
            group.bank_size, group.rate,
            color=color, marker="o", linewidth=2.0, label=metric,
        )

    ax_c.axhline(0.05, color="black", linestyle="--", linewidth=1.2)
    ax_c.set_xlabel("Healthy units per environment bank")
    ax_c.set_ylabel("Probability")
    ax_c.set_xticks(CELL10B_CONFIG["calibration_bank_sizes"])
    ax_c.set_ylim(-0.02, 1.05)
    ax_c.legend(loc="upper center", bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.20), ncol=2)
    style_axis(ax_c)

    # (d) Unrepresented impulsive shift
    impulse = impulse_table.copy()
    plot_summary_curve(
        ax_d, impulse, "Healthy FWER", "ALL",
        COLORS["Healthy FWER"], "Healthy FWER"
    )
    for target in CELL10B_CONFIG["target_orders"]:
        plot_summary_curve(
            ax_d, impulse, "Correct diagnosis", target,
            COLORS[target], f"{target} diagnosis"
        )

    ax_d.axhline(0.05, color="black", linestyle="--", linewidth=1.2)
    ax_d.set_xlabel("Added resonant-impulse RMS / background RMS")
    ax_d.set_ylabel("Probability")
    ax_d.set_xticks(CELL10B_CONFIG["added_impulse_rms"])
    ax_d.set_ylim(-0.02, 1.05)
    ax_d.legend(loc="upper center", bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.20), ncol=3)
    style_axis(ax_d)

    for axis, panel in zip(axes.ravel(), ["(a)", "(b)", "(c)", "(d)"]):
        axis.text(
            0.5, -0.22, panel,
            transform=axis.transAxes,
            ha="center", va="top",
            fontsize=15, fontweight="bold",
        )

    fig.subplots_adjust(
        left=0.08, right=0.98, top=0.94, bottom=0.10,
        hspace=0.47, wspace=0.23,
    )

    FIGURE_PATH = FIGURE_ROOT / "S057_v4_postregistration_robustness.png"
    fig.savefig(
        FIGURE_PATH,
        dpi=600,
        bbox_inches="tight",
        facecolor="white",
        edgecolor="none",
        pad_inches=0.12,
    )
    plt.close(fig)


    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 9. Completion report
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("\n" + "=" * 104)
    print("CELL 10B POST-REGISTRATION SIMULATION ROBUSTNESS REPORT")
    print("=" * 104)
    print(f"Status                  : {CELL10B_CONFIG['status']}")
    print(f"Parent Cell 10 SHA-256 : {V4_CONFIG_HASH}")
    print(f"Cell 10B SHA-256       : {CELL10B_CONFIG_HASH}")
    print(f"Registered trials      : {len(CELL10B_DESIGN)}")
    print(f"Completed trial rows   : {len(CELL10B_TRIALS)}")
    print(f"Valid evaluated rows   : {len(CELL10B_RESULTS)}")
    print(f"Extraction errors      : {len(error_rows)}")
    print(f"Primary Cell 10 gate   : UNCHANGED")
    print("\nSaved tables:")
    for filename in table_outputs:
        print(f"  {TABLE_ROOT / filename}")
    print("\nSaved figure:")
    print(f"  {FIGURE_PATH}")
    print("\nCELL 10B COMPLETE")

    """
    Registered proposed-versus-comparator analysis
    ================================================
    Uses ONLY frozen Cell 10 publication tables.

    Outputs
    -------
    Tables:
      T_method_comparison_detailed.csv
      T_method_comparison_CWRU.csv
      T_method_comparison_Paderborn.csv
      T_noncontaining_intervals_diagnostic.csv
      T_method_comparison_CWRU.tex
      T_method_comparison_Paderborn.tex

    Figures:
      Fig08_registered_method_comparison.png
      Fig09_calibration_detection_surface.png

    Scientific safeguards
    ---------------------
    1. No method is rerun or tuned.
    2. No missing value is invented.
    3. Cluster/bootstrap intervals are retained even when they do not contain
       the raw point estimate.
    4. Confidence intervals are drawn using their actual endpoints rather than
       symmetric matplotlib yerr values.
    5. Oracle-environment calibration is explicitly labelled non-deployable.
    """

    from pathlib import Path
    import warnings
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
    import matplotlib as mpl
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch


    # =============================================================================
    # 1. Output directories
    # =============================================================================

    if "MSSP_DIRS" not in globals():
        MSSP_DIRS = {}

    DEFAULT_ROOT = Path("results_v2") / "MSSP_study_v1"

    TABLE_ROOT = Path(
        MSSP_DIRS.get("tables", DEFAULT_ROOT / "09_tables")
    )

    FIGURE_ROOT = Path(
        MSSP_DIRS.get("figures_main", DEFAULT_ROOT / "07_figures_main")
    )

    TABLE_ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    FIGURE_ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print("Table root :", TABLE_ROOT)
    print("Figure root:", FIGURE_ROOT)


    # =============================================================================
    # 2. Locate frozen publication tables
    # =============================================================================

    REQUIRED_FILES = {
        "overall": "T_v4_fwer_overall_publication.csv",
        "conditional": "T_v4_fwer_conditional_publication.csv",
        "detection": "T_v4_power_publication.csv",
        "diagnosis": "T_v4_correct_diagnosis_publication.csv",
    }


    def locate_unique_file(root, filename):
        """Locate one frozen table without silently choosing an ambiguous copy."""
        direct = root / filename
        if direct.exists():
            return direct

        candidates = sorted(
            root.rglob(filename),
            key=lambda path: (len(path.parts), str(path).lower())
        )

        if not candidates:
            raise FileNotFoundError(
                f"Could not locate {filename!r} under:\n{root}"
            )

        if len(candidates) > 1:
            print(
                f"Warning: multiple copies of {filename} were found. "
                f"Using the shortest path:\n  {candidates[0]}"
            )

        return candidates[0]


    PATHS = {
        name: locate_unique_file(TABLE_ROOT, filename)
        for name, filename in REQUIRED_FILES.items()
    }

    print("\nLocated frozen publication tables:")
    for name, path in PATHS.items():
        print(f"  {name:12s}: {path}")


    # =============================================================================
    # 3. Read and standardize
    # =============================================================================

    FW = pd.read_csv(PATHS["overall"])
    FWC = pd.read_csv(PATHS["conditional"])
    PW = pd.read_csv(PATHS["detection"])
    CD = pd.read_csv(PATHS["diagnosis"])


    def strip_column_names(frame):
        out = frame.copy()
        out.columns = [str(column).strip() for column in out.columns]
        return out


    FW = strip_column_names(FW)
    FWC = strip_column_names(FWC)
    PW = strip_column_names(PW)
    CD = strip_column_names(CD)


    def require_columns(frame, required, table_name):
        missing = [column for column in required if column not in frame.columns]
        if missing:
            raise KeyError(
                f"{table_name} is missing required columns: {missing}\n"
                f"Available columns: {list(frame.columns)}"
            )


    require_columns(
        FW,
        ["bearing_key", "method", "rate", "ci_low", "ci_high"],
        "Overall FWER table"
    )
    require_columns(
        FWC,
        ["bearing_key", "method", "rate", "ci_low", "ci_high"],
        "Conditional FWER table"
    )
    require_columns(
        PW,
        [
            "bearing_key", "method", "target_order", "snr_db",
            "rate", "ci_low", "ci_high"
        ],
        "Detection-power table"
    )
    require_columns(
        CD,
        [
            "bearing_key", "method", "target_order", "snr_db",
            "rate", "ci_low", "ci_high"
        ],
        "Correct-diagnosis table"
    )


    for frame in (FW, FWC, PW, CD):
        frame["bearing_key"] = frame["bearing_key"].astype(str).str.strip()
        frame["method"] = frame["method"].astype(str).str.strip()

    for frame in (PW, CD):
        frame["target_order"] = frame["target_order"].astype(str).str.strip()
        frame["snr_db"] = pd.to_numeric(frame["snr_db"], errors="coerce")


    # =============================================================================
    # 4. Scientifically appropriate interval validation
    # =============================================================================

    def validate_probability_table(frame, table_name):
        """
        Validate probability estimates without assuming that a cluster/bootstrap
        interval must contain the raw point estimate.

        A percentile/bootstrap or cluster-aware interval can legitimately exclude
        the uncorrected sample proportion. Such rows are retained and documented.
        """
        out = frame.copy()

        for column in ["rate", "ci_low", "ci_high"]:
            out[column] = pd.to_numeric(out[column], errors="coerce")

        required = out[["rate", "ci_low", "ci_high"]]
        valid_rows = required.notna().all(axis=1)

        if not valid_rows.any():
            raise ValueError(
                f"{table_name} contains no complete estimate/interval rows."
            )

        values = required.loc[valid_rows]

        if (
            (values < -1e-10).any().any()
            or (values > 1 + 1e-10).any().any()
        ):
            bad = out.loc[
                valid_rows
                & (
                    (out["rate"] < 0)
                    | (out["rate"] > 1)
                    | (out["ci_low"] < 0)
                    | (out["ci_low"] > 1)
                    | (out["ci_high"] < 0)
                    | (out["ci_high"] > 1)
                )
            ]
            raise ValueError(
                f"{table_name} contains values outside [0,1]:\n"
                f"{bad.head(20).to_string(index=False)}"
            )

        reversed_interval = (
            valid_rows & (out["ci_low"] > out["ci_high"])
        )

        if reversed_interval.any():
            bad = out.loc[reversed_interval]
            raise ValueError(
                f"{table_name} contains reversed interval endpoints:\n"
                f"{bad.head(20).to_string(index=False)}"
            )

        noncontaining = (
            valid_rows
            & (
                (out["rate"] < out["ci_low"])
                | (out["rate"] > out["ci_high"])
            )
        )

        diagnostic = out.loc[noncontaining].copy()
        diagnostic.insert(0, "source_table", table_name)

        if len(diagnostic):
            print(
                f"Notice: {table_name} contains {len(diagnostic)} "
                "interval(s) that do not contain the raw point estimate. "
                "They are retained because cluster/bootstrap percentile "
                "intervals need not be centered on that estimate."
            )

        return out, diagnostic


    FW, diag_fw = validate_probability_table(FW, "Overall FWER")
    FWC, diag_fwc = validate_probability_table(FWC, "Conditional FWER")
    PW, diag_pw = validate_probability_table(PW, "Detection power")
    CD, diag_cd = validate_probability_table(CD, "Correct diagnosis")

    NONCONTAINING = pd.concat(
        [diag_fw, diag_fwc, diag_pw, diag_cd],
        ignore_index=True,
        sort=False
    )

    NONCONTAINING_PATH = (
        TABLE_ROOT / "T_noncontaining_intervals_diagnostic.csv"
    )
    NONCONTAINING.to_csv(NONCONTAINING_PATH, index=False)

    print(
        "\nNon-containing interval diagnostic:",
        NONCONTAINING_PATH,
        f"({len(NONCONTAINING)} rows)"
    )


    # =============================================================================
    # 5. Registered methods and geometries
    # =============================================================================

    METHODS = [
        "order_null",
        "pooled_condition",
        "oracle_environment",
        "robust_environment",
    ]

    METHOD_LABELS = {
        "order_null": "Order-null Holm",
        "pooled_condition": "Pooled conformal",
        "oracle_environment": "Oracle environment†",
        "robust_environment": "Robust max-p conformal*",
    }

    METHOD_SHORT = {
        "order_null": "ON",
        "pooled_condition": "PC",
        "oracle_environment": "OE",
        "robust_environment": "RM",
    }

    BEARING_KEYS = [
        "CWRU_DE_6205",
        "PADERBORN_6203",
    ]

    BEARING_LABELS = {
        "CWRU_DE_6205": "CWRU 6205",
        "PADERBORN_6203": "Paderborn 6203",
    }

    METHOD_COLORS = {
        "order_null": "#7F7F7F",
        "pooled_condition": "#4C78A8",
        "oracle_environment": "#B279A2",
        "robust_environment": "#2E8B57",
    }

    BEARING_MARKERS = {
        "CWRU_DE_6205": "o",
        "PADERBORN_6203": "s",
    }

    available_methods = sorted(
        set(FW["method"])
        | set(FWC["method"])
        | set(PW["method"])
        | set(CD["method"])
    )

    print("\nAvailable methods in frozen files:")
    for method in available_methods:
        print(" ", method)

    missing_methods = [method for method in METHODS if method not in available_methods]

    if missing_methods:
        warnings.warn(
            "The following registered comparison methods were not present in "
            "the frozen tables and will be reported as N/E: "
            + ", ".join(missing_methods)
        )


    # =============================================================================
    # 6. Conservative extraction helpers
    # =============================================================================

    ID_COLUMNS = [
        "bearing_key",
        "method",
        "target_order",
        "snr_db",
        "noise_profile",
        "fr",
        "condition",
        "environment",
    ]


    def source_description(row):
        pieces = []

        for column in ID_COLUMNS:
            if column in row.index and pd.notna(row[column]):
                pieces.append(f"{column}={row[column]}")

        return "; ".join(pieces)


    def empty_metric():
        return {
            "rate": np.nan,
            "ci_low": np.nan,
            "ci_high": np.nan,
            "source": "N/E",
        }


    def select_overall_fwer(frame, bearing_key, method):
        subset = frame[
            (frame["bearing_key"] == bearing_key)
            & (frame["method"] == method)
        ].copy()

        subset = subset.dropna(subset=["rate", "ci_low", "ci_high"])

        if subset.empty:
            return empty_metric()

        # If duplicates exist, retain the largest observed overall FWER.
        subset = subset.sort_values(
            ["rate", "ci_high"],
            ascending=[False, False],
            kind="mergesort"
        )

        row = subset.iloc[0]

        return {
            "rate": float(row["rate"]),
            "ci_low": float(row["ci_low"]),
            "ci_high": float(row["ci_high"]),
            "source": source_description(row),
        }


    def select_worst_conditional_fwer(frame, bearing_key, method):
        subset = frame[
            (frame["bearing_key"] == bearing_key)
            & (frame["method"] == method)
        ].copy()

        subset = subset.dropna(subset=["rate", "ci_low", "ci_high"])

        if subset.empty:
            return empty_metric()

        # Largest observed conditional FWER; highest upper endpoint breaks ties.
        subset = subset.sort_values(
            ["rate", "ci_high"],
            ascending=[False, False],
            kind="mergesort"
        )

        row = subset.iloc[0]

        return {
            "rate": float(row["rate"]),
            "ci_low": float(row["ci_low"]),
            "ci_high": float(row["ci_high"]),
            "source": source_description(row),
        }


    def select_minimum_performance(
        frame,
        bearing_key,
        method,
        snr_db
    ):
        subset = frame[
            (frame["bearing_key"] == bearing_key)
            & (frame["method"] == method)
            & np.isclose(
                pd.to_numeric(frame["snr_db"], errors="coerce"),
                float(snr_db),
                atol=1e-8
            )
        ].copy()

        subset = subset.dropna(subset=["rate", "ci_low", "ci_high"])

        if subset.empty:
            return empty_metric()

        # Minimum across registered fault orders.
        # Lowest lower endpoint breaks ties conservatively.
        subset = subset.sort_values(
            ["rate", "ci_low"],
            ascending=[True, True],
            kind="mergesort"
        )

        row = subset.iloc[0]

        return {
            "rate": float(row["rate"]),
            "ci_low": float(row["ci_low"]),
            "ci_high": float(row["ci_high"]),
            "source": source_description(row),
        }


    # =============================================================================
    # 7. Build detailed comparison table
    # =============================================================================

    comparison_rows = []

    for bearing_key in BEARING_KEYS:
        for method in METHODS:
            overall = select_overall_fwer(
                FW, bearing_key, method
            )
            conditional = select_worst_conditional_fwer(
                FWC, bearing_key, method
            )
            detection_m4 = select_minimum_performance(
                PW, bearing_key, method, -4
            )
            detection_0 = select_minimum_performance(
                PW, bearing_key, method, 0
            )
            diagnosis_m4 = select_minimum_performance(
                CD, bearing_key, method, -4
            )
            diagnosis_0 = select_minimum_performance(
                CD, bearing_key, method, 0
            )

            comparison_rows.append({
                "bearing_key": bearing_key,
                "geometry": BEARING_LABELS[bearing_key],
                "method": method,
                "method_label": METHOD_LABELS[method],
                "is_proposed": method == "robust_environment",
                "is_oracle": method == "oracle_environment",

                "overall_fwer": overall["rate"],
                "overall_ci_low": overall["ci_low"],
                "overall_ci_high": overall["ci_high"],
                "overall_source": overall["source"],

                "worst_conditional_fwer": conditional["rate"],
                "conditional_ci_low": conditional["ci_low"],
                "conditional_ci_high": conditional["ci_high"],
                "conditional_source": conditional["source"],

                "minimum_detection_m4": detection_m4["rate"],
                "detection_m4_ci_low": detection_m4["ci_low"],
                "detection_m4_ci_high": detection_m4["ci_high"],
                "detection_m4_source": detection_m4["source"],

                "minimum_detection_0": detection_0["rate"],
                "detection_0_ci_low": detection_0["ci_low"],
                "detection_0_ci_high": detection_0["ci_high"],
                "detection_0_source": detection_0["source"],

                "minimum_diagnosis_m4": diagnosis_m4["rate"],
                "diagnosis_m4_ci_low": diagnosis_m4["ci_low"],
                "diagnosis_m4_ci_high": diagnosis_m4["ci_high"],
                "diagnosis_m4_source": diagnosis_m4["source"],

                "minimum_diagnosis_0": diagnosis_0["rate"],
                "diagnosis_0_ci_low": diagnosis_0["ci_low"],
                "diagnosis_0_ci_high": diagnosis_0["ci_high"],
                "diagnosis_0_source": diagnosis_0["source"],
            })


    COMPARISON = pd.DataFrame(comparison_rows)

    method_order = {
        method: index for index, method in enumerate(METHODS)
    }
    bearing_order = {
        bearing: index for index, bearing in enumerate(BEARING_KEYS)
    }

    COMPARISON["_method_order"] = COMPARISON["method"].map(method_order)
    COMPARISON["_bearing_order"] = COMPARISON["bearing_key"].map(bearing_order)

    COMPARISON = (
        COMPARISON
        .sort_values(["_bearing_order", "_method_order"])
        .drop(columns=["_method_order", "_bearing_order"])
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )

    DETAILED_PATH = TABLE_ROOT / "T_method_comparison_detailed.csv"
    COMPARISON.to_csv(DETAILED_PATH, index=False)

    print("\nDetailed comparison table:")
    print(COMPARISON.to_string(index=False))
    print("\nSaved:", DETAILED_PATH)


    # =============================================================================
    # 8. Reviewer-readable manuscript tables
    # =============================================================================

    def format_estimate_interval(rate, lower, upper):
        if not np.isfinite(rate):
            return "N/E"

        if np.isfinite(lower) and np.isfinite(upper):
            return (
                f"{100 * rate:.1f} "
                f"[{100 * lower:.1f}, {100 * upper:.1f}]"
            )

        return f"{100 * rate:.1f}"


    def build_manuscript_table(bearing_key):
        subset = (
            COMPARISON[COMPARISON["bearing_key"] == bearing_key]
            .set_index("method")
            .reindex(METHODS)
            .reset_index()
        )

        rows = []

        for _, row in subset.iterrows():
            method = row["method"]
            label = METHOD_LABELS.get(method, method)

            rows.append({
                "Method": label,
                "Overall FWER": format_estimate_interval(
                    row["overall_fwer"],
                    row["overall_ci_low"],
                    row["overall_ci_high"],
                ),
                "Worst FWER": format_estimate_interval(
                    row["worst_conditional_fwer"],
                    row["conditional_ci_low"],
                    row["conditional_ci_high"],
                ),
                "Detection −4 dB": format_estimate_interval(
                    row["minimum_detection_m4"],
                    row["detection_m4_ci_low"],
                    row["detection_m4_ci_high"],
                ),
                "Diagnosis −4 dB": format_estimate_interval(
                    row["minimum_diagnosis_m4"],
                    row["diagnosis_m4_ci_low"],
                    row["diagnosis_m4_ci_high"],
                ),
            })

        return pd.DataFrame(rows)


    MANUSCRIPT_TABLES = {}

    for bearing_key in BEARING_KEYS:
        label = BEARING_LABELS[bearing_key]
        safe_name = (
            "CWRU"
            if bearing_key == "CWRU_DE_6205"
            else "Paderborn"
        )

        table = build_manuscript_table(bearing_key)
        MANUSCRIPT_TABLES[bearing_key] = table

        csv_path = (
            TABLE_ROOT / f"T_method_comparison_{safe_name}.csv"
        )
        tex_path = (
            TABLE_ROOT / f"T_method_comparison_{safe_name}.tex"
        )

        table.to_csv(csv_path, index=False)

        with warnings.catch_warnings():
            warnings.simplefilter("ignore", FutureWarning)
            latex = table.to_latex(
                index=False,
                escape=True,
                column_format="lcccc"
            )

        tex_path.write_text(latex, encoding="utf-8")

        print(f"\n{label} manuscript comparison:")
        print(table.to_string(index=False))
        print("Saved:", csv_path)
        print("Saved:", tex_path)


    # =============================================================================
    # 9. Publication style
    # =============================================================================

    mpl.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "DejaVu Sans",
        "font.weight": "bold",
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "font.size": 10.5,
        "axes.labelsize": 12,
        "xtick.labelsize": 10,
        "ytick.labelsize": 10,
        "legend.fontsize": 10,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.5,
        "xtick.major.width": 1.5,
        "ytick.major.width": 1.5,
        "xtick.major.size": 5,
        "ytick.major.size": 5,
        "xtick.direction": "out",
        "ytick.direction": "out",
        "savefig.dpi": 600,
    })


    def finish_axes(ax):
        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_visible(True)
            spine.set_color("black")
            spine.set_linewidth(1.5)

        for label in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            label.set_fontweight("bold")


    def add_panel_label(ax, label, y=-0.27):
        ax.text(
            0.5,
            y,
            label,
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            ha="center",
            va="top",
            fontsize=14,
            fontweight="bold"
        )


    # =============================================================================
    # 10. Figure 8: registered method comparison
    # =============================================================================

    METRIC_PANELS = [
        {
            "column": "overall_fwer",
            "low": "overall_ci_low",
            "high": "overall_ci_high",
            "ylabel": "Overall healthy FWER",
            "direction": "lower",
            "criterion": 0.075,
            "panel": "(a)",
        },
        {
            "column": "worst_conditional_fwer",
            "low": "conditional_ci_low",
            "high": "conditional_ci_high",
            "ylabel": "Worst conditional FWER",
            "direction": "lower",
            "criterion": 0.10,
            "panel": "(b)",
        },
        {
            "column": "minimum_detection_m4",
            "low": "detection_m4_ci_low",
            "high": "detection_m4_ci_high",
            "ylabel": "Minimum detection probability",
            "direction": "higher",
            "criterion": 0.75,
            "panel": "(c)",
        },
        {
            "column": "minimum_diagnosis_m4",
            "low": "diagnosis_m4_ci_low",
            "high": "diagnosis_m4_ci_high",
            "ylabel": "Minimum correct-diagnosis probability",
            "direction": "higher",
            "criterion": None,
            "panel": "(d)",
        },
    ]


    def plot_endpoint_interval(
        ax,
        x,
        rate,
        lower,
        upper,
        color,
        marker,
    ):
        """
        Plot the point estimate and interval independently.

        This remains valid when a cluster/bootstrap interval does not contain
        the raw point estimate.
        """
        if not np.isfinite(rate):
            return

        if np.isfinite(lower) and np.isfinite(upper):
            ax.vlines(
                x,
                lower,
                upper,
                color=color,
                linewidth=2.0,
                zorder=2
            )
            ax.hlines(
                [lower, upper],
                x - 0.045,
                x + 0.045,
                color=color,
                linewidth=2.0,
                zorder=2
            )

        ax.scatter(
            x,
            rate,
            s=90,
            marker=marker,
            color=color,
            edgecolor="black",
            linewidth=1.1,
            zorder=4
        )


    fig, axes = plt.subplots(
        2,
        2,
        figsize=(12.4, 8.5),
        sharex=True
    )

    axes = axes.ravel()
    x_base = np.arange(len(METHODS), dtype=float)
    offsets = {
        "CWRU_DE_6205": -0.10,
        "PADERBORN_6203": 0.10,
    }

    for ax, specification in zip(axes, METRIC_PANELS):
        metric = specification["column"]
        lower_column = specification["low"]
        upper_column = specification["high"]

        for bearing_key in BEARING_KEYS:
            subset = (
                COMPARISON[
                    COMPARISON["bearing_key"] == bearing_key
                ]
                .set_index("method")
                .reindex(METHODS)
            )

            for method_index, method in enumerate(METHODS):
                if method not in subset.index:
                    continue

                row = subset.loc[method]

                plot_endpoint_interval(
                    ax=ax,
                    x=x_base[method_index] + offsets[bearing_key],
                    rate=float(row[metric])
                    if pd.notna(row[metric]) else np.nan,
                    lower=float(row[lower_column])
                    if pd.notna(row[lower_column]) else np.nan,
                    upper=float(row[upper_column])
                    if pd.notna(row[upper_column]) else np.nan,
                    color=METHOD_COLORS[method],
                    marker=BEARING_MARKERS[bearing_key],
                )

        criterion = specification["criterion"]

        if criterion is not None:
            ax.axhline(
                criterion,
                color="black",
                linestyle="--",
                linewidth=1.7,
                zorder=1
            )

        ax.set_ylabel(specification["ylabel"])

        if specification["direction"] == "lower":
            observed_high = pd.to_numeric(
                COMPARISON[upper_column],
                errors="coerce"
            ).max()

            if not np.isfinite(observed_high):
                observed_high = 0.20

            ymax = min(1.0, max(0.15, observed_high * 1.15))
            ax.set_ylim(0, ymax)
        else:
            ax.set_ylim(0.30, 1.03)
            ax.set_yticks(np.arange(0.4, 1.01, 0.2))

        ax.set_xticks(x_base)
        ax.set_xticklabels(
            ["Order-null", "Pooled", "Oracle†", "Robust*"],
            rotation=0
        )

        ax.grid(
            axis="y",
            color="0.85",
            linestyle=":",
            linewidth=1.0,
            zorder=0
        )

        finish_axes(ax)
        add_panel_label(ax, specification["panel"])


    legend_handles = [
        Line2D(
            [],
            [],
            marker="o",
            linestyle="none",
            markerfacecolor="white",
            markeredgecolor="black",
            markersize=8,
            label="CWRU 6205"
        ),
        Line2D(
            [],
            [],
            marker="s",
            linestyle="none",
            markerfacecolor="white",
            markeredgecolor="black",
            markersize=8,
            label="Paderborn 6203"
        ),
        Line2D(
            [],
            [],
            color="black",
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=1.7,
            label="Registered criterion"
        ),
    ]

    fig.legend(
        handles=legend_handles,
        loc="upper center",
        bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.015),
        ncol=3,
        frameon=True,
        edgecolor="black",
        framealpha=1.0
    )

    fig.subplots_adjust(
        left=0.09,
        right=0.985,
        top=0.91,
        bottom=0.11,
        hspace=0.48,
        wspace=0.28
    )

    FIG08_PATH = (
        FIGURE_ROOT / "Fig08_registered_method_comparison.png"
    )

    fig.savefig(
        FIG08_PATH,
        dpi=600,
        bbox_inches="tight",
        facecolor="white",
        edgecolor="none",
        pad_inches=0.12
    )

    plt.close(fig)

    print("\nSaved:", FIG08_PATH)


    # =============================================================================
    # 11. Figure 9: calibration–detection operating surface
    # =============================================================================

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8.6, 6.3))

    for bearing_key in BEARING_KEYS:
        subset = (
            COMPARISON[
                COMPARISON["bearing_key"] == bearing_key
            ]
            .set_index("method")
            .reindex(METHODS)
        )

        for method in METHODS:
            if method not in subset.index:
                continue

            row = subset.loc[method]

            x_value = row["worst_conditional_fwer"]
            y_value = row["minimum_detection_m4"]

            if not (
                np.isfinite(x_value)
                and np.isfinite(y_value)
            ):
                continue

            ax.scatter(
                x_value,
                y_value,
                s=145,
                marker=BEARING_MARKERS[bearing_key],
                color=METHOD_COLORS[method],
                edgecolor="black",
                linewidth=1.2,
                zorder=4
            )

            ax.annotate(
                METHOD_SHORT[method],
                (x_value, y_value),
                xytext=(6, 5),
                textcoords="offset points",
                fontsize=9,
                fontweight="bold",
                color=METHOD_COLORS[method]
            )


    ax.axvline(
        0.10,
        color="black",
        linestyle="--",
        linewidth=1.7
    )

    ax.axhline(
        0.75,
        color="black",
        linestyle=":",
        linewidth=1.7
    )

    ax.fill_between(
        [0, 0.10],
        0.75,
        1.02,
        color="#DFF0D8",
        alpha=0.45,
        zorder=0
    )

    ax.set_xlabel("Worst conditional healthy FWER")
    ax.set_ylabel("Minimum detection probability at −4 dB")
    ax.set_xlim(
        0,
        max(
            0.12,
            min(
                1.0,
                pd.to_numeric(
                    COMPARISON["worst_conditional_fwer"],
                    errors="coerce"
                ).max() * 1.15
            )
        )
    )
    ax.set_ylim(0.35, 1.02)

    ax.grid(
        color="0.86",
        linestyle=":",
        linewidth=1.0,
        zorder=0
    )

    method_handles = [
        Patch(
            facecolor=METHOD_COLORS[method],
            edgecolor="black",
            label=METHOD_SHORT[method]
        )
        for method in METHODS
    ]

    geometry_handles = [
        Line2D(
            [],
            [],
            marker=BEARING_MARKERS[bearing_key],
            linestyle="none",
            markerfacecolor="white",
            markeredgecolor="black",
            markersize=8,
            label=BEARING_LABELS[bearing_key]
        )
        for bearing_key in BEARING_KEYS
    ]

    ax.legend(
        handles=method_handles + geometry_handles,
        loc="upper center",
        bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.18),
        ncol=3,
        frameon=True,
        edgecolor="black",
        framealpha=1.0
    )

    finish_axes(ax)

    fig.subplots_adjust(
        left=0.13,
        right=0.98,
        bottom=0.14,
        top=0.83
    )

    FIG09_PATH = (
        FIGURE_ROOT / "Fig09_calibration_detection_surface.png"
    )

    fig.savefig(
        FIG09_PATH,
        dpi=600,
        bbox_inches="tight",
        facecolor="white",
        edgecolor="none",
        pad_inches=0.12
    )

    plt.close(fig)

    print("Saved:", FIG09_PATH)


    # =============================================================================
    # 12. Automated integrity report
    # =============================================================================

    def check_expected_rows(frame, label):
        expected = len(BEARING_KEYS) * len(METHODS)

        if len(frame) != expected:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"{label} has {len(frame)} rows; expected {expected}."
            )


    check_expected_rows(COMPARISON, "Comparison table")

    numeric_columns = [
        "overall_fwer",
        "worst_conditional_fwer",
        "minimum_detection_m4",
        "minimum_detection_0",
        "minimum_diagnosis_m4",
        "minimum_diagnosis_0",
    ]

    for column in numeric_columns:
        finite = pd.to_numeric(
            COMPARISON[column],
            errors="coerce"
        ).dropna()

        if not finite.between(0, 1).all():
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Comparison metric {column!r} contains invalid probabilities."
            )


    print("\n" + "=" * 88)
    print("REGISTERED METHOD-COMPARISON EXPORT COMPLETE")
    print("=" * 88)
    print("No simulations were rerun.")
    print("No missing comparator values were imputed.")
    print("No post-gate candidate was selected.")
    print("Oracle environment is retained only as a non-deployable reference.")
    print("Cluster/bootstrap intervals were plotted from their actual endpoints.")
    print("\n* Proposed method.")
    print("† Oracle condition information; not available in deployment.")
    print("N/E = not estimable from the frozen publication table.")

    print("\nSuggested Table caption:")
    print(
        "Comparison of the registered calibration strategies under the controlled "
        "simulation design. Values are percentages with 95% intervals in brackets. "
        "Detection and correct diagnosis report the minimum across registered "
        "physical fault orders. The oracle-environment method is a non-deployable "
        "reference; robust max-p conformal is the proposed method."
    )

    print("\nSuggested Fig. 8 caption:")
    print(
        "Registered comparison of calibration strategies for the CWRU 6205 and "
        "Paderborn 6203 simulation geometries: (a) overall healthy FWER, "
        "(b) worst condition-specific healthy FWER, (c) minimum fault-detection "
        "probability at −4 dB, and (d) minimum detection with correct physical-order "
        "localization at −4 dB. Vertical lines show the reported 95% interval "
        "endpoints; dashed lines denote registered criteria where applicable."
    )

    print("\nSuggested Fig. 9 caption:")
    print(
        "Calibration–detection operating surface at −4 dB. The shaded upper-left "
        "region satisfies both the registered conditional false-alarm limit and "
        "minimum detection criterion. ON: order-null Holm; PC: pooled conformal; "
        "OE: oracle environment; RM: robust max-p conformal."
    )

    """
    Figure 8 — registered method comparison (simulation).
    Assumes COMPARISON, METHODS, BEARING_KEYS, METHOD_COLORS, BEARING_MARKERS,
    METHOD_LABELS already exist from the comparison-export cell.
    """
    from pathlib import Path
    import numpy as np, pandas as pd
    import matplotlib as mpl, matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D

    FIGURE_ROOT = Path(MSSP_DIRS["figures_main"]); FIGURE_ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Reference levels. SET THIS HONESTLY once you have checked Cell 10.
    #   True  -> lines are labelled "Registered criterion"
    #   False -> lines are labelled "Reference level" (criteria applied to
    #            the real-data evaluations only)
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    SIM_GATE_REGISTERED = False
    REF_LINE_LABEL = ("Registered criterion" if SIM_GATE_REGISTERED
                      else "Reference level (not a simulation criterion)")

    METRIC_PANELS = [
        dict(column="overall_fwer", low="overall_ci_low", high="overall_ci_high",
             ylabel="Overall healthy FWER", kind="fwer", ref=0.075, panel="(a)"),
        dict(column="worst_conditional_fwer", low="conditional_ci_low",
             high="conditional_ci_high", ylabel="Worst conditional FWER",
             kind="fwer", ref=0.100, panel="(b)"),
        dict(column="minimum_detection_m4", low="detection_m4_ci_low",
             high="detection_m4_ci_high",
             ylabel="Minimum detection probability", kind="prob", ref=0.75,
             panel="(c)"),
        dict(column="minimum_diagnosis_m4", low="diagnosis_m4_ci_low",
             high="diagnosis_m4_ci_high",
             ylabel="Minimum correct-diagnosis probability", kind="prob",
             ref=None, panel="(d)"),
    ]

    mpl.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "DejaVu Sans", "font.weight": "bold",
        "axes.labelweight": "bold", "font.size": 10.5, "axes.labelsize": 12,
        "xtick.labelsize": 10.5, "ytick.labelsize": 10.5, "legend.fontsize": 10,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.5, "xtick.major.width": 1.5, "ytick.major.width": 1.5,
        "xtick.major.size": 5, "ytick.major.size": 5, "savefig.dpi": 600,
    })

    def finish_axes(ax):
        for s in ax.spines.values():
            s.set_visible(True); s.set_color("black"); s.set_linewidth(1.5)
        for t in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            t.set_fontweight("bold")

    def add_panel_label(ax, text, y=-0.20):
        ax.text(0.5, y, text, transform=ax.transAxes, ha="center", va="top",
                fontsize=15, fontweight="bold", clip_on=False)

    def plot_interval(ax, x, rate, lo, hi, color, marker):
        """Point estimate and interval drawn independently, so a cluster or
        percentile interval that excludes the raw estimate is still valid."""
        if not np.isfinite(rate):
            ax.text(x, 0.5, "N/E", transform=ax.get_xaxis_transform(),
                    ha="center", va="center", fontsize=9, fontweight="bold",
                    color="0.45")
            return
        if np.isfinite(lo) and np.isfinite(hi):
            ax.vlines(x, lo, hi, color=color, linewidth=2.2, zorder=2)
            ax.hlines([lo, hi], x - 0.05, x + 0.05, color=color,
                      linewidth=2.2, zorder=2)
        ax.scatter(x, rate, s=105, marker=marker, color=color,
                   edgecolor="black", linewidth=1.2, zorder=4)

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(12.6, 9.4), sharex=False)
    axes = axes.ravel()
    x_base = np.arange(len(METHODS), dtype=float)
    OFFSET = {"CWRU_DE_6205": -0.11, "PADERBORN_6203": 0.11}
    XTICKS = ["Order-null\nHolm", "Pooled\nconformal", "Oracle†", "Robust\nmax-p*"]

    for ax, spec in zip(axes, METRIC_PANELS):
        for bk in BEARING_KEYS:
            sub = (COMPARISON[COMPARISON.bearing_key == bk]
                   .set_index("method").reindex(METHODS))
            for mi, method in enumerate(METHODS):
                if method not in sub.index:
                    continue
                row = sub.loc[method]
                plot_interval(
                    ax, x_base[mi] + OFFSET[bk],
                    float(row[spec["column"]]) if pd.notna(row[spec["column"]]) else np.nan,
                    float(row[spec["low"]])    if pd.notna(row[spec["low"]])    else np.nan,
                    float(row[spec["high"]])   if pd.notna(row[spec["high"]])   else np.nan,
                    METHOD_COLORS[method], BEARING_MARKERS[bk])

        if spec["ref"] is not None:
            ax.axhline(spec["ref"], color="black", ls="--", lw=1.8, zorder=1)

        if spec["kind"] == "fwer":
            top = pd.to_numeric(COMPARISON[spec["high"]], errors="coerce").max()
            ax.set_ylim(0, min(1.0, max(0.12, (top if np.isfinite(top) else 0.2) * 1.18)))
        else:
            ax.set_ylim(0, 1.05)                       # full probability range
            ax.set_yticks(np.arange(0, 1.01, 0.2))

        ax.set_xticks(x_base)
        ax.set_xticklabels(XTICKS)                     # every panel labelled
        ax.set_xlim(-0.5, len(METHODS) - 0.5)
        ax.set_xlabel("Calibration method")
        ax.set_ylabel(spec["ylabel"])
        ax.grid(axis="y", color="0.85", ls=":", lw=1.0, zorder=0)
        finish_axes(ax)
        add_panel_label(ax, spec["panel"])

    fig.legend(handles=[
        Line2D([], [], marker="o", ls="none", mfc="white", mec="black", ms=9,
               label="CWRU 6205"),
        Line2D([], [], marker="s", ls="none", mfc="white", mec="black", ms=9,
               label="Paderborn 6203"),
        Line2D([], [], color="black", ls="--", lw=1.8, label=REF_LINE_LABEL),
    ], loc="upper center", bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.005), ncol=3, frameon=True,
       edgecolor="black", framealpha=1.0)

    fig.subplots_adjust(left=0.085, right=0.985, top=0.925, bottom=0.095,
                        hspace=0.52, wspace=0.28)
    FIG08_PATH = FIGURE_ROOT / "Fig08_registered_method_comparison.png"
    fig.savefig(FIG08_PATH, dpi=600, bbox_inches="tight", facecolor="white",
                edgecolor="none", pad_inches=0.12)
    plt.close(fig); print("Saved:", FIG08_PATH)

    # =====================================================================
    # 6. REVISED PUBLICATION FIGURE
    # =====================================================================

    import matplotlib as mpl
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    from matplotlib.patches import FancyArrowPatch


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Publication style
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    mpl.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "DejaVu Sans",
        "font.weight": "bold",
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "axes.linewidth": 1.6,
        "font.size": 11.5,
        "axes.labelsize": 12.5,
        "xtick.labelsize": 10.5,
        "ytick.labelsize": 10.5,
        "xtick.major.width": 1.5,
        "ytick.major.width": 1.5,
        "xtick.major.size": 5,
        "ytick.major.size": 5,
        "xtick.direction": "out",
        "ytick.direction": "out",
        "mathtext.fontset": "stix",
        "mathtext.default": "it",
        "savefig.dpi": 600,
    })

    # Colorblind-safe palette
    LOW_COLOR  = "#0072B2"   # blue
    HIGH_COLOR = "#D55E00"   # vermillion
    BPFO_COLOR = "#009E73"   # green
    BPFI_COLOR = "#CC3366"   # magenta-red
    DARK        = "#17212B"
    GRID_COLOR  = "#D9DEE5"
    PANEL_BG    = "#FCFCFD"


    fig, axes = plt.subplots(
        2,
        2,
        figsize=(11.2, 7.4),
        gridspec_kw={
            "height_ratios": [0.88, 1.0],
            "hspace": 0.66,
            "wspace": 0.20,
        },
    )

    ax_a, ax_b = axes[0]
    ax_c, ax_d = axes[1]


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Shared formatting
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    def format_panel(ax, show_y=True):
        """Apply publication-style four-sided borders."""

        ax.set_facecolor(PANEL_BG)

        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_visible(True)
            spine.set_linewidth(1.6)
            spine.set_color(DARK)

        ax.tick_params(
            axis="both",
            colors=DARK,
            width=1.5,
            length=5,
        )

        for label in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            label.set_fontweight("bold")

        if not show_y:
            ax.tick_params(axis="y", labelleft=False)


    def add_panel_letter(ax, letter, y=-0.25):
        ax.text(
            0.5,
            y,
            letter,
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            ha="center",
            va="top",
            fontsize=15,
            fontweight="bold",
            color=DARK,
            clip_on=False,
        )


    def add_speed_label(ax, text):
        """Centered operating-speed label."""

        ax.text(
            0.5,
            0.92,
            text,
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            ha="center",
            va="top",
            fontsize=13,
            fontweight="bold",
            color=DARK,
            bbox={
                "boxstyle": "round,pad=0.25",
                "facecolor": "white",
                "edgecolor": "none",
                "alpha": 0.88,
            },
            zorder=10,
        )


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Time-domain display crops
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    n_low_display = min(
        seg_low.size,
        int(round(
            DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS * FS_HZ / FR_LOW_HZ
        )),
    )

    n_high_display = min(
        seg_high.size,
        int(round(
            DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS * FS_HZ / FR_HIGH_HZ
        )),
    )

    rev_low = (
        np.arange(n_low_display) * FR_LOW_HZ / FS_HZ
    )

    rev_high = (
        np.arange(n_high_display) * FR_HIGH_HZ / FS_HZ
    )

    low_display = np.clip(
        seg_low[:n_low_display], -5.0, 5.0
    )

    high_display = np.clip(
        seg_high[:n_high_display], -5.0, 5.0
    )


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # (a) 900-rpm vibration
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    ax_a.plot(
        rev_low,
        low_display,
        color=LOW_COLOR,
        lw=0.72,
        alpha=0.92,
