        solid_capstyle="round",
        rasterized=True,
    )

    ax_a.axhline(
        0,
        color="0.45",
        lw=0.7,
        alpha=0.55,
        zorder=0,
    )

    ax_a.set_xlim(0, DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS)
    ax_a.set_ylim(-5.3, 5.3)
    ax_a.set_xticks(
        np.arange(0, DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS + 1, 1)
    )
    ax_a.set_yticks([-4, 0, 4])

    ax_a.set_xlabel("Shaft revolutions")
    ax_a.set_ylabel("Vibration (a.u.)")

    add_speed_label(ax_a, "900 rpm")
    format_panel(ax_a, show_y=True)
    add_panel_letter(ax_a, "(a)")


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # (b) 1500-rpm vibration
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    ax_b.plot(
        rev_high,
        high_display,
        color=HIGH_COLOR,
        lw=0.72,
        alpha=0.92,
        solid_capstyle="round",
        rasterized=True,
    )

    ax_b.axhline(
        0,
        color="0.45",
        lw=0.7,
        alpha=0.55,
        zorder=0,
    )

    ax_b.set_xlim(0, DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS)
    ax_b.set_ylim(-5.3, 5.3)
    ax_b.set_xticks(
        np.arange(0, DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS + 1, 1)
    )
    ax_b.set_yticks([-4, 0, 4])

    ax_b.set_xlabel("Shaft revolutions")

    add_speed_label(ax_b, "1500 rpm")
    format_panel(ax_b, show_y=False)
    add_panel_letter(ax_b, "(b)")


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Order-spectrum formatting
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    def format_order_panel(ax, show_y=True):
        format_panel(ax, show_y=show_y)

        ax.set_xlim(0.25, 8.0)
        ax.set_ylim(0.0, 1.10)

        ax.set_xticks(np.arange(1, 9, 1))
        ax.set_yticks([0.0, 0.5, 1.0])

        ax.set_xlabel("Order")

        ax.grid(
            axis="y",
            color=GRID_COLOR,
            linewidth=0.75,
            linestyle=":",
            alpha=0.80,
            zorder=0,
        )

        # Registered characteristic orders
        ax.axvline(
            BPFO_ORDER,
            color=BPFO_COLOR,
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=1.9,
            alpha=0.95,
            zorder=3,
        )

        ax.axvline(
            BPFI_ORDER,
            color=BPFI_COLOR,
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=1.9,
            alpha=0.95,
            zorder=3,
        )


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # (c) 900-rpm order spectrum
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    ax_c.plot(
        order_low,
        spectrum_low,
        color=LOW_COLOR,
        lw=2.0,
        alpha=0.98,
        solid_capstyle="round",
        zorder=4,
    )

    ax_c.fill_between(
        order_low,
        0,
        spectrum_low,
        color=LOW_COLOR,
        alpha=0.08,
        linewidth=0,
        zorder=1,
    )

    ax_c.set_ylabel("Envelope (a.u.)")

    format_order_panel(ax_c, show_y=True)
    add_panel_letter(ax_c, "(c)", y=-0.25)


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # (d) 1500-rpm order spectrum
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    ax_d.plot(
        order_high,
        spectrum_high,
        color=HIGH_COLOR,
        lw=2.0,
        alpha=0.98,
        solid_capstyle="round",
        zorder=4,
    )

    ax_d.fill_between(
        order_high,
        0,
        spectrum_high,
        color=HIGH_COLOR,
        alpha=0.08,
        linewidth=0,
        zorder=1,
    )

    format_order_panel(ax_d, show_y=False)
    add_panel_letter(ax_d, "(d)", y=-0.25)


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Shared characteristic-order legend
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    legend_handles = [
        Line2D(
            [],
            [],
            color=BPFO_COLOR,
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=2.2,
            label="BPFO",
        ),
        Line2D(
            [],
            [],
            color=BPFI_COLOR,
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=2.2,
            label="BPFI",
        ),
    ]

    legend = fig.legend(
        handles=legend_handles,
        loc="upper center",
        bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 0.992),
        ncol=2,
        columnspacing=2.2,
        handlelength=2.4,
        frameon=True,
        fancybox=True,
        edgecolor=DARK,
        framealpha=1.0,
        borderpad=0.55,
    )

    legend.get_frame().set_linewidth(1.3)

    for text_item in legend.get_texts():
        text_item.set_fontweight("bold")


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Properly typeset frequency-to-order transformation
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    formula_y = 0.505

    # Short downward arrow
    arrow = FancyArrowPatch(
        (0.5, formula_y + 0.045),
        (0.5, formula_y - 0.038),
        transform=fig.transFigure,
        arrowstyle="-|>",
        mutation_scale=16,
        linewidth=1.5,
        color=DARK,
        clip_on=False,
    )

    fig.add_artist(arrow)

    # Matplotlib MathText fraction
    fig.text(
        0.5,
        formula_y,
        r"$o=\frac{f}{f_{\mathrm{r}}}$",
        ha="center",
        va="center",
        fontsize=16,
        color=DARK,
        bbox={
            "boxstyle": "round,pad=0.30",
            "facecolor": "white",
            "edgecolor": DARK,
            "linewidth": 1.2,
        },
    )


    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final spacing and output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------

    fig.subplots_adjust(
        left=0.085,
        right=0.985,
        top=0.905,
        bottom=0.105,
    )

    fig.savefig(
        OUTPUT_PATH,
        dpi=600,
        bbox_inches="tight",
        facecolor="white",
        edgecolor="none",
        pad_inches=0.10,
    )

    plt.show()

    print("Saved:", OUTPUT_PATH)

    # ================================================================
    # FINAL REAL-DATA ORDER-NORMALIZATION FIGURE
    #
    # Required variables from the preceding signal-processing code:
    #   seg_low, seg_high
    #   order_low, spectrum_low
    #   order_high, spectrum_high
    #   FS_HZ, FR_LOW_HZ, FR_HIGH_HZ
    #   BPFO_ORDER, BPFI_ORDER
    # ================================================================

    from pathlib import Path
    import numpy as np
    import matplotlib as mpl
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Configuration
    # ------------------------------------------------
    LOW_RPM = int(round(60.0 * FR_LOW_HZ))
    HIGH_RPM = int(round(60.0 * FR_HIGH_HZ))

    DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS = 6.0
    ORDER_LIMIT = (0.25, 8.0)

    # Keep the formula when the figure must be self-contained.
    # Set False if the equation is already stated immediately before the figure.
    SHOW_TRANSFORM_FORMULA = True

    # Colorblind-friendly palette
    LOW_COLOR = "#0072B2"       # blue
    HIGH_COLOR = "#D55E00"      # vermillion
    BPFO_COLOR = "#009E73"      # green
    BPFI_COLOR = "#CC3366"      # magenta-red
    AXIS_COLOR = "#17212B"
    GRID_COLOR = "#CBD2D9"

    # Resolve output directory
    if "MSSP_DIRS" in globals() and "figures_main" in MSSP_DIRS:
        FIGURE_DIR = Path(MSSP_DIRS["figures_main"])
    elif "OUTPUT_PATH" in globals():
        FIGURE_DIR = Path(OUTPUT_PATH).parent
    else:
        FIGURE_DIR = Path.cwd()

    FIGURE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    FINAL_PATH = FIGURE_DIR / "Fig_order_normalization_real_data_final.png"

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Validate and prepare the data
    # ------------------------------------------------
    required_arrays = {
        "seg_low": seg_low,
        "seg_high": seg_high,
        "order_low": order_low,
        "spectrum_low": spectrum_low,
        "order_high": order_high,
        "spectrum_high": spectrum_high,
    }

    for name, values in required_arrays.items():
        values = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)

        if values.ndim != 1:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} must be one-dimensional.")

        if len(values) == 0:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} is empty.")

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(values)):
            raise ValueError(f"{name} contains NaN or Inf values.")

    seg_low = np.asarray(seg_low, dtype=float)
    seg_high = np.asarray(seg_high, dtype=float)

    order_low = np.asarray(order_low, dtype=float)
    order_high = np.asarray(order_high, dtype=float)

    spec_low = np.asarray(spectrum_low, dtype=float)
    spec_high = np.asarray(spectrum_high, dtype=float)

    # Normalize spectra only for display; peak locations remain unchanged.
    spec_low = spec_low / max(np.nanmax(spec_low), np.finfo(float).eps)
    spec_high = spec_high / max(np.nanmax(spec_high), np.finfo(float).eps)

    # Convert the time axes into accumulated shaft revolutions.
    rev_low = np.arange(len(seg_low), dtype=float) * FR_LOW_HZ / FS_HZ
    rev_high = np.arange(len(seg_high), dtype=float) * FR_HIGH_HZ / FS_HZ

    # Display exactly the requested mechanical duration.
    low_keep = rev_low <= DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS
    high_keep = rev_high <= DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS

    rev_low_plot = rev_low[low_keep]
    rev_high_plot = rev_high[high_keep]

    seg_low_plot = seg_low[low_keep]
    seg_high_plot = seg_high[high_keep]

    # Use common time-domain limits for a fair visual comparison.
    combined_signal = np.concatenate([seg_low_plot, seg_high_plot])
    signal_limit = 1.04 * np.nanmax(np.abs(combined_signal))

    if not np.isfinite(signal_limit) or signal_limit <= 0:
        signal_limit = 1.0

    # Restrict the order spectra to the displayed range.
    order_mask_low = (
        (order_low >= ORDER_LIMIT[0]) &
        (order_low <= ORDER_LIMIT[1])
    )
    order_mask_high = (
        (order_high >= ORDER_LIMIT[0]) &
        (order_high <= ORDER_LIMIT[1])
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Publication styling
    # ------------------------------------------------
    mpl.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "DejaVu Sans",
        "font.size": 11,
        "font.weight": "bold",
        "axes.labelsize": 13,
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 11,
        "ytick.labelsize": 11,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.6,
        "xtick.major.width": 1.5,
        "ytick.major.width": 1.5,
        "xtick.major.size": 5,
        "ytick.major.size": 5,
        "xtick.direction": "out",
        "ytick.direction": "out",
        "mathtext.fontset": "stix",
        "savefig.dpi": 600,
    })

    def style_axes(ax, grid=True):
        """Apply identical publication styling to every panel."""
        ax.set_facecolor("#FFFFFF")

        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_visible(True)
            spine.set_color(AXIS_COLOR)
            spine.set_linewidth(1.6)

        ax.tick_params(
            axis="both",
            colors=AXIS_COLOR,
            width=1.5,
            length=5
        )

        for label in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            label.set_fontweight("bold")

        if grid:
            ax.grid(
                axis="y",
                color=GRID_COLOR,
                linestyle=":",
                linewidth=0.9,
                alpha=0.70,
                zorder=0
            )

    def add_panel_letter(ax, letter, offset=-0.25):
        ax.text(
            0.5, offset, letter,
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            ha="center",
            va="top",
            fontsize=16,
            fontweight="bold",
            color=AXIS_COLOR,
            clip_on=False
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Figure layout
    # ------------------------------------------------
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(14.5, 9.5), facecolor="white")

    gs = fig.add_gridspec(
        nrows=2,
        ncols=2,
        left=0.080,
        right=0.985,
        bottom=0.090,
        top=0.855,
        wspace=0.20,
        hspace=0.70
    )

    ax_a = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 0])
    ax_b = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 1], sharey=ax_a)
    ax_c = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 0])
    ax_d = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 1], sharey=ax_c)

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Panels (a) and (b): vibration signals
    # ------------------------------------------------
    ax_a.plot(
        rev_low_plot,
        seg_low_plot,
        color=LOW_COLOR,
        linewidth=0.65,
        alpha=0.90,
        rasterized=True
    )

    ax_b.plot(
        rev_high_plot,
        seg_high_plot,
        color=HIGH_COLOR,
        linewidth=0.65,
        alpha=0.90,
        rasterized=True
    )

    for ax in (ax_a, ax_b):
        ax.axhline(
            0,
            color=AXIS_COLOR,
            linewidth=0.7,
            alpha=0.35,
            zorder=0
        )
        ax.set_xlim(0, DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS)
        ax.set_ylim(-signal_limit, signal_limit)
        ax.set_xlabel("Shaft revolutions")
        style_axes(ax, grid=True)

    ax_a.set_ylabel("Vibration (a.u.)")
    ax_b.tick_params(labelleft=False)

    # Signal notation outside the upper-left corner
    ax_a.text(
        0.01, 1.045,
        r"$x_1(t)$",
        transform=ax_a.transAxes,
        ha="left",
        va="bottom",
        fontsize=15,
        fontweight="bold",
        color=AXIS_COLOR,
        clip_on=False
    )

    ax_b.text(
        0.01, 1.045,
        r"$x_2(t)$",
        transform=ax_b.transAxes,
        ha="left",
        va="bottom",
        fontsize=15,
        fontweight="bold",
        color=AXIS_COLOR,
        clip_on=False
    )

    # RPM values centered above and outside each panel border
    ax_a.text(
        0.50, 1.045,
        rf"$\mathbf{{{LOW_RPM}\ rpm}}$",
        transform=ax_a.transAxes,
        ha="center",
        va="bottom",
        fontsize=14,
        color=AXIS_COLOR,
        bbox=dict(
            boxstyle="round,pad=0.18",
            facecolor="white",
            edgecolor="none",
            alpha=0.95
        ),
        clip_on=False
    )

    ax_b.text(
        0.50, 1.045,
        rf"$\mathbf{{{HIGH_RPM}\ rpm}}$",
        transform=ax_b.transAxes,
        ha="center",
        va="bottom",
        fontsize=14,
        color=AXIS_COLOR,
        bbox=dict(
            boxstyle="round,pad=0.18",
            facecolor="white",
            edgecolor="none",
            alpha=0.95
        ),
        clip_on=False
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Panels (c) and (d): order spectra
    # ------------------------------------------------
    ax_c.fill_between(
        order_low[order_mask_low],
        0,
        spec_low[order_mask_low],
        color=LOW_COLOR,
        alpha=0.10,
        zorder=1
    )

    ax_c.plot(
        order_low[order_mask_low],
        spec_low[order_mask_low],
        color=LOW_COLOR,
        linewidth=2.0,
        zorder=3
    )

    ax_d.fill_between(
        order_high[order_mask_high],
        0,
        spec_high[order_mask_high],
        color=HIGH_COLOR,
        alpha=0.10,
        zorder=1
    )

    ax_d.plot(
        order_high[order_mask_high],
        spec_high[order_mask_high],
        color=HIGH_COLOR,
        linewidth=2.0,
        zorder=3
    )

    for ax in (ax_c, ax_d):
        ax.axvline(
            BPFO_ORDER,
            color=BPFO_COLOR,
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=2.0,
            zorder=4
        )

        ax.axvline(
            BPFI_ORDER,
            color=BPFI_COLOR,
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=2.0,
            zorder=4
        )

        ax.set_xlim(*ORDER_LIMIT)
        ax.set_ylim(0, 1.10)
        ax.set_xlabel("Order")
        ax.set_yticks([0.0, 0.5, 1.0])
        style_axes(ax, grid=True)

    ax_c.set_ylabel("Envelope (a.u.)")
    ax_d.tick_params(labelleft=False)

    # Correct order-domain notation
    ax_c.text(
        0.01, 1.045,
        r"$X_1(o)$",
        transform=ax_c.transAxes,
        ha="left",
        va="bottom",
        fontsize=15,
        fontweight="bold",
        color=AXIS_COLOR,
        clip_on=False
    )

    ax_d.text(
        0.01, 1.045,
        r"$X_2(o)$",
        transform=ax_d.transAxes,
        ha="left",
        va="bottom",
        fontsize=15,
        fontweight="bold",
        color=AXIS_COLOR,
        clip_on=False
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Shared legend outside the panels
    # ------------------------------------------------
    legend_handles = [
        Line2D(
            [0], [0],
            color=BPFO_COLOR,
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=2.2,
            label="BPFO"
        ),
        Line2D(
            [0], [0],
            color=BPFI_COLOR,
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=2.2,
            label="BPFI"
        ),
    ]

    legend = fig.legend(
        handles=legend_handles,
        loc="upper center",
        bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 0.985),
        ncol=2,
        frameon=True,
        fancybox=True,
        edgecolor=AXIS_COLOR,
        framealpha=1.0,
        fontsize=12,
        columnspacing=2.5,
        handlelength=2.7
    )

    for text_item in legend.get_texts():
        text_item.set_fontweight("bold")

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 8. Transformation formula
    # ------------------------------------------------
    if SHOW_TRANSFORM_FORMULA:
        # Find the vertical midpoint between the two plot rows.
        upper_bottom = min(
            ax_a.get_position().y0,
            ax_b.get_position().y0
        )
        lower_top = max(
            ax_c.get_position().y1,
            ax_d.get_position().y1
        )
        formula_y = 0.5 * (upper_bottom + lower_top)

        fig.text(
            0.5,
            formula_y,
            r"$o=\dfrac{f}{f_{\mathrm{r}}}$",
            ha="center",
            va="center",
            fontsize=18,
            color=AXIS_COLOR,
            bbox=dict(
                boxstyle="round,pad=0.30",
                facecolor="white",
                edgecolor=AXIS_COLOR,
                linewidth=1.3
            )
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 9. Panel letters centered below each panel
    # ------------------------------------------------
    add_panel_letter(ax_a, "(a)", offset=-0.25)
    add_panel_letter(ax_b, "(b)", offset=-0.25)
    add_panel_letter(ax_c, "(c)", offset=-0.24)
    add_panel_letter(ax_d, "(d)", offset=-0.24)

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 10. Save
    # ------------------------------------------------
    fig.savefig(
        FINAL_PATH,
        dpi=600,
        bbox_inches="tight",
        facecolor="white",
        edgecolor="none",
        pad_inches=0.12
    )

    plt.show()

    print("Saved:", FINAL_PATH)

    BPFO_ORDER = 3.0498
    BPFI_ORDER = 4.9502

    assert BPFO_ORDER < BPFI_ORDER

    for rpm in (900, 1500):
        fr_hz = rpm / 60.0
        print(
            f"{rpm} rpm: "
            f"BPFO={BPFO_ORDER * fr_hz:.3f} Hz, "
            f"BPFI={BPFI_ORDER * fr_hz:.3f} Hz"
        )

    # ================================================================
    # FINAL REAL-DATA ORDER-NORMALIZATION FIGURE
    #
    # Required variables from the preceding signal-processing code:
    #   seg_low, seg_high
    #   order_low, spectrum_low
    #   order_high, spectrum_high
    #   FS_HZ, FR_LOW_HZ, FR_HIGH_HZ
    #   BPFO_ORDER, BPFI_ORDER
    # ================================================================

    from pathlib import Path
    import numpy as np
    import matplotlib as mpl
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Configuration
    # ------------------------------------------------
    LOW_RPM = int(round(60.0 * FR_LOW_HZ))
    HIGH_RPM = int(round(60.0 * FR_HIGH_HZ))

    DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS = 6.0
    ORDER_LIMIT = (0.25, 8.0)

    SHOW_TRANSFORM_FORMULA = False      # equation stated in the text instead
    SIGNAL_LINEWIDTH = 0.95             # thicker traces
    DPI = 300

    # Colorblind-friendly palette
    LOW_COLOR = "#0072B2"       # blue
    HIGH_COLOR = "#D55E00"      # vermillion
    BPFO_COLOR = "#009E73"      # green
    BPFI_COLOR = "#CC3366"      # magenta-red
    AXIS_COLOR = "#17212B"
    GRID_COLOR = "#CBD2D9"

    if "MSSP_DIRS" in globals() and "figures_main" in MSSP_DIRS:
        FIGURE_DIR = Path(MSSP_DIRS["figures_main"])
    elif "OUTPUT_PATH" in globals():
        FIGURE_DIR = Path(OUTPUT_PATH).parent
    else:
        FIGURE_DIR = Path.cwd()

    FIGURE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    FINAL_PATH = FIGURE_DIR / "Fig_order_normalization_real_data_final.png"

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Validate and prepare the data
    # ------------------------------------------------
    required_arrays = {
        "seg_low": seg_low,
        "seg_high": seg_high,
        "order_low": order_low,
        "spectrum_low": spectrum_low,
        "order_high": order_high,
        "spectrum_high": spectrum_high,
    }

    for name, values in required_arrays.items():
        values = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
        if values.ndim != 1:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} must be one-dimensional.")
        if len(values) == 0:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} is empty.")
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(values)):
            raise ValueError(f"{name} contains NaN or Inf values.")

    seg_low = np.asarray(seg_low, dtype=float)
    seg_high = np.asarray(seg_high, dtype=float)

    order_low = np.asarray(order_low, dtype=float)
    order_high = np.asarray(order_high, dtype=float)

    spec_low = np.asarray(spectrum_low, dtype=float)
    spec_high = np.asarray(spectrum_high, dtype=float)

    # Normalize spectra only for display; peak locations remain unchanged.
    spec_low = spec_low / max(np.nanmax(spec_low), np.finfo(float).eps)
    spec_high = spec_high / max(np.nanmax(spec_high), np.finfo(float).eps)

    # Convert the time axes into accumulated shaft revolutions.
    rev_low = np.arange(len(seg_low), dtype=float) * FR_LOW_HZ / FS_HZ
    rev_high = np.arange(len(seg_high), dtype=float) * FR_HIGH_HZ / FS_HZ

    low_keep = rev_low <= DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS
    high_keep = rev_high <= DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS

    rev_low_plot = rev_low[low_keep]
    rev_high_plot = rev_high[high_keep]

    seg_low_plot = seg_low[low_keep]
    seg_high_plot = seg_high[high_keep]

    combined_signal = np.concatenate([seg_low_plot, seg_high_plot])
    signal_limit = 1.04 * np.nanmax(np.abs(combined_signal))

    if not np.isfinite(signal_limit) or signal_limit <= 0:
        signal_limit = 1.0

    order_mask_low = (
        (order_low >= ORDER_LIMIT[0]) &
        (order_low <= ORDER_LIMIT[1])
    )
    order_mask_high = (
        (order_high >= ORDER_LIMIT[0]) &
        (order_high <= ORDER_LIMIT[1])
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Publication styling
    # ------------------------------------------------
    mpl.rcParams.update({
        "font.family": "DejaVu Sans",
        "font.size": 11,
        "font.weight": "bold",
        "axes.labelsize": 13,
        "axes.labelweight": "bold",
        "xtick.labelsize": 11,
        "ytick.labelsize": 11,
        "axes.linewidth": 1.6,
        "xtick.major.width": 1.5,
        "ytick.major.width": 1.5,
        "xtick.major.size": 5,
        "ytick.major.size": 5,
        "xtick.direction": "out",
        "ytick.direction": "out",
        "mathtext.fontset": "stix",
        "savefig.dpi": DPI,
    })


    def style_axes(ax, grid=True):
        """Apply identical publication styling to every panel."""
        ax.set_facecolor("#FFFFFF")

        for spine in ax.spines.values():
            spine.set_visible(True)
            spine.set_color(AXIS_COLOR)
            spine.set_linewidth(1.6)

        ax.tick_params(axis="both", colors=AXIS_COLOR, width=1.5, length=5)

        for label in ax.get_xticklabels() + ax.get_yticklabels():
            label.set_fontweight("bold")

        if grid:
            ax.grid(
                axis="y",
                color=GRID_COLOR,
                linestyle=":",
                linewidth=0.9,
                alpha=0.70,
                zorder=0
            )


    def add_panel_letter(ax, text, offset=-0.25):
        ax.text(
            0.5, offset, text,
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            ha="center",
            va="top",
            fontsize=16,
            fontweight="bold",
            color=AXIS_COLOR,
            clip_on=False
        )


    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Figure layout
    # ------------------------------------------------
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(14.5, 9.0), facecolor="white")

    gs = fig.add_gridspec(
        nrows=2,
        ncols=2,
        left=0.080,
        right=0.985,
        bottom=0.090,
        top=0.870,
        wspace=0.20,
        hspace=0.52
    )

    ax_a = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 0])
    ax_b = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 1], sharey=ax_a)
    ax_c = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 0])
    ax_d = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 1], sharey=ax_c)

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Panels (a) and (b): vibration signals
    # ------------------------------------------------
    ax_a.plot(
        rev_low_plot,
        seg_low_plot,
        color=LOW_COLOR,
        linewidth=SIGNAL_LINEWIDTH,
        solid_joinstyle="round",
        solid_capstyle="round",
        alpha=0.92,
        rasterized=True
    )

    ax_b.plot(
        rev_high_plot,
        seg_high_plot,
        color=HIGH_COLOR,
        linewidth=SIGNAL_LINEWIDTH,
        solid_joinstyle="round",
        solid_capstyle="round",
        alpha=0.92,
        rasterized=True
    )

    for ax in (ax_a, ax_b):
        ax.axhline(0, color=AXIS_COLOR, linewidth=0.7, alpha=0.35, zorder=0)
        ax.set_xlim(0, DISPLAY_REVOLUTIONS)
        ax.set_ylim(-signal_limit, signal_limit)
        ax.set_xlabel("Shaft revolutions")
        style_axes(ax, grid=True)

    ax_a.set_ylabel("Vibration (a.u.)")
    ax_b.tick_params(labelleft=False)

    ax_a.text(
        0.01, 1.045, r"$x_1(t)$",
        transform=ax_a.transAxes, ha="left", va="bottom",
        fontsize=15, fontweight="bold", color=AXIS_COLOR, clip_on=False
    )

    ax_b.text(
        0.01, 1.045, r"$x_2(t)$",
        transform=ax_b.transAxes, ha="left", va="bottom",
        fontsize=15, fontweight="bold", color=AXIS_COLOR, clip_on=False
    )

    ax_a.text(
        0.50, 1.045, rf"$\mathbf{{{LOW_RPM}\ rpm}}$",
        transform=ax_a.transAxes, ha="center", va="bottom",
        fontsize=14, color=AXIS_COLOR,
        bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.18", facecolor="white",
                  edgecolor="none", alpha=0.95),
        clip_on=False
    )

    ax_b.text(
        0.50, 1.045, rf"$\mathbf{{{HIGH_RPM}\ rpm}}$",
        transform=ax_b.transAxes, ha="center", va="bottom",
        fontsize=14, color=AXIS_COLOR,
        bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.18", facecolor="white",
                  edgecolor="none", alpha=0.95),
        clip_on=False
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 6. Panels (c) and (d): order spectra
    # ------------------------------------------------
    ax_c.fill_between(
        order_low[order_mask_low], 0, spec_low[order_mask_low],
        color=LOW_COLOR, alpha=0.10, zorder=1
    )

    ax_c.plot(
        order_low[order_mask_low], spec_low[order_mask_low],
        color=LOW_COLOR, linewidth=2.0, zorder=3
    )

    ax_d.fill_between(
        order_high[order_mask_high], 0, spec_high[order_mask_high],
        color=HIGH_COLOR, alpha=0.10, zorder=1
    )

    ax_d.plot(
        order_high[order_mask_high], spec_high[order_mask_high],
        color=HIGH_COLOR, linewidth=2.0, zorder=3
    )

    for ax in (ax_c, ax_d):
        ax.axvline(BPFO_ORDER, color=BPFO_COLOR, linestyle="--",
                   linewidth=2.0, zorder=4)
        ax.axvline(BPFI_ORDER, color=BPFI_COLOR, linestyle="--",
                   linewidth=2.0, zorder=4)
        ax.set_xlim(*ORDER_LIMIT)
        ax.set_ylim(0, 1.10)
        ax.set_xlabel("Order")
        ax.set_yticks([0.0, 0.5, 1.0])
        style_axes(ax, grid=True)

    ax_c.set_ylabel("Envelope (a.u.)")
    ax_d.tick_params(labelleft=False)

    ax_c.text(
        0.01, 1.045, r"$X_1(o)$",
        transform=ax_c.transAxes, ha="left", va="bottom",
        fontsize=15, fontweight="bold", color=AXIS_COLOR, clip_on=False
    )

    ax_d.text(
        0.01, 1.045, r"$X_2(o)$",
        transform=ax_d.transAxes, ha="left", va="bottom",
        fontsize=15, fontweight="bold", color=AXIS_COLOR, clip_on=False
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 7. Shared legend outside the panels
    # ------------------------------------------------
    legend_handles = [
        Line2D([0], [0], color=BPFO_COLOR, linestyle="--",
               linewidth=2.2, label="BPFO"),
        Line2D([0], [0], color=BPFI_COLOR, linestyle="--",
               linewidth=2.2, label="BPFI"),
    ]

    legend = fig.legend(
        handles=legend_handles,
        loc="upper center",
        bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 0.985),
        ncol=2,
        frameon=True,
        fancybox=True,
        edgecolor=AXIS_COLOR,
        framealpha=1.0,
        fontsize=12,
        columnspacing=2.5,
        handlelength=2.7
    )

    for text_item in legend.get_texts():
        text_item.set_fontweight("bold")

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 8. Transformation formula (disabled)
    # ------------------------------------------------
    if SHOW_TRANSFORM_FORMULA:
        upper_bottom = min(ax_a.get_position().y0, ax_b.get_position().y0)
        lower_top = max(ax_c.get_position().y1, ax_d.get_position().y1)
        formula_y = 0.5 * (upper_bottom + lower_top)

        fig.text(
            0.5, formula_y, r"$o=\dfrac{f}{f_{\mathrm{r}}}$",
            ha="center", va="center", fontsize=18, color=AXIS_COLOR,
            bbox=dict(boxstyle="round,pad=0.30", facecolor="white",
                      edgecolor=AXIS_COLOR, linewidth=1.3)
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 9. Panel letters centered below each panel
    # ------------------------------------------------
    add_panel_letter(ax_a, "(a)", offset=-0.25)
    add_panel_letter(ax_b, "(b)", offset=-0.25)
    add_panel_letter(ax_c, "(c)", offset=-0.24)
    add_panel_letter(ax_d, "(d)", offset=-0.24)

    # ------------------------------------------------
    # 10. Save
    # ------------------------------------------------
    fig.savefig(
        FINAL_PATH,
        dpi=DPI,
        bbox_inches="tight",
        facecolor="white",
        edgecolor="none",
        pad_inches=0.12
    )

    plt.show()

    print("Saved:", FINAL_PATH, f"({DPI} dpi)")
